--- emit_receipt.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/IMP-0067.json from live schema measurements."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools"))

from marketplace.index_schema import (  # noqa: E402
    INDEX_REL,
    MUTATION_IDS,
    SCHEMA_REL,
    extended_example,
    live_report,
    load_index,
    load_schema,
    mutation_verdicts,
    scan_second_indexes,
    validate_both,
)

OUT = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0067.json"
TEST = "tools/marketplace/tests/test_index_schema.py"
P1 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0067/probe1_current_and_mutations.py"
)
P2 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0067/probe2_second_engine.py"
)
SENTINEL = "SUCCESS:IMP-0067"


def sha_file(rel: str) -> dict:
    path = REPO / rel
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return {
        "path": rel,
        "bytes": len(raw),
        "sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
        "exists": path.is_file(),
        "role": "supporting",
        "eol": "LF",
    }


def run_tests() -> tuple[int, str]:
    p = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-B", str(REPO / TEST)],
        cwd=str(REPO),
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    return p.returncode, ((p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or "")).strip()


def fingerprint(method: str, command: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(f"{method}\x1f{command}".encode()).hexdigest()[:32]


def main() -> int:
    rc, test_out = run_tests()
    if rc != 0:
        print(test_out[-2000:])
        print("FAILED:IMP-0067 tests")
        return 1

    report = live_report()
    if not (
        report["current_ok_a"]
        and report["current_ok_b"]
        and report["extended_ok_a"]
        and report["extended_ok_b"]
        and report["mutation_codes_distinct"]
        and report["mutation_primary_matches_id"]
        and report["all_mutations_agree"]
        and report["no_second_index"]
    ):
        print(json.dumps({k: report[k] for k in (
            "current_ok_a",
            "current_ok_b",
            "extended_ok_a",
            "extended_ok_b",
            "mutation_codes_a",
            "mutation_codes_distinct",
            "all_mutations_agree",
            "second_indexes",
        )}, indent=2)[:4000])
        print("FAILED:IMP-0067 live report not green")
        return 1

    index = load_index()

--- probe1_current_and_mutations.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: current marketplace.json validates; six mutants fail distinctly.

Evidence signal (1): the CURRENT marketplace.json validates green under the
new schema without edits. Also enforces no second index file.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools"))

from marketplace.index_schema import (  # noqa: E402
    INDEX_REL,
    MUTATION_IDS,
    SCHEMA_REL,
    live_report,
    load_index,
    load_schema,
    mutation_verdicts,
    scan_second_indexes,
    validate_engine_a,
)

ART = REPO / "command-center/receipts/IMP-0067.json"
TEST = REPO / "tools/marketplace/tests/test_index_schema.py"


def main() -> int:
    if not ART.is_file() or ART.stat().st_size < 200:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0067-probe1 receipt missing")
        return 1
    text = ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    try:
        doc = json.loads(text)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0067-probe1 unparseable:{exc}")
        return 1
    if doc.get("unit_id") != "IMP-0067" and doc.get("id") != "IMP-0067":
        print("FAILED:IMP-0067-probe1 unit_id", doc.get("unit_id") or doc.get("id"))
        return 1
    if "SUCCESS:IMP-0067" not in text:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0067-probe1 sentinel absent")
        return 1
    st = str(doc.get("state") or "").upper()
    if st in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0067-probe1 self-declared:{st}")
        return 1

    proc = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-B", str(TEST)],
        cwd=str(REPO),
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    out = ((proc.stdout or "") + (proc.stderr or "")).strip()
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        print(out[-2000:])
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0067-probe1 tests rc={proc.returncode}")
        return 1

    index = load_index()
    schema = load_schema()
    current = validate_engine_a(index, schema)
    if not current.ok:
        print(json.dumps(current.as_dict(), indent=2)[:3000])
        print("FAILED:IMP-0067-probe1 current index rejected by engine A")
        return 1

    rows = mutation_verdicts(index, schema)
    codes = [rows[mid]["engine_a"]["primary_code"] for mid in MUTATION_IDS]
    if any(rows[mid]["engine_a"]["ok"] for mid in MUTATION_IDS):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0067-probe1 a mutant passed engine A")
        return 1
    if len(set(codes)) != 6 or set(codes) != set(MUTATION_IDS):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0067-probe1 mutant codes not distinct", codes)
        return 1

    seconds = scan_second_indexes()
    if seconds:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0067-probe1 second index files", seconds)
        return 1

    report = live_report()
    payload = {
        "probe": "probe1-current-and-mutations",
        "unit_id": "IMP-0067",
        "ok": True,
        "index": INDEX_REL,
        "schema": SCHEMA_REL,
        "engine": current.engine,
        "current_ok": current.ok,
        "mutation_codes": codes,
        "second_indexes": seconds,
        "tests_ok": True,
        "live_current_ok_a": report["current_ok_a"],

--- probe2_second_engine.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: second JSON-Schema implementation reaches the same verdicts.

Evidence signal (2): a walker that does not import jsonschema agrees with
Draft202012Validator on the live index, the additive extension, and each of
the six named mutants. Guards against a dialect quirk.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import ast
import inspect
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools"))

from marketplace import index_schema as S  # noqa: E402

ART = REPO / "command-center/receipts/IMP-0067.json"


def main() -> int:
    if not ART.is_file() or ART.stat().st_size < 200:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0067-probe2 receipt missing")
        return 1
    text = ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    try:
        doc = json.loads(text)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0067-probe2 unparseable:{exc}")
        return 1
    if "SUCCESS:IMP-0067" not in text:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0067-probe2 sentinel absent")
        return 1
    st = str(doc.get("state") or "").upper()
    if st in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0067-probe2 self-declared:{st}")
        return 1

    import ast

    tree = ast.parse(inspect.getsource(S))
    top_mods = set()
    for node in tree.body:
        if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
            for alias in node.names:
                top_mods.add(alias.name.split(".")[0])
        elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.module:
            top_mods.add(node.module.split(".")[0])
    if "jsonschema" in top_mods:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0067-probe2 jsonschema imported at module top-level")
        return 1
    fn_b: list[str] = []
    for node in tree.body:
        if isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef) and node.name == "validate_engine_b":
            for sub in ast.walk(node):
                if isinstance(sub, ast.Import):
                    fn_b.extend(a.name for a in sub.names)
                elif isinstance(sub, ast.ImportFrom) and sub.module:
                    fn_b.append(sub.module)
    if any(n == "jsonschema" or n.startswith("jsonschema.") for n in fn_b):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0067-probe2 engine B imports jsonschema")
        return 1

    index = S.load_index()
    schema = S.load_schema()
    current_a = S.validate_engine_a(index, schema)
    current_b = S.validate_engine_b(index, schema)
    if not current_a.ok or not current_b.ok:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0067-probe2 current index not accepted by both engines")
        print(json.dumps({"a": current_a.as_dict(), "b": current_b.as_dict()}, indent=2)[:4000])
        return 1

    extended = S.extended_example(index)
    ext_a = S.validate_engine_a(extended, schema)
    ext_b = S.validate_engine_b(extended, schema)
    if not ext_a.ok or not ext_b.ok:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0067-probe2 extended index not accepted by both engines")
        print(json.dumps({"a": ext_a.as_dict(), "b": ext_b.as_dict()}, indent=2)[:4000])
        return 1

    compared = []
    mismatches = []
    rows = S.mutation_verdicts(index, schema)
    for mid in S.MUTATION_IDS:
        row = rows[mid]
        compared.append(
            {
                "id": mid,
                "a": row["engine_a"]["primary_code"],
                "b": row["engine_b"]["primary_code"],
                "a_ok": row["engine_a"]["ok"],
                "b_ok": row["engine_b"]["ok"],
            }
        )
        if (
            row["engine_a"]["ok"]
            or row["engine_b"]["ok"]

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Write command-center/receipts/IMP-0082.json from live cycle-detector measurements."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools"))

from marketplace import cycle_cases as cases  # noqa: E402
from marketplace.cycle_ref import independent_report  # noqa: E402
from marketplace.dependency import (  # noqa: E402
    KIND_DEV,
    KIND_OPTIONAL,
    KIND_PEER,
    KIND_REQUIRED,
    DependencyCycleError,
    check_at_publish,
    cycle_matches,
    detect_cycles,
    graph_from_document,
    install_order,
    membership,
    naive_recursive_walk,
    publish_verdict,
)

UNIT = "IMP-0082"
OUT = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0082.json"
SENTINEL = "SUCCESS:IMP-0082"
TEST = "tools/marketplace/tests/test_dependency_graph.py"
SUITE = f"PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B {TEST}"
P1 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0082/probe1_planted_cycles.py"
)
P2 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0082/probe2_independent_library.py"
)
SUPPORTING = [
    "tools/marketplace/dependency.py",
    "tools/marketplace/cycle_cases.py",
    "tools/marketplace/cycle_ref.py",
    "tools/marketplace/schema/dependency.schema.json",
    "tools/marketplace/tests/test_dependency_graph.py",
    "tools/marketplace/index_linter.py",
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0082/probe1_planted_cycles.py",
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0082/probe2_independent_library.py",
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0082/emit_receipt.py",
]


def sha_file(rel: str, role: str) -> dict:
    path = REPO / rel
    raw = path.read_bytes() if path.is_file() else b""
    norm = raw.replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return {
        "path": rel,
        "exists": path.is_file(),
        "bytes": len(raw),
        "sha256": hashlib.sha256(norm).hexdigest() if raw else "",
        "role": role,
        "eol": "LF" if raw and b"\r\n" not in raw else ("CRLF" if raw else "NOT-CHECKED"),
    }


def run_cmd(cmd: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
    p = subprocess.run(
        cmd,
        cwd=str(REPO),
        shell=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        env=env,
    )
    return p.returncode, ((p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or "")).strip()


def live_signal_1() -> dict:
    self_g = graph_from_document(cases.self_cycle())
    two_g = graph_from_document(cases.two_node_cycle())
    long_g = graph_from_document(cases.long_cycle())
    diamond_g = graph_from_document(cases.diamond())

    self_found = detect_cycles(self_g)
    two_found = detect_cycles(two_g)
    long_found = detect_cycles(long_g)
    diamond_found = detect_cycles(diamond_g)

    publish_diamond = check_at_publish(diamond_g)

--- probe1_planted_cycles.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: planted-cycle exact sequences + diamond negative control.

Evidence signal (1): the primary detector reports the exact node sequence of
self-cycle, two-node cycle, and long cycle, and is silent on an acyclic
diamond. Cycle detection is a publish-time gate, not RecursionError at install.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools"))

from marketplace import cycle_cases as cases  # noqa: E402
from marketplace.dependency import (  # noqa: E402
    DependencyCycleError,
    check_at_publish,
    cycle_matches,
    detect_cycles,
    graph_from_document,
    install_order,
    naive_recursive_walk,
    publish_verdict,
)

TEST = REPO / "tools/marketplace/tests/test_dependency_graph.py"


def _paths(g) -> list[list[str]]:
    return [list(c.path) for c in detect_cycles(g)]


def main() -> int:
    proc = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-B", str(TEST)],
        cwd=str(REPO),
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    test_out = ((proc.stdout or "") + (proc.stderr or "")).strip()
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        print(test_out)
        print("FAILED:IMP-0082-probe1 unit tests")
        return 1

    self_g = graph_from_document(cases.self_cycle())
    two_g = graph_from_document(cases.two_node_cycle())
    long_g = graph_from_document(cases.long_cycle())
    diamond_g = graph_from_document(cases.diamond())

    self_found = detect_cycles(self_g)
    two_found = detect_cycles(two_g)
    long_found = detect_cycles(long_g)
    diamond_found = detect_cycles(diamond_g)

    if not cycle_matches(self_found, cases.EXPECTED_SELF):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0082-probe1 self-cycle sequence", _paths(self_g))
        return 1
    if not cycle_matches(two_found, cases.EXPECTED_TWO):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0082-probe1 two-node sequence", _paths(two_g))
        return 1
    if not cycle_matches(long_found, cases.EXPECTED_LONG):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0082-probe1 long-cycle sequence", _paths(long_g))
        return 1
    if diamond_found:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0082-probe1 NEGATIVE CONTROL diamond reported as cycle", _paths(diamond_g))
        return 1

    publish_ok = check_at_publish(diamond_g)
    if not publish_ok.get("ok") or publish_ok.get("stage") != "publish":
        print("FAILED:IMP-0082-probe1 diamond publish gate", publish_ok)
        return 1

    try:
        check_at_publish(two_g)
        print("FAILED:IMP-0082-probe1 two-node cycle was not refused at publish")
        return 1
    except DependencyCycleError as exc:
        if exc.stage != "publish" or "a -> b -> a" not in str(exc):
            print("FAILED:IMP-0082-probe1 cycle error shape", exc.stage, exc)
            return 1

    try:
        install_order(two_g)
        print("FAILED:IMP-0082-probe1 install_order accepted a cycle")
        return 1
    except DependencyCycleError:
        pass

    recursion_fired = False
    old = sys.getrecursionlimit()
    try:
        sys.setrecursionlimit(64)
        try:
            naive_recursive_walk(two_g, "a")

--- probe2_independent_library.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: independent graph library agreement + kind classification.

Evidence signal (2): stdlib graphlib.TopologicalSorter (and a Kosaraju SCC
pass) run over the same edge list agree with the primary detector on
existence AND membership. Optional and peer edges stay distinct from required.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools"))

from marketplace import cycle_cases as cases  # noqa: E402
from marketplace.cycle_ref import independent_report  # noqa: E402
from marketplace.dependency import (  # noqa: E402
    KIND_DEV,
    KIND_OPTIONAL,
    KIND_PEER,
    KIND_REQUIRED,
    cycle_matches,
    detect_cycles,
    graph_from_document,
    membership,
)


def _agree(doc, expected, members: set[str]) -> dict:
    g = graph_from_document(doc)
    found = detect_cycles(g)
    primary_members = membership(found)
    report = independent_report(g.pair_list())
    ok = (
        cycle_matches(found, expected)
        and report["graphlib"]["cyclic"] is True
        and set(report["members"]) == members
        and primary_members == members
        and report["internal_existence_agree"]
        and report["independent_existence"] is True
    )
    return {
        "ok": ok,
        "primary_paths": [list(c.path) for c in found],
        "primary_members": sorted(primary_members),
        "independent": report,
        "expected_members": sorted(members),
    }


def main() -> int:
    self_r = _agree(cases.self_cycle(), cases.EXPECTED_SELF, {"loop"})
    two_r = _agree(cases.two_node_cycle(), cases.EXPECTED_TWO, {"a", "b"})
    long_r = _agree(cases.long_cycle(), cases.EXPECTED_LONG, {"p", "q", "r", "s"})

    diamond_g = graph_from_document(cases.diamond())
    diamond_found = detect_cycles(diamond_g)
    diamond_ind = independent_report(diamond_g.pair_list())
    diamond_ok = (
        diamond_found == []
        and diamond_ind["graphlib"]["cyclic"] is False
        and diamond_ind["members"] == []
        and diamond_ind["independent_existence"] is False
    )

    kinds_g = graph_from_document(cases.kinds_distinct())
    by_kind = kinds_g.classify()
    kinds_ok = (
        by_kind[KIND_REQUIRED] == [("consumer", "lib-req", "^2.0.0")]
        and by_kind[KIND_OPTIONAL] == [("consumer", "lib-opt", "~1.4.0")]
        and by_kind[KIND_PEER] == [("consumer", "lib-peer", ">=3.0.0 <4.0.0")]
        and by_kind[KIND_DEV] == [("consumer", "lib-dev", "*")]
        and {e.kind for e in kinds_g.edges}
        == {KIND_REQUIRED, KIND_OPTIONAL, KIND_PEER, KIND_DEV}
        and detect_cycles(kinds_g) == []
    )

    peer_g = graph_from_document(cases.peer_only_mutual())
    peer_ok = (
        peer_g.classify()[KIND_REQUIRED] == []
        and detect_cycles(peer_g) == []
        and detect_cycles(peer_g, kinds=(KIND_PEER,)) != []
    )

    report = {
        "probe": "probe2-independent-library",
        "unit_id": "IMP-0082",
        "library": "graphlib.TopologicalSorter + Kosaraju SCC",
        "self_cycle": self_r,
        "two_node_cycle": two_r,
        "long_cycle": long_r,
        "diamond_negative_control": {
            "ok": diamond_ok,
            "primary_silent": diamond_found == [],
            "independent": diamond_ind,
        },
        "kinds_distinct_from_required": kinds_ok,

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit IMP-0090 receipt and per-unit G-1 return + manifest.

Order: run tests, write command-center/receipts/IMP-0090.json, re-run named
probes, then write SUBAGENT-RETURN.json and UNIT-ARTIFACT-MANIFEST.json.
Does not run tools/command-center/unit_manifest.py --write.
"""

import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

REPO = HERE.parents[3]

)

)

def digest_file(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def run_cmd(cmd: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    p = subprocess.run(
        cmd,
        cwd=str(REPO),
        shell=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        timeout=180,
    )
    text = ((p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or "")).strip()
    return p.returncode, text


def _load_fix(name: str) -> dict:
    return json.loads((REPO / FIX / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def main() -> int:
    rc_t, out_t = run_cmd(TEST_CMD)
    if rc_t != 0:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090 tests rc={rc_t}")
        print(out_t[-4000:])
        return 1

    mixed = _load_fix("declared-mixed.json")
    none = _load_fix("declared-none.json")
    missing = _load_fix("declared-missing.json")
    deco = _load_fix("declared-decorative.json")
    gated = _load_fix("declared-object-form.json")

    mixed_surface = render_permission_surface(mixed)

--- probe1_rendered_equals_declared.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Named probe 1: rendered surface set equals the declared metadata set.

Parses PERM lines from the rendered text with a local regex. Does not ask
the renderer for the id set, so a renderer that drops or invents an id fails.
Also holds the negative control: declaring nothing prints
'no permissions declared', never a blank screen.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
TOOLS = REPO / "tools"
sys.path.insert(0, str(TOOLS))

from marketplace.permission_catalog import (  # noqa: E402
    EMPTY_SURFACE_LINE,
    extract_declared,
)
from marketplace.permission_surface import (  # noqa: E402
    HEADER,
    high_risk_visually_distinct,
    render_permission_surface,
)

ART = REPO / "command-center" / "receipts" / "IMP-0090.json"
FIX = REPO / "tools" / "marketplace" / "tests" / "fixtures"
PERM_LINE = re.compile(r"^PERM\s+(\S+)\s+RISK=")


def _load(name: str) -> dict:
    return json.loads((FIX / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def _rendered_ids(surface: str) -> set[str]:
    return {m.group(1) for line in surface.splitlines() if (m := PERM_LINE.match(line))}


def main() -> int:
    if not ART.is_file() or ART.stat().st_size < 200:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0090-probe1 receipt missing")
        return 1
    text = ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    try:
        doc = json.loads(text)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090-probe1 receipt unparseable {exc}")
        return 1
    if not isinstance(doc, dict):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0090-probe1 receipt not an object")
        return 1
    if str(doc.get("state") or "").upper() in {
        "BLOCKED",
        "FAILED",
        "SEVERED",
        "TIMEOUT",
    }:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090-probe1 self-declared {doc.get('state')}")
        return 1
    if "SUCCESS:IMP-0090" not in text:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0090-probe1 sentinel absent")
        return 1
    if doc.get("unit_id") != "IMP-0090":
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090-probe1 unit_id {doc.get('unit_id')}")
        return 1

    mixed = _load("declared-mixed.json")
    surface = render_permission_surface(mixed)
    declared = set(extract_declared(mixed))
    rendered = _rendered_ids(surface)
    if rendered != declared:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090-probe1 set mismatch rendered={sorted(rendered)} declared={sorted(declared)}")
        return 1
    if not high_risk_visually_distinct(surface, extract_declared(mixed)):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0090-probe1 high-risk not visually distinct")
        return 1
    if "=== CRITICAL ===" not in surface or "!!!" not in surface:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0090-probe1 critical grouping missing")
        return 1

    empty_ok = True
    for name in ("declared-none.json", "declared-missing.json"):
        pkg = _load(name)
        empty_surface = render_permission_surface(pkg)
        if not empty_surface.strip():
            print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090-probe1 {name} blank screen")
            return 1
        if HEADER not in empty_surface or EMPTY_SURFACE_LINE not in empty_surface:
            print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090-probe1 {name} missing named empty")
            return 1
        if _rendered_ids(empty_surface):
            print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090-probe1 {name} invented PERM lines")
            return 1
        if extract_declared(pkg):
            print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090-probe1 {name} declared not empty")

--- probe2_declared_equals_enforced.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Named probe 2: runtime enforcement query vs declared set.

Does not import marketplace.permission_surface. Queries
plugin.permission_enforcement independently. A declared id with no gated
hook is decorative and must be flagged. The gated-only fixture must match
exactly; the decorative fixture must not be reported as fully enforced.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
TOOLS = REPO / "tools"
sys.path.insert(0, str(TOOLS))

from plugin.permission_enforcement import (  # noqa: E402
    query_decorative,
    query_effective,
    query_enforced_set,
)

ART = REPO / "command-center" / "receipts" / "IMP-0090.json"
FIX = REPO / "tools" / "marketplace" / "tests" / "fixtures"


def _load(name: str) -> dict:
    return json.loads((FIX / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def main() -> int:
    if not ART.is_file() or ART.stat().st_size < 200:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0090-probe2 receipt missing")
        return 1
    text = ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    try:
        doc = json.loads(text)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090-probe2 receipt unparseable {exc}")
        return 1
    if not isinstance(doc, dict):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0090-probe2 receipt not an object")
        return 1
    if str(doc.get("state") or "").upper() in {
        "BLOCKED",
        "FAILED",
        "SEVERED",
        "TIMEOUT",
    }:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090-probe2 self-declared {doc.get('state')}")
        return 1
    if "SUCCESS:IMP-0090" not in text:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0090-probe2 sentinel absent")
        return 1

    gated = _load("declared-object-form.json")
    gated_eff = query_effective(gated)
    if not gated_eff["declared_equals_enforced"]:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090-probe2 gated fixture mismatch {gated_eff}")
        return 1
    if gated_eff["decorative"]:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090-probe2 gated fixture decorative {gated_eff['decorative']}")
        return 1
    if set(gated_eff["enforced"]) != {"filesystem.write", "network.connect"}:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090-probe2 gated enforced {gated_eff['enforced']}")
        return 1

    mixed = _load("declared-mixed.json")
    mixed_eff = query_effective(mixed)
    if not mixed_eff["declared_equals_enforced"]:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090-probe2 mixed mismatch {mixed_eff}")
        return 1

    deco = _load("declared-decorative.json")
    deco_enforced = set(query_enforced_set(deco))
    deco_flags = set(query_decorative(deco))
    if deco_enforced != {"filesystem.write"}:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090-probe2 decorative enforced {deco_enforced}")
        return 1
    if deco_flags != {"ui.notify", "telemetry.collect"}:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0090-probe2 decorative flags {deco_flags}")
        return 1
    if not deco_flags:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0090-probe2 decorative declarations were not flagged")
        return 1

    empty = _load("declared-none.json")
    if query_enforced_set(empty) or query_decorative(empty):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0090-probe2 empty package not empty")
        return 1

    payload = {
        "decorative_flagged": sorted(deco_flags),
        "gated_equal": True,
        "mixed_equal": True,
        "pass": True,
        "probe": "probe2-declared-equals-enforced",

--- probe1_matrix.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: 20-case affected/unaffected matrix on the advisory interval engine.

Signal 1 of IMP-0099. Boundary loci (at-fixed, prerelease-of-fixed) are
asserted by name, not averaged into a pass rate.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools"))

from marketplace.advisory import from_record, is_affected  # noqa: E402

FIX = REPO / "tools/marketplace/fixtures/imp0099_matrix.json"
ART = REPO / "command-center/receipts/IMP-0099.json"
REQUIRED_LOCI = {
    "below",
    "at-lower-bound",
    "inside",
    "at-fixed",
    "above",
    "prerelease-of-fixed",
}


def main() -> int:
    doc = json.loads(FIX.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    cases = doc["cases"]
    if len(cases) != 20:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0099-probe1 matrix size {len(cases)} want=20")
        return 1
    loci = {c["locus"] for c in cases}
    missing = REQUIRED_LOCI - loci
    if missing:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0099-probe1 missing loci {sorted(missing)}")
        return 1
    adv = from_record(doc["advisory"])
    rows = []
    fail = False
    for row in cases:
        got = is_affected(adv, row["version"])
        ok = got == row["expected_affected"]
        if not ok:
            fail = True
        rec = {
            "id": row["id"],
            "version": row["version"],
            "locus": row["locus"],
            "expected": row["expected_affected"],
            "advisory_engine": got,
            "match": ok,
        }
        rows.append(rec)
        print(
            f"case {row['id']:02d} version={row['version']:<16} "
            f"locus={row['locus']:<22} expected={row['expected_affected']!s:<5} "
            f"got={got!s:<5} ok={ok}"
        )
    matched = sum(1 for r in rows if r["match"])
    print(f"matched={matched}/20 fail={fail}")

    at_fixed = [r for r in rows if r["locus"] == "at-fixed"]
    pre_fixed = [r for r in rows if r["locus"] == "prerelease-of-fixed"]
    if not at_fixed or any(r["advisory_engine"] for r in at_fixed):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0099-probe1 fixed-version boundary off-by-one")
        return 1
    if not pre_fixed or not all(r["advisory_engine"] for r in pre_fixed):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0099-probe1 prerelease-of-fixed must be affected")
        return 1

    if ART.is_file():
        text = ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if "SUCCESS:IMP-0099" not in text:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0099-probe1 receipt sentinel absent")
            return 1
        receipt = json.loads(text)
        st = str(receipt.get("state") or "").upper()
        if st in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
            print(f"FAILED:IMP-0099-probe1 receipt self-declared {st}")
            return 1

    summary = {
        "probe": "probe1-matrix",
        "unit_id": "IMP-0099",
        "cases": 20,
        "matched": matched,
        "at_fixed_unaffected": True,
        "prerelease_of_fixed_affected": True,
        "pass": not fail and matched == 20,
    }
    print(json.dumps(summary, sort_keys=True))
    if fail or matched != 20:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0099-probe1 matrix disagreement")
        return 1
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0099-probe1 20-case advisory range matrix")

--- probe2_independent_engine.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: IMP-0070 comparator engine vs advisory intervals, plus controls.

Mechanically different from probe 1: does not walk advisory intervals.
Converts the same OSV events to an npm-like range and evaluates with
``version.satisfies`` (caret/tilde/relational comparator walker).
Also: sibling-package negative control, and open-ended disclosure.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools"))

from marketplace.advisory import (  # noqa: E402
    OpenEndedUndisclosedError,
    evaluate_installed,
    from_record,
    is_affected,
)
from marketplace.version import events_to_range, satisfies  # noqa: E402

FIX = REPO / "tools/marketplace/fixtures/imp0099_matrix.json"
ART = REPO / "command-center/receipts/IMP-0099.json"


def main() -> int:
    doc = json.loads(FIX.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    adv = from_record(doc["advisory"])
    spec = events_to_range(list(adv.affected_version_ranges))
    if spec != doc["imp0070_range"]:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0099-probe2 range {spec!r} != {doc['imp0070_range']!r}")
        return 1
    disagree = []
    for row in doc["cases"]:
        interval = is_affected(adv, row["version"])
        comparator = satisfies(row["version"], spec, include_prerelease=True)
        ok = interval == comparator == row["expected_affected"]
        print(
            f"case {row['id']:02d} version={row['version']:<16} "
            f"interval={interval!s:<5} comparator={comparator!s:<5} "
            f"expected={row['expected_affected']!s:<5} ok={ok}"
        )
        if not ok:
            disagree.append(row["version"])
    if disagree:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0099-probe2 engine disagreement {disagree}")
        return 1

    ctrl = doc["negative_control"]
    pkg = doc["package"]
    sib = doc["sibling_package"]
    bad = evaluate_installed(pkg, ctrl["affected_package_version"], [adv])
    good_ver = evaluate_installed(pkg, ctrl["unaffected_sibling_version"], [adv])
    good_pkg = evaluate_installed(sib, ctrl["unaffected_sibling_package_version"], [adv])
    print(
        f"negative_control affected={pkg}@{ctrl['affected_package_version']} "
        f"flagged={bad.affected} installable={bad.installable}"
    )
    print(
        f"negative_control sibling_version={pkg}@{ctrl['unaffected_sibling_version']} "
        f"flagged={good_ver.affected} installable={good_ver.installable}"
    )
    print(
        f"negative_control sibling_package={sib}@"
        f"{ctrl['unaffected_sibling_package_version']} "
        f"flagged={good_pkg.affected} installable={good_pkg.installable}"
    )
    if not (
        bad.affected
        and not bad.installable
        and not good_ver.affected
        and good_ver.installable
        and not good_pkg.affected
        and good_pkg.installable
    ):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0099-probe2 sibling negative control")
        return 1

    open_adv = from_record(doc["open_ended_advisory"])
    future = evaluate_installed(pkg, "99.0.0", [open_adv])
    past = evaluate_installed(pkg, "8.9.9", [open_adv])
    print(
        f"open_ended future_affected={future.affected} "
        f"disclosed={bool(future.open_ended_hits)} notes={len(future.notes)}"
    )
    print(f"open_ended past_affected={past.affected}")
    if not future.affected or not future.open_ended_hits or past.affected:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0099-probe2 open-ended disclosure")
        return 1
    silent = dict(doc["open_ended_advisory"])
    silent["unbounded_future_disclosed"] = False
    refused = False
    try:
        from_record(silent)
    except OpenEndedUndisclosedError:

--- emit_receipt.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit IMP-0091 receipt, then per-unit SUBAGENT-RETURN and UNIT-ARTIFACT-MANIFEST."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
TOOLS = REPO / "tools"
sys.path.insert(0, str(TOOLS))

from marketplace.permission_analysis import (  # noqa: E402
    NET,
    STATIC_LIMITATION,
    analyze_catalog_triplet,
    analyze as analyze_pkg,
    seed_all_fixtures,
    write_fixture,
)

import tempfile

OUT = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0091.json"
P1 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0091/probe1_fixtures.py"
)
P2 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0091/probe2_dynamic_dispatch.py"
)
SUPPORTING = [
    "tools/marketplace/permission_analysis.py",
    "tools/marketplace/tests/test_permission_analysis.py",
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0091/probe1_fixtures.py",
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0091/probe2_dynamic_dispatch.py",
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0091/emit_receipt.py",
]
QUESTION = (
    "Statically analyse a package's code against its declared permissions and "
    "report both directions: permissions declared but never exercised (excessive) "
    "and capabilities used but not declared (undeclared). The seeded abuse class "
    "is a package with malicious or excessive permissions."
)


def digest(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def run_cmd(cmd: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    p = subprocess.run(
        cmd,
        cwd=str(REPO),
        shell=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    return p.returncode, ((p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or "")).strip()


def run_unittests() -> dict:
    code, text = run_cmd("python3 -B tools/marketplace/tests/test_permission_analysis.py")
    passed = code == 0
    ran = text.count(" ... ok")
    return {
        "tests_run": ran if passed else ran,
        "failures": 0 if passed else 1,
        "errors": 0,
        "passed": passed,
        "command": "python3 -B tools/marketplace/tests/test_permission_analysis.py",
        "result": "PASS" if passed else "FAIL",
        "tail": text[-2000:],
    }


def live_evidence() -> dict:
    triplet = analyze_catalog_triplet()
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0091-dispatch-") as td:
        dispatch = analyze_pkg(write_fixture(td, "dispatch-kit"))
    excessive = triplet["excessive-kit"]
    undeclared = triplet["undeclared-kit"]
    exact = triplet["exact-kit"]
    ok = (
        NET in undeclared.undeclared
        and undeclared.verdict != "CLEAN"
        and undeclared.detector_control == "undeclared-network-caught-before-clean"
        and exact.findings_count == 0
        and exact.verdict == "CLEAN"
        and "net:connect" in excessive.excessive
        and "spawn:python" in excessive.excessive
        and NET not in dispatch.static_used
        and NET in dispatch.dynamic_used
        and any(d.get("kind") == "static_misses_dynamic_dispatch" for d in dispatch.discrepancies)

--- probe1_fixtures.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: catalog fixtures — excessive, undeclared, exact-match in one run.

DETECTOR CONTROL: undeclared network is caught before CLEAN.
NEGATIVE CONTROL: exact-match reports zero findings in that same run.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]
TEST = REPO / "tools/marketplace/tests/test_permission_analysis.py"
ART = REPO / "command-center/receipts/IMP-0091.json"
TOOLS = REPO / "tools"
if str(TOOLS) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(TOOLS))

from marketplace.permission_analysis import analyze_catalog_triplet, NET  # noqa: E402


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:IMP-0091-probe1 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    proc = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-B", str(TEST)],
        cwd=str(REPO),
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    text = ((proc.stdout or "") + (proc.stderr or "")).strip()
    print(text)
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        return fail("unittest")

    needed = (
        "test_excessive_fixture",
        "test_undeclared_fixture",
        "test_exact_match_fixture",
        "test_detector_control_undeclared_network_blocks_clean",
        "test_negative_control_exact_match_zero_findings_same_run",
    )
    for name in needed:
        if name not in text:
            return fail(f"missing {name}")

    reports = analyze_catalog_triplet()
    undeclared = reports["undeclared-kit"]
    exact = reports["exact-kit"]
    excessive = reports["excessive-kit"]
    if NET not in undeclared.undeclared or undeclared.verdict == "CLEAN":
        return fail("detector control did not catch undeclared network")
    if exact.findings_count != 0 or exact.verdict != "CLEAN":
        return fail(f"negative control findings={exact.findings_count} verdict={exact.verdict}")
    if "net:connect" not in excessive.excessive or "spawn:python" not in excessive.excessive:
        return fail(f"abuse class not excessive: {excessive.excessive}")

    if ART.is_file():
        doc = json.loads(ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        blob = json.dumps(doc)
        if doc.get("unit_id") != "IMP-0091":
            return fail(f"unit_id {doc.get('unit_id')}")
        if "SUCCESS:IMP-0091" not in blob:
            return fail("sentinel absent")
        st = str(doc.get("state") or "").upper()
        if st in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
            return fail(f"self-declared {st}")

    summary = {
        "probe": "probe1-fixtures",
        "unit_id": "IMP-0091",
        "undeclared_verdict": undeclared.verdict,
        "undeclared_caps": undeclared.undeclared,
        "exact_findings": exact.findings_count,
        "exact_verdict": exact.verdict,
        "excessive": excessive.excessive,
        "detector_control": undeclared.detector_control,
        "pass": True,
    }
    print(json.dumps(summary, sort_keys=True))
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0091-probe1 catalog fixtures, detector control, negative control")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe2_dynamic_dispatch.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: reviewer hide-behind-dynamic-dispatch.

Static pass must miss the computed getattr network call; dynamic (strace)
must catch it; the discrepancy must be named. The limitation is documented
inline on the receipt, not hidden.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]
ART = REPO / "command-center/receipts/IMP-0091.json"
TOOLS = REPO / "tools"
HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
if str(TOOLS) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(TOOLS))

from marketplace.permission_analysis import (  # noqa: E402
    NET,
    STATIC_LIMITATION,
    analyze,
    write_fixture,
)


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:IMP-0091-probe2 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0091-p2-") as td:
        root = write_fixture(td, "dispatch-kit")
        report = analyze(root)

    if NET in report.static_used:
        return fail(f"static unexpectedly saw network: {report.static_used}")
    if NET not in report.dynamic_used:
        return fail(
            f"dynamic missed network method={report.dynamic_method} used={report.dynamic_used} detail={report.dynamic_detail}"
        )
    if NET not in report.union_used or NET not in report.undeclared:
        return fail(f"union/undeclared missing net: union={report.union_used} und={report.undeclared}")
    kinds = [d.get("kind") for d in report.discrepancies]
    if "static_misses_dynamic_dispatch" not in kinds:
        return fail(f"discrepancy not named: {report.discrepancies}")
    if report.verdict == "CLEAN":
        return fail("CLEAN despite undeclared dynamic-dispatch network")
    if STATIC_LIMITATION not in report.static_limitation:
        return fail("static limitation text missing on report")

    if not ART.is_file():
        return fail("receipt absent")
    doc = json.loads(ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    blob = json.dumps(doc)
    if "SUCCESS:IMP-0091" not in blob:
        return fail("sentinel absent")
    if str(doc.get("state") or "").upper() in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
        return fail(f"self-declared {doc.get('state')}")
    reviewer = doc.get("reviewer") or {}
    if not reviewer.get("independent"):
        return fail("reviewer.independent is not true")
    if "dynamic dispatch" not in blob.lower() and "dynamic-dispatch" not in blob.lower():
        return fail("receipt hides the dynamic-dispatch limitation")
    if "getattr" not in blob.lower() and "computed" not in blob.lower():
        return fail("receipt does not name getattr/computed dispatch")
    if STATIC_LIMITATION not in blob and "expected limitation" not in blob.lower():
        return fail("receipt does not document the static limitation inline")

    summary = {
        "probe": "probe2-dynamic-dispatch",
        "unit_id": "IMP-0091",
        "static_used": report.static_used,
        "dynamic_used": report.dynamic_used,
        "dynamic_method": report.dynamic_method,
        "union_used": report.union_used,
        "discrepancy_kinds": kinds,
        "verdict": report.verdict,
        "reviewer_verdict": reviewer.get("verdict"),
        "pass": True,
    }
    print(json.dumps(summary, sort_keys=True))
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0091-probe2 static misses dynamic dispatch; named; documented inline")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Write IMP-0105 catalog receipt, then per-unit SUBAGENT-RETURN and manifest.

Hashes the receipt after it is on disk so the manifest sha256 matches G-1.
Does not run tools/command-center/unit_manifest.py --write.

import hashlib
import json
import os

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]

def digest(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def run_cmd(cmd: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    proc = subprocess.run(
        cmd,
        cwd=str(REPO),
        shell=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        env={**os.environ, "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"},
        timeout=120,
    )
    text = ((proc.stdout or "") + (proc.stderr or "")).strip()
    return proc.returncode, text


def row(rel: str, role: str) -> dict:
    sha, size = digest(REPO / rel)
    return {
        "bytes": size,
        "path": rel,
        "present": True,
        "receipt_path": "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0105/SUBAGENT-RETURN.json",
        "role": role,
        "sha256": sha,
        "unit_id": "IMP-0105",
    }

                "sat_milli": sat,
                "term": component["term"],

--- probe1_reconstruct_score.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: reconstruct ranking and quality scores from `why` output alone.

Independent arithmetic: millipoint BM25 and quality weights are reimplemented
here and are not imported from the library. The live `why` command is invoked
as a subprocess so the printed JSON is the evidence, not an in-process handle.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]
WHY = REPO / "tools" / "marketplace" / "explain.py"
SCALE = 1000
K1 = 1200
B = 750


def independent_sat(tf: int, dl: int, avgdl: int) -> int:
    if tf <= 0:
        return 0
    avgdl = max(avgdl, 1)
    length = (SCALE - B) + (B * dl) // avgdl
    numerator = tf * (K1 + SCALE) * SCALE * SCALE
    denominator = tf * SCALE * SCALE + K1 * length
    return numerator // denominator


def independent_idf(n_docs: int, df: int) -> int:
    return ((n_docs - df + 1) * SCALE) // max(df, 1)


def run_why(subject: str, package: str, query: str | None = None) -> dict:
    cmd = [
        sys.executable,
        "-B",
        str(WHY),
        "why",
        subject,
        "--package",
        package,
    ]
    if query is not None:
        cmd.extend(["--query", query])
    proc = subprocess.run(
        cmd,
        cwd=str(REPO),
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        env={**os.environ, "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"},
        timeout=60,
    )
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"why exit {proc.returncode}: {(proc.stdout or '') + (proc.stderr or '')}"
        )
    first = (proc.stdout or "").splitlines()[0]
    return json.loads(first)


def main() -> int:
    report = run_why("search", "pkg.alpha", "json lint")
    ranking_rep = report.get("ranking") or {}
    mismatches = []
    reconstructed = 0
    for row in ranking_rep.get("components") or []:
        sat = independent_sat(int(row["tf_weighted"]), int(row["dl"]), int(row["avgdl"]))
        idf = independent_idf(int(row["N"]), int(row["df"]))
        value = (idf * sat) // SCALE
        reconstructed += value
        if value != int(row["value"]):
            mismatches.append(
                {
                    "id": row.get("id"),
                    "printed": row.get("value"),
                    "recomputed": value,
                    "idf": idf,
                    "sat": sat,
                }
            )
        if sat != int(row["sat_milli"]) or idf != int(row["idf_milli"]):
            mismatches.append(
                {
                    "id": row.get("id"),
                    "kind": "printed-intermediates",
                    "sat_printed": row.get("sat_milli"),
                    "sat_recomputed": sat,
                    "idf_printed": row.get("idf_milli"),
                    "idf_recomputed": idf,
                }
            )

    quality_rep = report.get("quality") or {}
    q_sum = 0
    for row in quality_rep.get("components") or []:

--- probe2_input_change_and_deny_cell.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: one-input change moves score+line; deny names the policy cell.

Both-states in one process: ALLOW (pkg.alpha) and DENY (pkg.delta). A deny
that only says 'denied' without the (signature, trust, advisory) triple fails.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools" / "marketplace"))

import explain  # noqa: E402


def main() -> int:
    corpus = explain.demo_corpus()
    before = explain.why(
        package_id="pkg.alpha",
        corpus=corpus,
        query="json lint",
        subject="search",
    )
    lint_before = next(
        row for row in before["ranking"]["components"] if row["term"] == "lint"
    )
    conf_before = next(
        row
        for row in before["quality"]["components"]
        if row["id"] == "conformance_pass_rate"
    )
    others_before = {
        row["id"]: int(row["value"])
        for row in before["quality"]["components"]
        if row["id"] != "conformance_pass_rate"
    }

    mutated = copy.deepcopy(corpus)
    alpha = next(pkg for pkg in mutated if pkg["id"] == "pkg.alpha")
    alpha["name"] = "alpha-json-helper"
    alpha["description"] = "json documents in a workspace"
    alpha["tags"] = ["json"]
    alpha["skills"] = ["json.help"]
    alpha["conformance_pass_rate"] = 100
    after = explain.why(
        package_id="pkg.alpha",
        corpus=mutated,
        query="json lint",
        subject="search",
    )
    lint_after = next(
        row for row in after["ranking"]["components"] if row["term"] == "lint"
    )
    conf_after = next(
        row
        for row in after["quality"]["components"]
        if row["id"] == "conformance_pass_rate"
    )
    others_after = {
        row["id"]: int(row["value"])
        for row in after["quality"]["components"]
        if row["id"] != "conformance_pass_rate"
    }

    ranking_moved = (
        int(lint_after["value"]) != int(lint_before["value"])
        and int(after["ranking"]["score_milli"]) != int(before["ranking"]["score_milli"])
        and int(lint_after["value"]) == 0
    )
    quality_moved = (
        int(conf_after["value"]) != int(conf_before["value"])
        and int(after["quality"]["score_milli"]) != int(before["quality"]["score_milli"])
        and others_after == others_before
    )

    denied = explain.why(package_id="pkg.delta", corpus=corpus, subject="install")
    allowed = explain.why(package_id="pkg.alpha", corpus=corpus, subject="install")
    deny_cell = denied["policy"]["cell_id"]
    allow_cell = allowed["policy"]["cell_id"]
    deny_ok = (
        denied["policy"]["verdict"] == "DENY"
        and denied["policy"]["specified_cell"] is True
        and deny_cell == "signature=invalid|trust=LOW|advisory=critical"
        and "policy cell" in denied["policy"]["message"]
        and deny_cell in denied["policy"]["message"]
        and deny_cell in "\n".join(denied["human_lines"])
    )
    allow_ok = (
        allowed["policy"]["verdict"] == "ALLOW"
        and allow_cell == "signature=valid|trust=HIGH|advisory=clean"
        and allow_cell != deny_cell
    )
    generic_only = (
        "deny" in denied["policy"]["message"].lower()
        and "signature=" not in denied["policy"]["message"]

--- probe1_set_parity.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: both-direction set difference, independently recomputed, plus
the catalog negative control (remove one doc entry → named exactly)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import shutil
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools"))

from marketplace.docs_parity import (  # noqa: E402
    Ident,
    compute_parity,
    drop_documented_entry,
)
from marketplace.tests.test_docs_parity import (  # noqa: E402
    test_both_direction_sets_are_empty,
    test_removed_doc_entry_is_named_exactly,
)

GOOD = REPO / "tools/marketplace/fixtures/docs-parity-good"


def _independent_declared(root: Path) -> set[tuple[str, str]]:
    data = json.loads((root / "plugin.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    out: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
    for item in data["commands"]:
        out.add(("command", item["id"]))
    for path in data["skills"]:
        out.add(("skill", Path(path).parent.name))
    for path in data["hooks"]:
        out.add(("hook", Path(path).stem))
    return out


def _independent_documented_surface(root: Path) -> set[tuple[str, str]]:
    """Parse SURFACE.md bullets only — not the library parser."""
    text = (root / "docs" / "SURFACE.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    kind = None
    out: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
    mapping = {"commands": "command", "skills": "skill", "hooks": "hook"}
    for line in text.splitlines():
        stripped = line.strip()
        if stripped.startswith("## "):
            token = stripped[3:].strip().lower()
            kind = mapping.get(token)
            continue
        if kind and stripped.startswith("- "):
            out.add((kind, stripped[2:].strip().strip("`")))
    return out


def main() -> int:
    test_both_direction_sets_are_empty()
    test_removed_doc_entry_is_named_exactly()

    report = compute_parity(GOOD)
    lib_decl = {(i.kind, i.name) for i in report.declared}
    lib_docs = {(i.kind, i.name) for i in report.documented}
    ind_decl = _independent_declared(GOOD)
    ind_docs = _independent_documented_surface(GOOD)
    if not ind_decl or not ind_docs:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0120-probe1 independent parse empty")
        return 1
    if lib_decl != ind_decl:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0120-probe1 declared mismatch", sorted(lib_decl), sorted(ind_decl))
        return 1
    if lib_docs != ind_docs:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0120-probe1 documented mismatch", sorted(lib_docs), sorted(ind_docs))
        return 1
    if lib_decl != lib_docs:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0120-probe1 set difference not empty", report.as_dict())
        return 1
    if not report.both_empty:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0120-probe1 both_empty false")
        return 1

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        clone = Path(tmp) / "pkg"
        shutil.copytree(GOOD, clone)
        drop_documented_entry(clone, "status", "command")
        mutated = compute_parity(clone)
        if mutated.named_commands() != ["status"]:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0120-probe1 negative control", mutated.as_dict())
            return 1
        if mutated.declared_minus_documented != {Ident("command", "status")}:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0120-probe1 negative control set", mutated.as_dict())
            return 1
        if mutated.documented_minus_declared:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0120-probe1 negative control extra", mutated.as_dict())
            return 1

    payload = {
        "pass": True,
        "probe": "probe1-set-parity",

--- probe2_commands_run.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: every documented command executed in documented form, plus
reviewer-required verbatim run of three documented commands."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools"))

from marketplace.docs_commands import (  # noqa: E402
    extract_documented_invocations,
    run_documented_commands,
)
from marketplace.tests.test_docs_commands_run import (  # noqa: E402
    test_broken_command_is_detected,
    test_every_documented_command_executes,
)

GOOD = REPO / "tools/marketplace/fixtures/docs-parity-good"
REVIEWER = [
    "python3 commands/hello.py",
    "python3 commands/status.py",
    "python3 commands/version.py",
]


def _run_verbatim(line: str) -> dict:
    proc = subprocess.run(
        line,
        cwd=str(GOOD),
        shell=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        timeout=30,
    )
    return {
        "argv_line": line,
        "returncode": proc.returncode,
        "stdout": (proc.stdout or "").strip(),
        "stderr": (proc.stderr or "").strip(),
        "ok": proc.returncode == 0,
    }


def main() -> int:
    test_every_documented_command_executes()
    test_broken_command_is_detected()

    invocations = extract_documented_invocations(GOOD)
    if len(invocations) < 4:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0120-probe2 too few invocations {len(invocations)}")
        return 1
    report = run_documented_commands(GOOD)
    if not report.ok:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0120-probe2 documented commands did not match exit")
        print(json.dumps(report.as_dict(), indent=2)[:2000])
        return 1

    reviewer_rows = [_run_verbatim(line) for line in REVIEWER]
    if any(not row["ok"] for row in reviewer_rows):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0120-probe2 reviewer verbatim command failed")
        print(json.dumps(reviewer_rows, indent=2))
        return 1

    unknown = next(r for r in report.results if "--unknown" in r.invocation.argv_line)
    if unknown.returncode != 2 or unknown.invocation.expected_exit != 2:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0120-probe2 documented exit-2 behaviour not observed")
        return 1

    payload = {
        "pass": True,
        "probe": "probe2-commands-run",
        "unit_id": "IMP-0120",
        "executed": len(report.results),
        "all_matched": True,
        "reviewer_verbatim": reviewer_rows,
        "documented_exit_2_observed": True,
        "results": [
            {
                "name": r.invocation.name,
                "argv_line": r.invocation.argv_line,
                "expected_exit": r.invocation.expected_exit,
                "returncode": r.returncode,
                "matched": r.matched,
            }
            for r in report.results
        ],
    }
    print(json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True))
    print(
        f"executed={len(report.results)} all_matched=True "
        f"reviewer={len(reviewer_rows)} exit2={unknown.returncode}"
    )
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0120-probe2 documented commands execute with documented exit")
    return 0

--- emit_receipt.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/IMP-0121.json from live store measurements."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
OUT = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0121.json"
P1 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0121/probe1_dedupe_listing.py"
)
P2 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0121/probe2_verify_refcount.py"
)
PYTEST = [
    sys.executable,
    "-B",
    "tools/marketplace/tests/test_blob_store.py",
]


def utc_now() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def run(cmd, timeout: int = 120) -> tuple[int, str]:
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
    if isinstance(cmd, str):
        p = subprocess.run(
            cmd,
            cwd=str(REPO),
            shell=True,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=timeout,
            env=env,
        )
    else:
        p = subprocess.run(
            cmd,
            cwd=str(REPO),
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=timeout,
            env=env,
        )
    text = ((p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or "")).strip()
    return p.returncode, text


def art(rel: str, role: str) -> dict:
    path = REPO / rel
    if not path.is_file():
        return {
            "path": rel,
            "exists": False,
            "bytes": 0,
            "sha256": None,
            "role": role,
        }
    sha, size = sha256_file(path)
    return {
        "path": rel,
        "exists": True,
        "bytes": size,
        "sha256": sha,
        "role": role,
    }


def main() -> int:
    started = utc_now()
    t1_rc, t1_out = run(PYTEST)
    p1_rc, p1_out = run(P1)
    p2_rc, p2_out = run(P2)
    tests_ok = t1_rc == 0 and "SUCCESS:test_blob_store" in t1_out
    pytest_note = (
        "stdlib runner python3 tools/marketplace/tests/test_blob_store.py "
        f"(pytest not required); exit={t1_rc}"
    )

    store = art("tools/marketplace/blob_store.py", "implementation")
    tests = art("tools/marketplace/tests/test_blob_store.py", "supporting")
    probe1 = art(
        "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0121/probe1_dedupe_listing.py",

--- probe1_dedupe_listing.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Named probe 1: storing the same blob twice yields one on-disk object.

Signal 1 is a directory listing of objects/ (not a store-internal counter).
Signal 2 is the persisted index.json refcount for the same digest, loaded
with json.loads so it is independent of BlobStore.refcount memory.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools" / "marketplace"))

from blob_store import (  # noqa: E402
    BlobStore,
    digest_bytes,
    list_object_files,
    refcount_from_index,
)

PAYLOAD = b"IMP-0121-probe1-identical-blob\n"


def main() -> int:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0121-p1-") as tmp:
        root = Path(tmp)
        store = BlobStore(root)
        d1 = store.put(PAYLOAD, "version:1.0.0")
        d2 = store.put(PAYLOAD, "collection:core")
        files = list_object_files(root)
        listing = [p.relative_to(root).as_posix() for p in files]
        index_raw = (root / "index.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        index = json.loads(index_raw)
        derived = refcount_from_index(index, d1)
        expected = digest_bytes(PAYLOAD)

        result = {
            "probe": "probe1-dedupe-listing",
            "unit_id": "IMP-0121",
            "digest": d1,
            "put_twice_same_digest": d1 == d2 == expected,
            "directory_listing": listing,
            "object_file_count": len(files),
            "index_refcount": derived,
            "index_refs": sorted((index.get("objects") or {}).get(d1, {}).get("refs") or {}),
            "pass": False,
        }
        if d1 != d2 or d1 != expected:
            print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
            print("FAILED:IMP-0121-probe1 digest mismatch")
            return 1
        if len(files) != 1:
            print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
            print(f"FAILED:IMP-0121-probe1 object_file_count={len(files)} want=1")
            return 1
        if derived != 2:
            print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
            print(f"FAILED:IMP-0121-probe1 index_refcount={derived} want=2")
            return 1
        other = store.put(b"IMP-0121-probe1-other\n", "version:other")
        files_after = list_object_files(root)
        if other == d1 or len(files_after) != 2:
            result["other_digest"] = other
            result["object_file_count_after_distinct"] = len(files_after)
            print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
            print("FAILED:IMP-0121-probe1 distinct blob did not add a second object")
            return 1
        result["object_file_count_after_distinct"] = 2
        result["other_digest"] = other
        result["pass"] = True
        print(json.dumps(result, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
        print(
            f"listing={listing} files={len(files)} refs={result['index_refs']} "
            f"refcount={derived}"
        )
        print("SUCCESS:IMP-0121-probe1 one on-disk object for two puts of identical bytes")
        return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe2_verify_refcount.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Named probe 2: verify-on-read, refuse referenced delete, crash-survivable refcount.

Independent of probe1's tempfile. Three claims, each with a both-states check:
  * a mutated object is detected on read (bad) and an intact object is served (good)
  * delete of a still-referenced blob is refused (bad) and delete after release
    succeeds (good)
  * after dropping the live BlobStore, refcount is re-derived from index.json
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools" / "marketplace"))

from blob_store import (  # noqa: E402
    BlobStore,
    CorruptedBlobError,
    ReferencedBlobError,
    digest_bytes,
    list_object_files,
    load_index_file,
    refcount_from_index,
)

PAYLOAD = b"IMP-0121-probe2-verify-me\n"
MUTATED = b"IMP-0121-probe2-MUTATED\n"


def main() -> int:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0121-p2-") as tmp:
        root = Path(tmp)

        # --- intact read (good state) ---
        store = BlobStore(root)
        digest = store.put(PAYLOAD, "install:pkg@1.0")
        served = store.get(digest)
        if served != PAYLOAD or digest_bytes(served) != digest:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0121-probe2 intact read did not round-trip")
            return 1

        # --- mutated read (bad state): must raise, must not return MUTATED ---
        files = list_object_files(root)
        if len(files) != 1:
            print(f"FAILED:IMP-0121-probe2 unexpected object count {len(files)}")
            return 1
        files[0].write_bytes(MUTATED)
        detected = False
        served_mutated = False
        actual = None
        try:
            leaked = store.get(digest)
            served_mutated = leaked == MUTATED
        except CorruptedBlobError as err:
            detected = True
            actual = err.actual
        if not detected or served_mutated or actual != digest_bytes(MUTATED):
            print(
                json.dumps(
                    {
                        "detected": detected,
                        "served_mutated": served_mutated,
                        "actual": actual,
                    }
                )
            )
            print("FAILED:IMP-0121-probe2 corruption not detected on read")
            return 1
        # Restore intact bytes so later checks use a real object.
        files[0].write_bytes(PAYLOAD)
        if store.get(digest) != PAYLOAD:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0121-probe2 restored object not served")
            return 1

        # --- referenced delete refused (bad) ---
        store.put(PAYLOAD, "install:pkg@2.0")
        refused = False
        try:
            store.delete(digest)
        except ReferencedBlobError as err:
            refused = err.refcount == 2
        still_there = bool(list_object_files(root))
        if not refused or not still_there:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0121-probe2 referenced delete was not refused")
            return 1

        # --- crash survival: drop instance, re-derive from index.json ---
        del store
        index = load_index_file(root)
        derived = refcount_from_index(index, digest)
        if derived != 2:
            print(f"FAILED:IMP-0121-probe2 crash-derived refcount={derived} want=2")
            return 1
        reopened = BlobStore(root)
        if reopened.refcount(digest) != 2:

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: known rail PASS, unknown rail FAIL, no-rival-core AST walk green."""

from __future__ import annotations

import runpy
import sys
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]
ROUTING = REPO / "tools" / "agentspec" / "tests" / "test_workunit_routing.py"
RIVAL = REPO / "tools" / "agentrail" / "tests" / "test_no_rival_core.py"


def _run(path: Path) -> None:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(path.parent))
    ns = runpy.run_path(str(path), run_name="imp0135_probe1")
    code = ns["main"]()
    if code not in (0, None):
        raise SystemExit(f"FAILED:IMP-0135-probe1 {path.name} rc={code}")


def main() -> int:
    _run(ROUTING)
    _run(RIVAL)
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0135-probe1 known/unknown rail and no-rival-core")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        raise SystemExit(main())
    except SystemExit:
        raise
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0135-probe1 {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        raise SystemExit(1)

--- probe2_mutation_default_vendor.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: hardcode a vendor default in the resolver; unknown-rail test goes red."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Mapping, Union

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools" / "agentspec"))
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools" / "agentrail"))

from agentspec.spec import Routing  # noqa: E402
from agentrail.rails import RailRecord, load_rails  # noqa: E402
from agentrail.resolve import (  # noqa: E402
    UNKNOWN_RAIL,
    ResolvedRail,
    RoutingViolation,
    match_rail,
    resolve_routing,
)

UNKNOWN = Routing(
    vendor="vendor-not-in-rails",
    model="no-such-model",
    role="no-such-role",
)
RESOLVE_PY = REPO / "tools" / "agentrail" / "agentrail" / "resolve.py"


def mutant_resolve_routing(
    routing: Routing,
    *,
    rails: Mapping[str, RailRecord] | None = None,
) -> Union[ResolvedRail, RoutingViolation]:
    """Same as resolve_routing, but a miss silently takes the first rails.toml row."""
    table = rails if rails is not None else load_rails()
    record = match_rail(table, routing)
    if record is None:
        record = next(iter(table.values()))  # MUTANT: silent vendor default
    return ResolvedRail(
        name=record.name,
        vendor=record.vendor,
        model=record.model,
        role=record.role,
        fallbacks=tuple(routing.fallbacks),
    )


def _unknown_must_fail(resolver) -> None:
    result = resolver(UNKNOWN)
    assert isinstance(result, RoutingViolation), result
    assert result.id == UNKNOWN_RAIL, result


def main() -> int:
    src = RESOLVE_PY.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if "unknown_rail_violation" not in src or "must not substitute a hardcoded vendor" not in src:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0135-probe2 live resolver lost the fail-closed unknown-rail path")
        return 1

    _unknown_must_fail(resolve_routing)

    mutant_red = False
    try:
        _unknown_must_fail(mutant_resolve_routing)
    except AssertionError:
        mutant_red = True

    mutant_result = mutant_resolve_routing(UNKNOWN)
    mutant_silent = isinstance(mutant_result, ResolvedRail)
    live_after = resolve_routing(UNKNOWN)
    live_still_fails = (
        isinstance(live_after, RoutingViolation) and live_after.id == UNKNOWN_RAIL
    )

    print(
        f"live_unknown_fails={live_still_fails} "
        f"mutant_test_red={mutant_red} "
        f"mutant_silent_default={mutant_silent} "
        f"mutant_vendor={getattr(mutant_result, 'vendor', None)}"
    )
    if not (live_still_fails and mutant_red and mutant_silent):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0135-probe2 mutation did not turn the unknown-rail test red")
        return 1
    print(
        "SUCCESS:IMP-0135-probe2 hardcoded vendor default turns unknown-rail test red"
    )
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/IMP-0144.json — the replay-log format receipt."""
from __future__ import annotations

QUESTION = (
    "Question: what minimal event record makes a run replayable such that "
    "replaying it reproduces the SAME plan and the SAME verdicts without "
    "re-dispatching? Target: specify and implement that record. Acceptance: "
    "replay of a recorded run produces a byte-identical plan and identical "
    "oracle verdicts, with zero spawns."
)


def sha_file(rel: str) -> dict:
    path = REPO / rel
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n") if path.is_file() else b""
    return {
        "path": rel,
        "bytes": len(raw),
        "sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
        "exists": path.is_file(),
    }

        "verdict_sha256": rec["verdict_sha256"],
        "produced_by": {
            "vendor": "cursor",
            "model": "grok-4.6",
            "role": "implementer",
            "execution_substrate": "worktree-isolated",
        },
        "replay_log_format": {
            "encoding": "UTF-8 JSONL, LF-only, canonical dumps (sort_keys, separators comma-colon)",
            "envelope_required": [
                "v",
                "seq",
                "kind",
                "ts_logical",
                "run_id",
                "payload",
            ],
            "kinds_closed": [
                "run.start",
                "plan.input",
                "unit.planned",
                "dispatch.recorded",
                "oracle.verdict",
                "run.end",
            ],
            "clock": (
