--- probe1_record_replay.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: record-then-replay yields a byte-identical plan and identical verdicts.

Evidence signals (catalog): (1) byte-equality of the replayed plan;
(2) zero spawns during replay, counted by the fake rail.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import scenario as S  # noqa: E402

ART = REPO / S.RECEIPT_REL


def main() -> int:
    run = S.record_and_replay()
    printable = {
        k: run[k]
        for k in (
            "dispatch_events",
            "event_count",
            "fail_verdicts",
            "plan_bytes",
            "plan_equal",
            "plan_sha256",
            "record_spawns",
            "replay_calls",
            "replay_spawns",
            "run_id",
            "slot_count",
            "slot_ids",
            "verdict_equal",
            "verdict_sha256",
            "verdicts",
        )
    }
    print(json.dumps(printable, sort_keys=True))
    if not run["pass"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0144-probe1 record-replay did not pass")
        return 1
    if run["replay_spawns"] != 0:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0144-probe1 replay spawns={run['replay_spawns']}")
        return 1
    if run["record_spawns"] < 1:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0144-probe1 record spawns vacuously zero")
        return 1
    if not ART.is_file():
        print("FAILED:IMP-0144-probe1 receipt missing")
        return 1
    text = ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    doc = json.loads(text)
    if str(doc.get("state") or "").upper() in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0144-probe1 self-declared {doc.get('state')}")
        return 1
    if "SUCCESS:IMP-0144" not in text:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0144-probe1 sentinel absent")
        return 1
    live_plan = run["plan_sha256"]
    art_plan = ((doc.get("report") or {}).get("plan_sha256")) or doc.get("plan_sha256")
    if art_plan != live_plan:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0144-probe1 receipt plan_sha256 {art_plan} != live {live_plan}")
        return 1
    print(
        f"plan_equal={run['plan_equal']} verdict_equal={run['verdict_equal']} "
        f"record_spawns={run['record_spawns']} replay_spawns={run['replay_spawns']}"
    )
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0144-probe1 record-then-replay byte-identical plan and verdicts")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe2_field_mutation.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: drop one load-bearing event field; byte-equality must go red.

Control: drop debug_note (non-load-bearing); plan stays byte-identical.
Both-states: intact log still replays equal.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import scenario as S  # noqa: E402

ART = REPO / S.RECEIPT_REL


def main() -> int:
    run = S.field_mutation()
    printable = {k: run[k] for k in sorted(run) if k != "pass"}
    print(json.dumps(printable, sort_keys=True))
    if not run["pass"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0144-probe2 mutation control did not pass")
        return 1
    if run["item_plan_equal"] is not False:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0144-probe2 dropping a fan-out item did not change the plan")
        return 1
    if run["params_equal"] is not False:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0144-probe2 dropping params did not go red")
        return 1
    if run["note_equal"] is not True:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0144-probe2 dropping debug_note changed the plan (vacuous detector)")
        return 1
    if run["intact_equal"] is not True:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0144-probe2 intact log no longer replays")
        return 1
    if not ART.is_file():
        print("FAILED:IMP-0144-probe2 receipt missing")
        return 1
    text = ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    doc = json.loads(text)
    if str(doc.get("state") or "").upper() in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0144-probe2 self-declared {doc.get('state')}")
        return 1
    if "SUCCESS:IMP-0144" not in text:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0144-probe2 sentinel absent")
        return 1
    print(
        f"params_red={run['params_equal'] is False} "
        f"item_red={run['item_plan_equal'] is False} "
        f"note_control={run['note_equal']} intact={run['intact_equal']}"
    )
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0144-probe2 load-bearing field drop turns byte-equality red")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- scenario.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Shared IMP-0144 scenario: record a fixture run, replay it, mutate a field."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import importlib.util
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]


def _load_replay():
    path = REPO / "tools" / "agentspec" / "agentspec" / "replay_log.py"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("imp0144_replay_log", path)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise ImportError(f"cannot load {path}")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[spec.name] = mod
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


_R = _load_replay()
FrozenClock = _R.FrozenClock
CountingFakeRail = _R.CountingFakeRail
drop_payload_field = _R.drop_payload_field
fixture_execute = _R.fixture_execute
mutate_producer_items = _R.mutate_producer_items
record_run = _R.record_run
reconstruct_unverified = _R.reconstruct_unverified
replay_run = _R.replay_run
sha256_bytes = _R.sha256_bytes
try_replay = _R.try_replay

UNIT = "IMP-0144"
RECEIPT_REL = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0144.json"
FIXTURE_LOG_REL = "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0144/fixtures/recorded-run.jsonl"


def digest_file(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def record_and_replay() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Catalog test 1 + evidence: byte-equal plan/verdicts, zero replay spawns."""
    record_rail = CountingFakeRail(fixture_execute)
    recorded = record_run(rail=record_rail, clock=FrozenClock(origin=0))
    replay_rail = CountingFakeRail(fixture_execute)
    replayed = replay_run(recorded.log_bytes, rail=replay_rail)
    kinds = [e["kind"] for e in recorded.events]
    fail_verdicts = [v for v in recorded.verdicts if v["verdict"] == "FAIL"]
    return {
        "dispatch_events": kinds.count("dispatch.recorded"),
        "event_count": len(recorded.events),
        "fail_verdicts": len(fail_verdicts),
        "kinds": kinds,
        "plan_bytes": len(recorded.plan_b),
        "plan_equal": replayed.plan_b == recorded.plan_b,
        "plan_sha256": sha256_bytes(recorded.plan_b),
        "record_spawns": recorded.spawns,
        "replay_calls": list(replay_rail.calls),
        "replay_spawns": replay_rail.spawns,
        "run_id": recorded.run_id,
        "slot_count": len(recorded.plan["slots"]),
        "slot_ids": [s["unit_id"] for s in recorded.plan["slots"]],
        "verdict_equal": replayed.verdicts_b == recorded.verdicts_b,
        "verdict_sha256": sha256_bytes(recorded.verdicts_b),
        "verdicts": list(recorded.verdicts),
        "log_bytes": recorded.log_bytes,
        "recorded": recorded,
        "pass": (
            replayed.plan_b == recorded.plan_b
            and replayed.verdicts_b == recorded.verdicts_b
            and replay_rail.spawns == 0
            and recorded.spawns > 0
            and len(fail_verdicts) == 1
        ),
    }


def field_mutation() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Catalog test 2: drop a load-bearing field; byte-equality goes red.

    Control: drop debug_note (declared non-load-bearing); plan stays equal.
    """
    recorded = record_run(clock=FrozenClock(origin=0))
    original = recorded.plan_b
    # Load-bearing: drop params from run.start (required for reconstruction).
    dropped_params = drop_payload_field(recorded.log_bytes, "run.start", "params")
    params_err = None
    params_equal: bool | None = None
    try:
        replay_run(dropped_params, rail=CountingFakeRail(fixture_execute))
        params_equal = True  # replay produced a plan; compare below via reconstruct
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — mutation must go red, any closed failure counts
        params_err = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/IMP-0149.json from live probe measurements."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
OUT = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0149.json"
P1 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0149/probe1_parity_sets.py"
)
P2 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0149/probe2_js_omission.py"
)
SUPPORTING = [
    "tools/agentspec/agentspec/ids.py",
    "tools/agentspec/agentspec/spec.py",
    "tools/agentspec/agentspec/validate.py",
    "tools/agentspec-js/src/ids.js",
    "tools/agentspec-js/src/spec.js",
    "tools/agentspec-js/src/validate.js",
    "tools/agentspec-js/parity/run.py",
    "tools/agentspec-js/parity/compare.py",
    "tools/agentspec-js/parity/emit_py.py",
    "tools/agentspec-js/parity/emit_js.js",
    "tools/agentspec-js/fixtures/build_corpus.py",
]


def digest(rel: str) -> dict:
    path = REPO / rel
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n") if path.is_file() else b""
    return {
        "path": rel,
        "exists": path.is_file(),
        "bytes": len(raw),
        "sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() if path.is_file() else None,
        "role": "supporting",
    }


def run_probe(cmd: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
    p = subprocess.run(
        cmd, cwd=str(REPO), shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, env=env
    )
    return p.returncode, ((p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or "")).strip()


def main() -> int:
    c1, t1 = run_probe(P1)
    c2, t2 = run_probe(P2)
    if c1 != 0 or c2 != 0:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0149 emit probes c1={c1} c2={c2}")
        print(t1[-2000:])
        print(t2[-2000:])
        return 1
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    arts = [digest(p) for p in SUPPORTING]
    doc = {
        "unit_id": "IMP-0149",
        "id": "IMP-0149",
        "state": "SUCCESS",
        "status": "DELIVERED",
        "sentinel": "SUCCESS:IMP-0149",
        "schema_version": 1,
        "title": "Port every new spec type to the JS parity package and prove parity mechanically",
        "question": (
            "Mirror all Phase-7 spec additions into tools/agentspec-js/src and extend "
            "tools/agentspec-js/parity so Python and JS emit the SAME violation-id SET "
            "for the SAME fixtures. A missing id on either side is a failure, not a warning. "
            "A planted JS-only omission must turn parity red (both-states control)."
        ),
        "decision": {
            "summary": (
                "This worktree had no tools/agentspec or tools/agentspec-js. IMP-0149 stands "
                "up both implementations from the catalog contract (Phase-7 node types + closed "
                "ids), a 50-fixture shared corpus covering every closed id, and a set-equality "
                "parity harness. Live probes: identical sets (green) and planted JS omission (red, "
                "then restored green). Cloud ok=true is not delivery."
            ),
            "phase7_node_types": [
                "Subworkflow",
                "Loop",
                "HumanCheckpoint",
                "DynamicFanOut",
                "RepairLoop",
            ],
            "comparison": "sets",

--- probe1_parity_sets.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: Python and JS emit identical violation-id SETS over shared fixtures."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
RUN = REPO / "tools" / "agentspec-js" / "parity" / "run.py"
BEHAVIOUR = REPO / "tools" / "agentspec-js" / "parity" / "emit_behaviour_py.py"
EMIT_PY = REPO / "tools" / "agentspec-js" / "parity" / "emit_py.py"
CORPUS = REPO / "tools" / "agentspec-js" / "fixtures" / "corpus"


def run(cmd: list[str]) -> tuple[int, str]:
    p = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=str(REPO), capture_output=True, text=True)
    return p.returncode, ((p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or "")).strip()


def main() -> int:
    if not any(CORPUS.glob("*.json")):
        code, text = run(
            [
                sys.executable,
                "-B",
                str(REPO / "tools" / "agentspec-js" / "fixtures" / "build_corpus.py"),
            ]
        )
        if code != 0:
            print(text)
            print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe1 corpus build")
            return 1
    code, text = run([sys.executable, "-B", str(RUN)])
    print(text[-4000:])
    if code != 0:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe1 parity not green")
        return 1
    start, end = text.find("{"), text.rfind("}")
    if start < 0 or end < 0:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe1 no JSON report")
        return 1
    report = json.loads(text[start : end + 1])
    if report.get("compared_as") != "sets":
        print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe1 comparison is not set-based")
        return 1
    if report.get("fixture_count", 0) < 40:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe1 too few fixtures")
        return 1
    if report.get("failures"):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe1 set mismatch")
        return 1
    c2, t2 = run([sys.executable, "-B", str(EMIT_PY)])
    if c2 != 0:
        print(t2)
        print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe1 emit_py")
        return 1
    s2, e2 = t2.find("{"), t2.rfind("}")
    py_map = json.loads(t2[s2 : e2 + 1])
    if "valid.json" not in py_map:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe1 valid.json missing")
        return 1
    if py_map["valid.json"] != []:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe1 valid fixture not clean", py_map["valid.json"])
        return 1
    if "param-unknown-key.json" not in py_map:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe1 planted-key fixture missing")
        return 1
    if "param-unknown-key" not in py_map["param-unknown-key.json"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe1 param-unknown-key not emitted")
        return 1
    phase7_types = {
        "phase7-all-types.json",
    }
    if "phase7-all-types.json" not in py_map or py_map["phase7-all-types.json"] != []:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe1 phase-7 all-types not clean", py_map.get("phase7-all-types.json"))
        return 1
    c3, t3 = run([sys.executable, "-B", str(BEHAVIOUR)])
    print(t3[-1500:])
    if c3 != 0:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe1 behaviour AST")
        return 1
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "probe": "probe1-parity-sets",
                "unit_id": "IMP-0149",
                "compared_as": "sets",
                "fixture_count": report["fixture_count"],
                "valid_clean": True,
                "phase7_all_types_clean": True,
                "pass": True,
            },
            sort_keys=True,
        )
    )
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0149-probe1 python/js violation-id sets identical")

--- probe2_js_omission.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: planted JS-only omission must fail parity (both-states control)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
PARITY = REPO / "tools" / "agentspec-js" / "parity"
OMIT_ID = "fanout-empty"


def run(cmd: list[str], env: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> tuple[int, str]:
    merged = os.environ.copy()
    if env:
        merged.update(env)
    p = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=str(REPO), capture_output=True, text=True, env=merged)
    return p.returncode, ((p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or "")).strip()


def emit_pair(extra_env: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> tuple[dict, dict]:
    env = extra_env or {}
    c1, t1 = run([sys.executable, "-B", str(PARITY / "emit_ids_py.py")], env)
    c2, t2 = run(["node", str(PARITY / "emit_ids_js.mjs")], env)
    if c1 != 0:
        raise SystemExit(f"FAILED:IMP-0149-probe2 emit_py {t1}")
    if c2 != 0:
        raise SystemExit(f"FAILED:IMP-0149-probe2 emit_js {t2}")
    py = json.loads(t1)
    js = json.loads(t2)
    return py, js


def set_diff(py: dict, js: dict) -> list[dict]:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(PARITY))
    from compare_ids import compare  # noqa: E402

    return compare(py, js)


def main() -> int:
    py_good, js_good = emit_pair({})
    good_fail = set_diff(py_good, js_good)
    if good_fail:
        print(json.dumps(good_fail[:3], indent=2))
        print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe2 good state is not green")
        return 1

    py_bad, js_bad = emit_pair({"AGENTSPEC_JS_OMIT_IDS": OMIT_ID})
    bad_fail = set_diff(py_bad, js_bad)
    if not bad_fail:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe2 planted omission stayed green")
        return 1
    planted = [
        f
        for f in bad_fail
        if OMIT_ID in (f.get("only_python") or []) and OMIT_ID not in (f.get("only_js") or [])
    ]
    if not planted:
        print(json.dumps(bad_fail[:5], indent=2))
        print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe2 red is not the planted JS omission")
        return 1

    py_again, js_again = emit_pair({})
    again = set_diff(py_again, js_again)
    if again:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0149-probe2 omit leaked into restored state")
        return 1

    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "probe": "probe2-js-omission",
                "unit_id": "IMP-0149",
                "omit_id": OMIT_ID,
                "good_state_green": True,
                "planted_state_red": True,
                "planted_fixture_count": len(planted),
                "restored_state_green": True,
                "both_states": True,
                "pass": True,
            },
            sort_keys=True,
        )
    )
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0149-probe2 planted JS omission fails parity (both-states)")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""Shared IMP-0165 scenario: live extractor vs a nearest-neighbour mutant."""
from __future__ import annotations

import ast
import difflib
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools"))

from workflow_foundry.role_extract import (  # noqa: E402
    CANONICAL_ROLES,
    SYNONYM_TABLE,
    UNKNOWN,
    extract_candidate_set,
    normalize_role,
    unknown_rate,
)
from candidates import CANDIDATES, labelled_stats  # noqa: E402

SRC = REPO / "tools/workflow_foundry/role_extract.py"

# Near-miss spellings a Levenshtein/difflib snap would pull onto critic.
NEAR_MISS = ("critc", "refutr", "red teem")
KNOWN_SYNONYMS = (
    ("critic", "critic"),
    ("red team", "critic"),
    ("refuter", "critic"),
    ("RED-TEAM", "critic"),
    ("devil's advocate", "critic"),
    ("author", "proposer"),
)
INVENTED = (
    "floor-wax-auditor",
    "banana-steward",
    "quantum-shepherd",
    "IMP-0165-invented-zxqv",
)
SUBSTRING_TRAPS = (
    "critical-path-owner",
    "red teammate",
    "referee-adjacent",
)
FORBIDDEN_MODULES = frozenset(
    {
        "difflib",
        "Levenshtein",
        "fuzzywuzzy",
        "rapidfuzz",
        "jellyfish",
        "textdistance",
    }
)
FORBIDDEN_NAMES = frozenset(
    {
        "get_close_matches",
        "SequenceMatcher",
        "levenshtein",
        "hamming",
        "jaro",
        "jaro_winkler",
        "closest",
        "nearest",
        "fuzzy",
    }
)


def snap_normalize(raw: str | None) -> str:
    """Forbidden mutant: snap an unknown key onto its nearest table neighbour."""
    if raw is None:
        return UNKNOWN
    key = (
        str(raw)
        .strip()
        .lower()
        .replace("_", " ")
        .replace("-", " ")
    )
    key = "".join(ch if ch.isalnum() or ch == " " else " " for ch in key)
    key = " ".join(key.split())
    if not key:
        return UNKNOWN
    if key in CANONICAL_ROLES:
        return key
    if key in SYNONYM_TABLE:
        return SYNONYM_TABLE[key]
    pool = sorted(set(SYNONYM_TABLE) | set(CANONICAL_ROLES))
    hits = difflib.get_close_matches(key, pool, n=1, cutoff=0.6)
    if not hits:
        return UNKNOWN
    hit = hits[0]
    if hit in CANONICAL_ROLES:
        return hit
    return SYNONYM_TABLE.get(hit, UNKNOWN)

    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0229/probe2_stale.py",
]


def digest(rel: str) -> dict:
    path = REPO / rel
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n") if path.is_file() else b""
    return {
        "path": rel,
        "bytes": len(raw),
        "sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
        "exists": path.is_file(),
        "role": "supporting",
        "eol": "LF",
    }


def probe(role: str, method: str, command: str, raw: str, exit_code: int) -> dict:
    return {
        "role": role,
        "method": method,
        "command": command,
        "raw_output": raw[:16000],
        "exit_code": exit_code,
        "fingerprint": hashlib.sha256(f"{method}\x1f{command}".encode()).hexdigest()[:32],
    }


def main() -> int:
    bitflip = run_bitflip_both_states()
    stale = run_stale_fixed_package()
    keys = run_seven_key_mutations()
    clock_free = all(
        source_has_no_clock(rel)
        for rel in (
            "tools/agentspec/agentspec/canonical.py",
            "tools/agentspec/agentspec/cache_key.py",
        )
    )
    if not (bitflip["pass"] and stale["pass"] and keys["pass"] and clock_free):
        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "bitflip": bitflip["pass"],
                    "stale": stale["pass"],
                    "keys": keys["pass"],
                    "clock_free": clock_free,
                }
            )
        )
        print("FAILED:IMP-0229 live scenarios did not pass; receipt not written")
        return 1

    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    arts = [digest(rel) for rel in IMPLEMENTATION]
    arts[2]["role"] = "implementation"
    p1 = probe(
        "source",
        "command",
        "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0229/probe1_bitflip.py",
        json.dumps(bitflip, sort_keys=True),
        0,
    )
    p2 = probe(
        "verifier",

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: bit-flipped CAS entry is detected and refetched; clean entry is a hit."""

            hits=result["counters_after_clean_hit"]["hits"],
            cr=result["counters_after_corrupt"]["corrupt_refetches"],
            sf=result["served_flipped"],
        )
    )

--- scenario.py ---
"""Shared IMP-0229 scenarios: bit-flip both-states, stale-package, key mutations.

Imports the live tools/agentspec backend. Probes do not reimplement the store.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any


def _load(name: str, rel: str):
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4] / rel
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise ImportError(f"cannot load {path}")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[name] = mod
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]
_cache = _load(
    "imp0229_artifact_cache",
    "tools/agentspec/agentspec/artifact_cache.py",
)
_key = _load(
    "imp0229_cache_key",
    "tools/agentspec/agentspec/cache_key.py",
)
FAILURE_CLASSES = _cache.FAILURE_CLASSES
ArtifactCache = _cache.ArtifactCache
PackageCache = _cache.PackageCache
sha256_hex = _cache.sha256_hex
CACHE_KEY_FIELDS = _key.CACHE_KEY_FIELDS
InvalidCacheKey = _key.InvalidCacheKey
derive_cache_key = _key.derive_cache_key

UNIT = "IMP-0229"
PACKAGE_ID = "pkg.fixture.cache-backend"


def base_inputs(**overrides: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    data: dict[str, Any] = {
        "workflow_id": "wf.cas-cache",
        "spec_version": "1.0.0",
        "unit_id": UNIT,
        "resolved_prompt": "content-addressed artifact cache",
        "param_values": {"mode": "cas", "integrity": "required"},
        "tool_policy": {"network": "deny", "write": "cache-root-only"},
        "input_artifact_hashes": ["ab" * 32],
    }
    data.update(overrides)
    return data


def flip_one_bit(path: Path) -> bytes:
    raw = bytearray(path.read_bytes())
    if not raw:
        raise AssertionError("cannot bit-flip an empty payload")
    raw[0] ^= 0x01
    path.write_bytes(bytes(raw))
    return bytes(raw)


def run_bitflip_both_states() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Clean entry is a measured hit; bit-flipped entry is refetched, never served."""
    origin = b"IMP-0229-clean-artifact-v1\npayload-bytes-for-integrity\n"
    fetches = {"n": 0}

    def fetcher() -> bytes:
        fetches["n"] += 1
        return origin

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0229-bitflip-") as tmp:
        cache = ArtifactCache(Path(tmp) / "cas")
        key = derive_cache_key(base_inputs(input_artifact_hashes=[sha256_hex(origin)]))

        first = cache.get(key, fetcher, expected_artifact_hash=sha256_hex(origin))
        miss_after_first = dict(cache.counters.as_dict())
        second = cache.get(key, fetcher, expected_artifact_hash=sha256_hex(origin))
        hit_after_second = dict(cache.counters.as_dict())

        flipped = flip_one_bit(cache.payload_path(key))
        after_corrupt = cache.get(
            key, fetcher, expected_artifact_hash=sha256_hex(origin)
        )
        final = dict(cache.counters.as_dict())

        served_flipped = after_corrupt == flipped
        clean_ok = first == origin and second == origin
        refetch_ok = after_corrupt == origin and not served_flipped
        both_states = (
            miss_after_first["misses"] == 1
            and hit_after_second["hits"] == 1
            and hit_after_second["corrupt_refetches"] == 0

--- emit_receipt.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/IMP-0265.json from a live registry run."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

import scenario as S  # noqa: E402

OUT = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0265.json"
QUESTION = (
    "Implement tools/council/participants/registry.py holding participant "
    "identity, vendor, model, declared expertise, calibration history pointer "
    "and tool grants. Falsifiable question: are two participants with identical "
    "vendor+model+prompt+tools distinguishable as independent voices, or should "
    "the registry mark them as one effective voice?"
)


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def main() -> int:
    R = S.load_registry()
    if not S.REGISTRY_PATH.is_file():
        print("FAILED:IMP-0265 registry.py missing")
        return 1

    twelve = S.twelve_seat_registry()
    report = twelve.collision_report()
    audit = twelve.independence_audit()
    roster = [p.as_dict() for p in twelve.roster()]

    base = R.Participant(**S.twelve_seat_specs()[0])
    fps = [
        R.voice_fingerprint(base.vendor, base.model, base.prompt, base.tool_grants)
        for _ in range(50)
    ]
    stable = len(set(fps)) == 1 and fps[0] == base.fingerprint

    perm = R.Participant(
        participant_id="perm-id",
        display_name="Other Name",
        vendor="  " + base.vendor + " ",
        model=base.model,
        prompt="\n" + base.prompt + "\n",
        declared_expertise=("unrelated-claim",),
        calibration_history_pointer="calibration://other",
        tool_grants=tuple(list(reversed(list(base.tool_grants))) + list(base.tool_grants)),
    )
    identity_ignored = perm.fingerprint == base.fingerprint

    mutated = R.Participant(
        participant_id="mut-prompt",
        display_name=base.display_name,
        vendor=base.vendor,
        model=base.model,
        prompt=base.prompt + " extra clause",
        declared_expertise=base.declared_expertise,
        calibration_history_pointer=base.calibration_history_pointer,
        tool_grants=base.tool_grants,
    )
    prompt_moves = mutated.fingerprint != base.fingerprint

    bad = S.duplicate_pair_registry()
    bad_report = bad.collision_report()
    bad_audit = bad.independence_audit()
    good = S.diverse_pair_registry()
    good_report = good.collision_report()
    g0, g1 = good.roster()
    good_audit = R.independence_audit(
        good.roster(), flagged_pairs=[(g0.participant_id, g1.participant_id)]
    )
    prompt_split = S.same_model_different_prompt_registry().collision_report()

    checks = {
        "fingerprint_stable_50": stable,
        "identity_fields_excluded_from_voice": identity_ignored,
        "prompt_change_moves_fingerprint": prompt_moves,
        "twelve_seat_count": report["seat_count"] == 12,
        "twelve_effective_voices_11": report["effective_voice_count"] == 11,
        "twelve_collision_fired": report["fired"] is True,
        "clone_pair_is_architect": set(report["collisions"][0]["seat_ids"])
        == {S.CLONE_OF, S.CLONE_ID}
        if report["collisions"]
        else False,
        "independence_rejects_flagged_pair": audit["all_flagged_pairs_non_independent"],
        "duplicate_pair_fires": bad_report["fired"] is True
        and bad_report["effective_voice_count"] == 1,
        "diverse_pair_silent": good_report["fired"] is False
        and good_report["effective_voice_count"] == 2,
        "same_model_different_prompt_distinct": prompt_split["fired"] is False,

--- probe1_fingerprint_stability.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: fingerprint computation stability + 12-seat collision report.

Recomputes voice fingerprints many times, permutes non-voice fields and tool
order, and requires the 12-seat roster collision report to fire on the planted
duplicate while keeping same-vendor/model different-prompt seats distinct.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

import scenario as S  # noqa: E402

ART = S.RECEIPT_PATH


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:IMP-0265-probe1 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    R = S.load_registry()
    if not S.REGISTRY_PATH.is_file():
        return fail("registry.py missing")

    base = dict(S.twelve_seat_specs()[0])
    person = R.Participant(**base)
    fps = [R.voice_fingerprint(person.vendor, person.model, person.prompt, person.tool_grants) for _ in range(50)]
    if len(set(fps)) != 1 or fps[0] != person.fingerprint:
        return fail(f"fingerprint unstable set_size={len(set(fps))}")

    shuffled_tools = list(reversed(list(person.tool_grants))) + list(person.tool_grants)
    again = R.Participant(
        participant_id="perm-id",
        display_name="Other Name",
        vendor="  " + person.vendor + " ",
        model=person.model,
        prompt="\n" + person.prompt + "\n",
        declared_expertise=("unrelated-claim",),
        calibration_history_pointer="calibration://other",
        tool_grants=tuple(shuffled_tools),
    )
    if again.fingerprint != person.fingerprint:
        return fail("identity/tool-order leaked into fingerprint")

    mutated = R.Participant(
        participant_id="mut-prompt",
        display_name=person.display_name,
        vendor=person.vendor,
        model=person.model,
        prompt=person.prompt + " extra clause",
        declared_expertise=person.declared_expertise,
        calibration_history_pointer=person.calibration_history_pointer,
        tool_grants=person.tool_grants,
    )
    if mutated.fingerprint == person.fingerprint:
        return fail("prompt change did not move fingerprint")

    reg = S.twelve_seat_registry()
    roster = reg.roster()
    if len(roster) != 12:
        return fail(f"roster size {len(roster)} want=12")
    report = reg.collision_report()
    if not report["fired"]:
        return fail("12-seat collision report did not fire")
    if report["seat_count"] != 12:
        return fail(f"seat_count={report['seat_count']}")
    if report["effective_voice_count"] != 11:
        return fail(f"effective_voice_count={report['effective_voice_count']} want=11")
    if report["collision_count"] != 1:
        return fail(f"collision_count={report['collision_count']} want=1")
    coll = report["collisions"][0]
    seats = set(coll["seat_ids"])
    if seats != {S.CLONE_OF, S.CLONE_ID}:
        return fail(f"unexpected collision seats {sorted(seats)}")

    same_model = S.same_model_different_prompt_registry().collision_report()
    if same_model["fired"] or same_model["effective_voice_count"] != 2:
        return fail("same vendor+model different prompt must stay two voices")

    audit = reg.independence_audit()
    if audit["pair_count"] != 1 or not audit["all_flagged_pairs_non_independent"]:
        return fail(f"independence audit {audit}")
    pair = audit["pairs"][0]
    if not pair["first_round_identical"] or pair["independent"]:
        return fail(f"flagged pair still treated independent {pair}")

    if not ART.is_file() or ART.stat().st_size <= 0:
        return fail("receipt missing")
    doc = json.loads(ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    blob = json.dumps(doc)
    if "SUCCESS:IMP-0265" not in blob:
        return fail("sentinel absent")
    if doc.get("unit_id") != "IMP-0265":

--- probe2_collision_negative.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: negative control — seat the same configuration twice; collision FIRES.

Starts from the tests, not the receipt narrative. Both-states:
  bad  = two seats, identical vendor+model+prompt+tools (tool order permuted)
  good = two seats, different prompts
Also requires the independence audit to reject the flagged pair's first rounds,
and a diverse pair to remain independent.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

import scenario as S  # noqa: E402


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:IMP-0265-probe2 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    R = S.load_registry()

    bad = S.duplicate_pair_registry()
    bad_report = bad.collision_report()
    if not bad_report["fired"]:
        return fail("same configuration seated twice did not fire")
    if bad_report["effective_voice_count"] != 1:
        return fail(f"bad effective_voice_count={bad_report['effective_voice_count']} want=1")
    if bad_report["seat_count"] != 2:
        return fail(f"bad seat_count={bad_report['seat_count']}")

    a, b = bad.roster()
    if a.fingerprint != b.fingerprint:
        return fail("duplicate pair fingerprints diverged")
    if a.participant_id == b.participant_id:
        return fail("duplicate pair reused participant_id")
    if a.display_name == b.display_name:
        return fail("clone display_name collapsed; identity must remain distinct")
    if a.declared_expertise == b.declared_expertise:
        return fail("clone expertise collapsed; claimed expertise is not a voice")

    bad_audit = bad.independence_audit()
    if not bad_audit["all_flagged_pairs_non_independent"]:
        return fail(f"independence audit failed to reject clone pair {bad_audit}")
    if not bad_audit["pairs"][0]["first_round_identical"]:
        return fail("clone first-round briefs differed")

    good = S.diverse_pair_registry()
    good_report = good.collision_report()
    if good_report["fired"]:
        return fail("diverse pair constant-fired the collision detector")
    if good_report["effective_voice_count"] != 2:
        return fail(f"good effective_voice_count={good_report['effective_voice_count']}")
    g0, g1 = good.roster()
    good_audit = R.independence_audit(good.roster(), flagged_pairs=[(g0.participant_id, g1.participant_id)])
    if good_audit["pairs"][0]["independence_rejected"]:
        return fail("diverse pair first-round briefs were identical")

    prompt_split = S.same_model_different_prompt_registry().collision_report()
    if prompt_split["fired"]:
        return fail("same vendor+model different prompt fired as one voice")

    art = S.RECEIPT_PATH
    if not art.is_file():
        return fail("receipt missing")
    doc = json.loads(art.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if "SUCCESS:IMP-0265" not in json.dumps(doc):
        return fail("sentinel absent")
    ncs = doc.get("negative_control_ids") or []
    if "IMP0265-NC-duplicate-config-fires" not in ncs:
        return fail("receipt omitted the duplicate-config negative control id")

    out = {
        "probe": "probe2-collision-negative",
        "unit_id": "IMP-0265",
        "pass": True,
        "fired_on_bad_state": True,
        "silent_on_good_state": True,
        "bad_effective_voice_count": 1,
        "good_effective_voice_count": 2,
        "independence_rejected_on_clone": True,
        "independence_kept_on_diverse": True,
        "same_model_different_prompt_distinct": True,
    }
    print(json.dumps(out, sort_keys=True))
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0265-probe2 same-config collision fires; diverse pair silent")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- scenario.py ---
"""IMP-0265 probe fixtures: 12-seat roster, duplicate pair, diverse pair."""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
REGISTRY_PATH = REPO / "tools" / "council" / "participants" / "registry.py"
RECEIPT_PATH = REPO / "command-center" / "receipts" / "IMP-0265.json"


def load_registry():
    name = "imp0265_council_registry"
    if name in sys.modules:
        return sys.modules[name]
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, REGISTRY_PATH)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise ImportError(f"cannot load {REGISTRY_PATH}")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[name] = mod
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


def _seat(
    participant_id: str,
    display_name: str,
    vendor: str,
    model: str,
    prompt: str,
    expertise: list[str],
    pointer: str | None,
    tools: list[str],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "participant_id": participant_id,
        "display_name": display_name,
        "vendor": vendor,
        "model": model,
        "prompt": prompt,
        "declared_expertise": expertise,
        "calibration_history_pointer": pointer,
        "tool_grants": tools,
    }

--- emit_receipt.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/IMP-0269.json after the named probes pass."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from scenario import load_selector, read_json  # noqa: E402

UNIT = "IMP-0269"
OUT = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0269.json"
MODULE = "tools/council/participants/select_expertise.py"
P1 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0269/probe1_tag_precision.py"
)
P2 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0269/probe2_bogus_claim.py"
)


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def run_probe(cmd: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    p = subprocess.run(
        cmd,
        cwd=str(REPO),
        shell=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    text = ((p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or "")).strip()
    return p.returncode, text


def art(rel: str, role: str) -> dict:
    path = REPO / rel
    data = path.read_bytes() if path.is_file() else b""
    return {
        "path": rel,
        "bytes": len(data),
        "sha256": hashlib.sha256(data.replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")).hexdigest() if data else "",
        "exists": path.is_file(),
        "role": role,
    }


def main() -> int:
    c1, t1 = run_probe(P1)
    c2, t2 = run_probe(P2)
    if c1 != 0 or c2 != 0:
        print(f"FAILED:{UNIT} probes c1={c1} c2={c2}")
        print(t1[-2000:])
        print(t2[-2000:])
        return 1

    sel = load_selector()
    labelled = read_json("labelled-tags.json")["rows"]
    metrics = sel.tag_match_metrics(labelled)
    bank_doc = read_json("competence-bank.json")
    cand_doc = read_json("candidates.json")
    selection = sel.select_expertise(
        cand_doc["task_tags"],
        cand_doc["candidates"],
        bank_doc["items"],
        baseline=float(bank_doc.get("baseline") or 0.5),
    )
    unchecked = sel.select_claimed_tags_unchecked(
        cand_doc["task_tags"], cand_doc["candidates"]
    )
    selection_d = selection.as_dict()

    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    module_art = art(MODULE, "implementation")

    doc = {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "unit_id": UNIT,
        "state": "SUCCESS",
        "sentinel": "SUCCESS:IMP-0269",
        "spec_anchor": "_USER-PROMPTS.md:11172-11244 (hallucinated expertise :11241)",
        "profile": "council-participants-module",
        "min_bytes_threshold": 128,
        "artifact_paths": [
            MODULE,
            OUT,
            "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0269/probe1_tag_precision.py",
            "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0269/probe2_bogus_claim.py",

--- probe1_tag_precision.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Named probe 1: tag-matching precision/recall on the labelled fixture.

Requires precision and recall both >= 0.8, both-states cells present (TP and TN),
and a near-miss (taxidermy) that must not match tax-law.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from scenario import load_selector, read_json  # noqa: E402

UNIT = "IMP-0269"
MIN_P = 0.8
MIN_R = 0.8


def main() -> int:
    sel = load_selector()
    fixture = read_json("labelled-tags.json")
    rows = fixture["rows"]
    metrics = sel.tag_match_metrics(rows)
    cells = {r["row_id"]: r for r in metrics["rows"]}

    near = cells.get("tax-near-miss-taxidermy")
    alias = cells.get("tax-alias-taxation")
    empty_d = cells.get("empty-declared")

    ok = (
        metrics["n"] == len(rows)
        and metrics["precision"] >= MIN_P
        and metrics["recall"] >= MIN_R
        and metrics["tp"] > 0
        and metrics["tn"] > 0
        and metrics["fp"] == 0
        and metrics["fn"] == 0
        and near is not None
        and near["predicted"] is False
        and alias is not None
        and alias["predicted"] is True
        and empty_d is not None
        and empty_d["predicted"] is False
    )
    payload = {
        "probe": "probe1-tag-precision-recall",
        "unit_id": UNIT,
        "n": metrics["n"],
        "tp": metrics["tp"],
        "fp": metrics["fp"],
        "tn": metrics["tn"],
        "fn": metrics["fn"],
        "precision": metrics["precision"],
        "recall": metrics["recall"],
        "f1": metrics["f1"],
        "near_miss_rejected": bool(near and not near["predicted"]),
        "alias_matched": bool(alias and alias["predicted"]),
        "pass": ok,
    }
    print(json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True))
    print(
        f"precision={metrics['precision']:.3f} recall={metrics['recall']:.3f} "
        f"tp={metrics['tp']} tn={metrics['tn']} fp={metrics['fp']} fn={metrics['fn']}"
    )
    if not ok:
        print(f"FAILED:{UNIT}-probe1 tag matcher below contract")
        return 1
    print(f"SUCCESS:{UNIT}-probe1 tag matching precision/recall")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe2_bogus_claim.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Named probe 2: bogus expertise claims must be rejected.

Both-states:
  good — alice-crypto and blake-tax are seated with an attached probe result.
  bad  — mallory (assertion-only), ned (below baseline), olga (unanswerable
         overclaim) are unseated claimants.

Negative control: select_claimed_tags_unchecked WOULD seat mallory, proving
the competence probe — not tag overlap — is what rejects hallucinated expertise.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from scenario import load_selector, read_json  # noqa: E402

UNIT = "IMP-0269"
MUST_SEAT = ("alice-crypto", "blake-tax")
MUST_REJECT = (
    "mallory-asserted-crypto",
    "ned-wrong-crypto",
    "olga-overclaim-ua",
)


def main() -> int:
    sel = load_selector()
    bank_doc = read_json("competence-bank.json")
    cand_doc = read_json("candidates.json")
    task = cand_doc["task_tags"]
    candidates = cand_doc["candidates"]
    bank = bank_doc["items"]
    baseline = float(bank_doc.get("baseline") or 0.5)

    unchecked = sel.select_claimed_tags_unchecked(task, candidates)
    selection = sel.select_expertise(task, candidates, bank, baseline=baseline)
    seated_ids = [s.participant_id for s in selection.seated]
    unseated_ids = [u.participant_id for u in selection.unseated_claimants]
    unseated_by_id = {u.participant_id: u for u in selection.unseated_claimants}
    seated_by_id = {s.participant_id: s for s in selection.seated}

    mallory_in_unchecked = "mallory-asserted-crypto" in unchecked
    mallory_not_seated = "mallory-asserted-crypto" not in seated_ids
    probe_is_load_bearing = mallory_in_unchecked and mallory_not_seated

    seated_ok = all(pid in seated_ids for pid in MUST_SEAT)
    rejected_ok = all(pid in unseated_ids for pid in MUST_REJECT)
    frontend_skipped = "pat-frontend" in selection.skipped_no_overlap

    attached = True
    for pid in MUST_SEAT:
        seat = seated_by_id.get(pid)
        if seat is None or not seat.probe_results or not all(p.passed for p in seat.probe_results):
            attached = False

    mallory = unseated_by_id.get("mallory-asserted-crypto")
    mallory_reason_ok = bool(
        mallory and mallory.reason == "hallucinated-expertise" and mallory.probe_results
    )

    assertion_only = any(
        p.assertion_only for p in (mallory.probe_results if mallory else ())
    )

    ok = (
        probe_is_load_bearing
        and seated_ok
        and rejected_ok
        and attached
        and mallory_reason_ok
        and assertion_only
        and frontend_skipped
        and "pat-frontend" not in seated_ids
        and selection.seated[0].role == "domain_authority"
    )
    payload = {
        "probe": "probe2-bogus-claim-rejected",
        "unit_id": UNIT,
        "unchecked_would_seat_mallory": mallory_in_unchecked,
        "mallory_seated": not mallory_not_seated,
        "probe_is_load_bearing": probe_is_load_bearing,
        "seated_ids": seated_ids,
        "unseated_ids": unseated_ids,
        "unseated_reasons": {u.participant_id: u.reason for u in selection.unseated_claimants},
        "each_seat_has_probe_result": attached,
        "pass": ok,
    }
    print(json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True))
    print(
        f"seated={seated_ids} unseated={unseated_ids} "
        f"load_bearing={probe_is_load_bearing}"
    )
    if not ok:
        print(f"FAILED:{UNIT}-probe2 bogus claim was not rejected")

        raise ImportError(f"cannot load {MODULE}")

IMP-0290
--- probe1_barrier.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Named probe 1: barrier under a slow seat and a timeout.

Also covers retry and streaming paths: an early submitter's text must not
reach a later participant before the legitimate barrier opens. Event-order
audit is the second signal (timestamps), distinct from the prompt scan.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from scenario import (  # noqa: E402
    ALICE,
    ALICE_TEXT,
    BOB,
    BOB_TEXT,
    CANARY_ALICE,
    CANARY_BOB,
    CANARY_CAROL,
    CAROL,
    CAROL_TEXT,
    SHARED_BRIEF,
    check_receipt_if_present,
    load_blind_first,
    make_round,
)


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:IMP-0290-probe1 {msg}")
    return 1


def _no_peer_canaries(prompt: str, own: str) -> list[str]:
    hits = []
    for tok in (CANARY_ALICE, CANARY_BOB, CANARY_CAROL):
        if tok in own:
            continue
        if tok in prompt:
            hits.append(tok)
    return hits


def main() -> int:
    mod = load_blind_first()
    rnd, clock = make_round(mod)

    # Shared brief is visible to everyone from t0.
    p_alice = rnd.assemble_prompt(ALICE)
    if SHARED_BRIEF not in p_alice:
        return fail("shared brief missing from alice prompt")
    clock.advance()

    # Alice submits first. Bob and Carol assemble: must not see Alice.
    rnd.submit(ALICE, ALICE_TEXT)
    clock.advance()
    p_bob = rnd.assemble_prompt(BOB)
    p_carol = rnd.assemble_prompt(CAROL)
    if _no_peer_canaries(p_bob, BOB_TEXT):
        return fail(f"bob saw alice before barrier: {_no_peer_canaries(p_bob, BOB_TEXT)}")
    if _no_peer_canaries(p_carol, CAROL_TEXT):
        return fail("carol saw a peer before barrier")
    if CANARY_ALICE in p_bob or CANARY_ALICE in p_carol:
        return fail("alice canary leaked pre-barrier")

    # Retry: Alice replaces her sealed text. Still must not leak.
    clock.advance()
    rnd.submit(ALICE, ALICE_TEXT + " RETRY-MARKER-alice")
    clock.advance()
    p_bob2 = rnd.assemble_prompt(BOB)
    if CANARY_ALICE in p_bob2 or "RETRY-MARKER-alice" in p_bob2:
        return fail("retry leaked alice text to bob")

    # Streaming: Bob streams his canary in chunks; Carol must not see it.
    clock.advance()
    rnd.submit_chunk(BOB, BOB_TEXT[:20])
    clock.advance()
    rnd.submit_chunk(BOB, BOB_TEXT[20:])
    clock.advance()
    p_carol2 = rnd.assemble_prompt(CAROL)
    if CANARY_BOB in p_carol2 or BOB_TEXT in p_carol2:
        return fail("stream buffer leaked to carol")
    rnd.finalize_stream(BOB)
    clock.advance()
    p_carol3 = rnd.assemble_prompt(CAROL)
    if CANARY_BOB in p_carol3:
        return fail("bob sealed text leaked to carol while carol still slow")

    # Slow participant (Carol) + timeout: tick to deadline without Carol.
    # Before deadline, Alice must not see Bob either (barrier still closed).
    p_alice_pre = rnd.assemble_prompt(ALICE)
    if CANARY_BOB in p_alice_pre:
        return fail("alice saw bob before timeout barrier")

    leaks_pre = rnd.scan_delivered_prompts()

--- probe2_early_open.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Named probe 2 (adversarial, starts from the tests): early-open negative control.

The catalog control: open the barrier early and assert the delivered-prompt
scan FIRES. Both-states: the same sequence without force-open must stay silent.
A scanner that trusted the protocol's own opened-at timestamp would be silent
on the bad state; this probe fails that mutant.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from scenario import (  # noqa: E402
    ALICE,
    ALICE_TEXT,
    BOB,
    CANARY_ALICE,
    check_receipt_if_present,
    load_blind_first,
    make_round,
)


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:IMP-0290-probe2 {msg}")
    return 1


def _mutant_scan_uses_actual_open(rnd) -> list:
    """What a dishonest scanner would do: trust actual opened_at.

    Prompts after force-open would be treated as post-barrier and skipped.
    """
    cutoff = rnd._actual_opened_at
    hits = []
    for rec in rnd.prompt_log():
        if cutoff is not None and rec.ts >= cutoff:
            continue
        if CANARY_ALICE in rec.text and rec.seat != ALICE:
            hits.append(rec.seat)
    return hits


def main() -> int:
    mod = load_blind_first()

    # --- bad state: force-open before Carol/Bob submit ---
    bad, clock = make_round(mod)
    bad.submit(ALICE, ALICE_TEXT)
    clock.advance()
    p_bob_pre = bad.assemble_prompt(BOB)
    if CANARY_ALICE in p_bob_pre:
        return fail("production assemble leaked before any open (not the control)")
    opened = bad.open_barrier(force=True)
    if not opened:
        return fail("force-open did not open")
    if bad.legitimate_barrier_at() is not None:
        return fail("force-open must not write the legitimate barrier timestamp")
    clock.advance()
    p_bob_bad = bad.assemble_prompt(BOB)
    if CANARY_ALICE not in p_bob_bad:
        return fail("force-open did not deliver alice text; control cannot fire")
    leaks_bad = bad.scan_delivered_prompts()
    if not leaks_bad:
        return fail("prompt scan SILENT on early-open (detector failure)")
    if not any(h.peer_seat == ALICE and h.prompt_seat == BOB for h in leaks_bad):
        return fail(f"scan fired but not on bob<-alice: {leaks_bad[:3]}")
    audit_bad = bad.audit_event_order()
    if audit_bad["ok"]:
        return fail("event-order audit silent on early DELIVER (second signal dead)")
    mutant_hits = _mutant_scan_uses_actual_open(bad)
    if mutant_hits:
        return fail("mutant scanner unexpectedly fired; control is mis-built")

    # --- good state: same submits, no force-open ---
    good, clock_g = make_round(mod)
    good.submit(ALICE, ALICE_TEXT)
    clock_g.advance()
    p_bob_good = good.assemble_prompt(BOB)
    if CANARY_ALICE in p_bob_good:
        return fail("good-state bob saw alice")
    leaks_good = good.scan_delivered_prompts()
    if leaks_good:
        return fail(f"scan fired on good state: {leaks_good[:3]}")
    audit_good = good.audit_event_order()
    if not audit_good["ok"]:
        return fail(f"event-order failed on good state: {audit_good['violations'][:3]}")
    good.open_barrier(force=False)
    if good.barrier_actually_open():
        return fail("non-force open succeeded with missing seats")

    rec = check_receipt_if_present()
    if not rec["ok"]:
        return fail(f"receipt {rec['reason']}")


--- scenario.py ---
"""Shared planted tokens and round factory for IMP-0290 probes."""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
MODULE = REPO / "tools/council/protocol/blind_first.py"

ALICE = "alice"
BOB = "bob"
CAROL = "carol"
SEATS = (ALICE, BOB, CAROL)

SHARED_BRIEF = (
    "SHARED-BRIEF-imp-0290-public-question: should the council adopt a "
    "sealed-envelope first pass?"
)
CANARY_ALICE = "CANARY-ALICE-7f3a9c2e-blind-first-sealed"
CANARY_BOB = "CANARY-BOB-d41e8b11-blind-first-sealed"
CANARY_CAROL = "CANARY-CAROL-a0c4ff90-blind-first-sealed"

ALICE_TEXT = f"Alice independent position: envelopes before talk. {CANARY_ALICE}"
BOB_TEXT = f"Bob independent position: no peeking across seats. {CANARY_BOB}"
CAROL_TEXT = f"Carol independent position: timeout must not leak. {CANARY_CAROL}"

RECEIPT = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0290.json"
SENTINEL = "SUCCESS:IMP-0290"
NOT_DELIVERING = frozenset({"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"})


def load_blind_first():
    """Load the live module from disk; a stale import is not evidence."""
    key = "imp0290_blind_first"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(key, MODULE)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise ImportError(f"cannot load {MODULE}")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[key] = mod
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


def make_round(mod, *, deadline: float = 1100.0, start: float = 1000.0):
    clock = mod.LogicalClock(start)
    rnd = mod.BlindFirstRound(
        SEATS,
        shared_brief=SHARED_BRIEF,
        deadline=deadline,
        clock=clock,
    )
    return rnd, clock


def check_receipt_if_present() -> dict:
    path = REPO / RECEIPT
    if not path.is_file():
        return {"present": False, "ok": True, "reason": "receipt-not-yet-written"}
    import json

    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    doc = json.loads(text)
    if SENTINEL not in text:
        return {"present": True, "ok": False, "reason": "sentinel-absent"}
    st = str(doc.get("state") or "").upper()
    if st in NOT_DELIVERING:
        return {"present": True, "ok": False, "reason": f"self-declared:{st}"}
    if doc.get("unit_id") != "IMP-0290":
        return {"present": True, "ok": False, "reason": "unit_id"}
    return {"present": True, "ok": True, "reason": "receipt-ok", "state": st}

IMP-0302
--- emit_receipt.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/IMP-0302.json from a live tally of the 24-case matrix."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from load import TALLY_PATH, load_tally  # noqa: E402

OUT = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0302.json"
EXPECTED_REL = "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0302/expected_matrix.json"


def digest(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def art_row(rel: str, role: str) -> dict:
    path = REPO / rel
    sha, size = digest(path)
    return {
        "path": rel,
        "sha256": sha,
        "bytes": size,
        "present": True,
        "exists": path.is_file(),
        "role": role,
        "unit_id": "IMP-0302",
    }


def main() -> int:
    expected = json.loads((REPO / EXPECTED_REL).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    cases = expected["cases"]
    if len(cases) != 24:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0302 expected 24 cases, got {len(cases)}")
        return 1
    tally = load_tally()
    rows = []
    mismatches = []
    for case in cases:
        decision = tally.tally_motion(
            expected["ballots"],
            threshold_id=case["threshold_id"],
            abstention_policy_id=case["abstention_policy_id"],
            quorum_required=case["quorum_required"],
            seated=expected["seated"],
            motion_id=case["case_id"],
        )
        row = {
            "case_id": case["case_id"],
            "threshold_id": case["threshold_id"],
            "abstention_policy_id": decision["abstention_policy_id"],
            "quorum_required": case["quorum_required"],
            "quorum_met": decision["quorum_met"],
            "yes": decision["yes"],
            "no": decision["no"],
            "abstain": decision["abstain"],
            "denominator": decision["denominator"],
            "ratio": decision["ratio"],
            "outcome": decision["outcome"],
            "hand_expected_outcome": case["expected_outcome"],
            "hand_ratio": case.get("hand_ratio"),
            "match": decision["outcome"] == case["expected_outcome"]
            and decision["yes"] == case["expected_yes"]
            and (
                case.get("expected_denominator") is None
                or decision["denominator"] == case["expected_denominator"]
            ),
        }
        rows.append(row)
        if not row["match"]:
            mismatches.append(row)
    if mismatches:
        print(json.dumps(mismatches, indent=2)[:4000])
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0302 matrix mismatches={len(mismatches)}")
        return 1

    exclude = tally.tally_motion(
        expected["ballots"],
        threshold_id="majority",
        abstention_policy_id="exclude_from_denominator",
        quorum_required=4,
        seated=6,
        motion_id="neg-exclude",
    )
    include = tally.tally_motion(
        expected["ballots"],
        threshold_id="majority",
        abstention_policy_id="include_in_denominator",
        quorum_required=4,
        seated=6,

--- load.py ---
"""Load tools/council/vote/tally.py without requiring an installed package."""

def load_tally():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("imp0302_council_vote_tally", TALLY_PATH)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise ImportError(f"cannot load {TALLY_PATH}")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)

--- probe1_tally_matrix.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Named probe 1: 24-case tally matrix vs hand-computed expected values.

Signal 1: live tally_motion outcomes match expected_matrix.json (authored by
hand, not generated from the engine). Signal 2: every decision artifact carries
the abstention_policy_id that produced its arithmetic.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from load import load_tally  # noqa: E402

ART = REPO / "command-center/receipts/IMP-0302.json"
EXPECTED_PATH = HERE / "expected_matrix.json"
THRESHOLDS = {"majority", "supermajority", "unanimity"}
POLICIES = {
    "exclude_from_denominator",
    "include_in_denominator",
    "count_as_no",
    "park_on_any_abstention",
}
QUORUMS = {4, 7}


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:IMP-0302-probe1 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    if not EXPECTED_PATH.is_file():
        return fail("expected_matrix.json missing")
    expected = json.loads(EXPECTED_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    cases = expected.get("cases") or []
    if len(cases) != 24:
        return fail(f"expected 24 cases, got {len(cases)}")

    combos = {
        (c["threshold_id"], c["abstention_policy_id"], c["quorum_required"])
        for c in cases
    }
    want = {(t, p, q) for t in THRESHOLDS for p in POLICIES for q in QUORUMS}
    if combos != want:
        missing = sorted(want - combos)
        extra = sorted(combos - want)
        return fail(f"matrix not a full 3x4x2 product missing={missing} extra={extra}")

    tally = load_tally()
    ballots = expected["ballots"]
    mismatches = []
    missing_policy = []
    for case in cases:
        art = tally.tally_motion(
            ballots,
            threshold_id=case["threshold_id"],
            abstention_policy_id=case["abstention_policy_id"],
            quorum_required=case["quorum_required"],
            seated=expected["seated"],
            motion_id=case["case_id"],
        )
        if art.get("abstention_policy_id") != case["abstention_policy_id"]:
            missing_policy.append(case["case_id"])
        if art.get("policy_recorded") is not True:
            missing_policy.append(case["case_id"])
        got = {
            "outcome": art.get("outcome"),
            "yes": art.get("yes"),
            "denominator": art.get("denominator")
            if case.get("expected_denominator") is not None
            else None,
        }
        exp = {
            "outcome": case["expected_outcome"],
            "yes": case["expected_yes"],
            "denominator": case.get("expected_denominator"),
        }
        if case.get("expected_effective_no") is not None:
            got["effective_no"] = art.get("effective_no")
            exp["effective_no"] = case["expected_effective_no"]
        if case.get("hand_ratio") and case["expected_outcome"] not in {
            "PARKED",
            "NO_QUORUM",
        }:
            got["ratio"] = art.get("ratio")
            exp["ratio"] = case["hand_ratio"]
        if got != exp:
            mismatches.append({"case_id": case["case_id"], "expected": exp, "got": got})

    if missing_policy:
        return fail(f"policy id absent on {missing_policy}")
    if mismatches:
        print(json.dumps(mismatches, indent=2)[:4000])
        return fail(f"{len(mismatches)} matrix mismatches")

--- probe2_policy_flip.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Named probe 2: changing the abstention policy must flip a recorded outcome.

Negative control required by the catalog: same ballots, same threshold, same
quorum; only abstention_policy_id changes; at least one outcome flips. That is
the proof that denominator treatment is configurable rather than implicit.

Both-states on the load-bearing pair:
  majority + quorum 4 + exclude_from_denominator -> ADOPT  (3/4 > 1/2)
  majority + quorum 4 + include_in_denominator   -> REJECT (3/6 is not > 1/2)

A second control: stripping abstention_policy_id must fail artifact validation.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from load import load_tally  # noqa: E402

ART = REPO / "command-center/receipts/IMP-0302.json"
BALLOTS = ["yes", "yes", "yes", "no", "abstain", "abstain"]


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:IMP-0302-probe2 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    tally = load_tally()
    exclude = tally.tally_motion(
        BALLOTS,
        threshold_id="majority",
        abstention_policy_id="exclude_from_denominator",
        quorum_required=4,
        seated=6,
        motion_id="neg-exclude",
    )
    include = tally.tally_motion(
        BALLOTS,
        threshold_id="majority",
        abstention_policy_id="include_in_denominator",
        quorum_required=4,
        seated=6,
        motion_id="neg-include",
    )
    if exclude.get("outcome") != "ADOPT":
        return fail(f"exclude outcome={exclude.get('outcome')} want=ADOPT")
    if include.get("outcome") != "REJECT":
        return fail(f"include outcome={include.get('outcome')} want=REJECT")
    if exclude.get("outcome") == include.get("outcome"):
        return fail("policy change did not flip the recorded outcome")
    if exclude.get("abstention_policy_id") == include.get("abstention_policy_id"):
        return fail("policy ids identical after policy change")
    if exclude.get("denominator") == include.get("denominator"):
        return fail("denominators identical; policy is not load-bearing")

    park = tally.tally_motion(
        BALLOTS,
        threshold_id="majority",
        abstention_policy_id="park_on_any_abstention",
        quorum_required=4,
        seated=6,
        motion_id="neg-park",
    )
    if park.get("outcome") != "PARKED":
        return fail(f"park outcome={park.get('outcome')} want=PARKED")

    stripped = tally.policy_without_id(exclude)
    fired = False
    try:
        tally.validate_decision_artifact(stripped)
    except ValueError:
        fired = True
    if not fired:
        return fail("missing policy id was accepted (policy is not load-bearing)")
    try:
        tally.validate_decision_artifact(exclude)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return fail(f"good artifact rejected: {exc}")

    if not ART.is_file() or ART.stat().st_size < 200:
        return fail("catalog receipt missing")
    receipt = json.loads(ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if "SUCCESS:IMP-0302" not in json.dumps(receipt):
        return fail("sentinel absent from receipt")
    controls = receipt.get("negative_controls") or []
    if not any(c.get("fired_on_bad_state") is True for c in controls if isinstance(c, dict)):
        return fail("receipt negative_controls missing fired_on_bad_state")

    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "probe": "probe2-policy-flip",

IMP-0317
--- emit_receipt.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/IMP-0317.json from live persist/scan/retention runs."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import sys
import tempfile
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO))
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from tools.council.artifacts.retention import (  # noqa: E402
    PersistenceRefused,
    RetentionPolicy,
    apply_retention,
    persist_transcript,
)
import corpus as C  # noqa: E402
import gitleaks_style as G  # noqa: E402

OUT = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0317.json"
MODULE = "tools/council/artifacts/retention.py"
P1 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0317/probe1_planted_zero_survivors.py"
)
P2 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0317/probe2_negative_control.py"
)


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def live_measure() -> dict:
    out: dict = {}
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0317-emit-") as td:
        root = Path(td)
        clean = root / "clean" / "transcript.json"
        dirty = root / "dirty" / "transcript.json"
        meta = persist_transcript(
            C.transcript(),
            clean,
            RetentionPolicy(redact=True, refuse_if_survivors=True),
        )
        clean_surv = C.survivor_kinds(clean.read_bytes())
        clean_gl = G.scan_dir(clean.parent)
        persist_transcript(
            C.transcript(),
            dirty,
            RetentionPolicy(
                redact=False,
                refuse_if_survivors=False,
                allow_unredacted_for_test=True,
            ),
        )
        dirty_surv = C.survivor_kinds(dirty.read_bytes())
        dirty_gl = G.scan_dir(dirty.parent)
        dirty_rules = sorted({h["rule_id"] for h in dirty_gl})
        refused = False
        try:
            persist_transcript(
                C.transcript(),
                root / "refused.json",
                RetentionPolicy(redact=False, allow_unredacted_for_test=False),
            )
        except PersistenceRefused:
            refused = True
        now = datetime(2026, 8, 24, 18, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        store = root / "retain"
        store.mkdir()
        ttl = RetentionPolicy(redact=True, retention_seconds=60)
        persist_transcript(
            {"schema": "council-transcript/v1", "turns": [{"text": "no secrets"}]},
            store / "expired.json",
            ttl,
            now=now - timedelta(seconds=120),
        )
        persist_transcript(
            {"schema": "council-transcript/v1", "turns": [{"text": "no secrets"}]},
            store / "fresh.json",
            ttl,
            now=now,
        )
        ret = apply_retention(store, ttl, now=now)
        out = {
            "clean_bytes": meta["bytes"],
            "clean_sha256": meta["sha256"],
            "clean_survivors": clean_surv,
            "clean_survivor_count": len(clean_surv),
            "clean_gitleaks_hits": len(clean_gl),
            "dirty_survivors": dirty_surv,

--- probe1_planted_zero_survivors.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: planted-secret corpus, redactor ON, zero survivors.

Live persist through tools/council/artifacts/retention.py, then:
  (1) exact-body survivor check across all 4 encodings (must be 0)
  (2) independent gitleaks-style scan of the same artifact directory (must be 0
      findings that still carry a planted body)

Both-states is probe 2 (redactor disabled). This probe is the good state.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO))
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from tools.council.artifacts.retention import (  # noqa: E402
    RetentionPolicy,
    persist_transcript,
)
import corpus as C  # noqa: E402
import gitleaks_style as G  # noqa: E402

ART = REPO / "command-center/receipts/IMP-0317.json"


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:IMP-0317-probe1 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    policy = RetentionPolicy(redact=True, refuse_if_survivors=True)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0317-p1-") as td:
        dest = Path(td) / "council" / "transcript.json"
        meta = persist_transcript(C.transcript(), dest, policy)
        data = dest.read_bytes()
        survivors = C.survivor_kinds(data)
        gl = G.scan_dir(dest.parent)

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "probe": "probe1-planted-zero-survivors",
                    "unit_id": "IMP-0317",
                    "bytes": meta["bytes"],
                    "redaction_applied": meta["redaction_applied"],
                    "survivor_kinds": survivors,
                    "survivor_count": len(survivors),
                    "gitleaks_hits": len(gl),
                    "encodings_checked": list(C.ENCODINGS),
                },
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )
        if not meta["redaction_applied"]:
            return fail("redaction_applied is false on the shipped persist path")
        if survivors:
            return fail(f"survivors in persisted artifact: {survivors}")
        if gl:
            return fail(f"gitleaks-style scan fired on redacted artifact: {gl}")
        if not dest.is_file() or dest.stat().st_size <= 0:
            return fail("persisted artifact missing")

    if not ART.is_file() or ART.stat().st_size <= 0:
        return fail("receipt missing")
    doc = json.loads(ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    st = str(doc.get("state") or "").upper()
    if st in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
        return fail(f"self-declared {st}")
    text = ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if "SUCCESS:IMP-0317" not in text:
        return fail("sentinel-absent")
    ev = doc.get("evidence") or []
    if not isinstance(ev, list) or len(ev) < 2:
        return fail("evidence[] missing two independent signals")
    zero = False
    for item in ev:
        if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("id") == "planted-secret-corpus":
            if item.get("survivors") == 0:
                zero = True
    if not zero:
        return fail("receipt does not record 0 survivors for planted-secret-corpus")
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0317-probe1 planted corpus zero survivors under redactor")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe2_negative_control.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: negative control — disable the redactor; all 4 forms must be found.

Proves the scanner is not silently passing: the same exact-body detector and
the independent gitleaks-style scanner both fire on the unredacted persist.
Also proves declared retention actually deletes an expired artifact and keeps
one still inside the window.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import tempfile
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO))
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from tools.council.artifacts.retention import (  # noqa: E402
    PersistenceRefused,
    RetentionPolicy,
    apply_retention,
    persist_transcript,
)
import corpus as C  # noqa: E402
import gitleaks_style as G  # noqa: E402

ART = REPO / "command-center/receipts/IMP-0317.json"


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:IMP-0317-probe2 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    # Hard rule: production persist without the test flag must refuse.
    refused = False
    try:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0317-p2-refuse-") as td:
            persist_transcript(
                C.transcript(),
                Path(td) / "x.json",
                RetentionPolicy(redact=False, allow_unredacted_for_test=False),
            )
    except PersistenceRefused:
        refused = True
    if not refused:
        return fail("unredacted persist without test flag was allowed")

    policy = RetentionPolicy(
        redact=False,
        refuse_if_survivors=False,
        allow_unredacted_for_test=True,
        persistence="negative-control-unredacted",
    )
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0317-p2-") as td:
        dest = Path(td) / "council" / "transcript.json"
        persist_transcript(C.transcript(), dest, policy)
        data = dest.read_bytes()
        survivors = C.survivor_kinds(data)
        gl = G.scan_dir(dest.parent)
        present = C.encodings_present(data)
        gl_rules = sorted({h["rule_id"] for h in gl})

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "probe": "probe2-negative-control",
                    "unit_id": "IMP-0317",
                    "production_unredacted_refused": True,
                    "survivor_kinds": survivors,
                    "survivor_count": len(survivors),
                    "gitleaks_hits": len(gl),
                    "gitleaks_rule_ids": gl_rules,
                    "encodings_present": present,
                },
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )
        missing = [k for k in C.ENCODINGS if k not in survivors]
        if missing:
            return fail(
                f"scanner silent on disabled-redactor persist; missed {missing}"
            )
        if len(gl) < 1:
            return fail("independent gitleaks-style scan found 0 hits on dirty artifact")
        # Each encoding should be visible to at least the exact-body detector;
        # gitleaks should fire on env, json, camelCase, and base64 forms.
        needed_rules = {
            "env-assignment",
            "json-password",
            "camelcase-apikey",
            "base64-assignment",
        }
        if not needed_rules.issubset(set(gl_rules)):
            return fail(
