IMP-0320
--- emit_receipt.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/IMP-0320.json from a live detector run."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from fixtures import (  # noqa: E402
    collapsed_council,
    diverse_council,
    hard_same_model_strong_roles,
)
from load import load_detector  # noqa: E402

OUT = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0320.json"
MODULE = "tools/council/antifail/role_collapse.py"


def digest(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def art_row(rel: str, role: str) -> dict:
    path = REPO / rel
    sha, size = digest(path)
    return {
        "path": rel,
        "sha256": sha,
        "bytes": size,
        "present": True,
        "exists": path.is_file(),
        "role": role,
        "unit_id": "IMP-0320",
    }


def slim(det: dict) -> dict:
    return {
        "council_id": det.get("council_id"),
        "status": det["status"],
        "fired": det["fired"],
        "mean_pairwise_jaccard": det["mean_pairwise_jaccard"],
        "mean_role_vocab_score": det["mean_role_vocab_score"],
        "pairwise": det["pairwise"],
        "role_vocab": det["role_vocab"],
        "unique_models": det["unique_models"],
        "reason": det["reason"],
    }


def main() -> int:
    D = load_detector()
    collapsed = collapsed_council()
    diverse = diverse_council()
    hard = hard_same_model_strong_roles()
    bad = D.detect(collapsed)
    good = D.detect(diverse)
    hard_det = D.detect(hard)
    decoy = D.declared_role_decoy(collapsed)
    if not bad["fired"] or good["fired"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0320 emit collapsed/diverse did not separate")
        return 1
    if decoy["fired"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0320 emit declared-role decoy fired on distinct labels")
        return 1
    if hard_det["fired"]:
        print(
            "FAILED:IMP-0320 emit hard same-model strong-role FIRE; "
            "do not retune — fixtures should be SILENT on behaviour"
        )
        return 1

    bad_vals = [p["jaccard"] for p in bad["pairwise"]]
    good_vals = [p["jaccard"] for p in good["pairwise"]]
    plot = D.side_by_side_histograms(bad_vals, good_vals)
    if min(bad_vals) <= max(good_vals):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0320 emit similarity distributions overlap")
        return 1

    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    artifacts = [
        art_row(MODULE, "primary-module"),
        art_row("command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0320/fixtures.py", "fixtures"),
        art_row(
            "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0320/probe1_collapsed_vs_diverse.py",
            "probe",
        ),
        art_row(
            "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0320/probe2_hard_case_vocab.py",
            "probe",
        ),
    ]

--- load.py ---
"""Load tools/council/antifail/role_collapse.py without an installed package."""
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
MODULE_PATH = REPO / "tools/council/antifail/role_collapse.py"


def load_detector():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
        "imp0320_role_collapse", MODULE_PATH
    )
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise ImportError(f"cannot load {MODULE_PATH}")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod

--- probe1_collapsed_vs_diverse.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: collapsed FIRE, diverse SILENT; similarity distributions separated.

Detector-control order: known-present collapsed is scored BEFORE diverse
silence. A declared-role-label decoy must stay SILENT on collapsed (roles
differ; outputs do not).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from fixtures import collapsed_council, diverse_council  # noqa: E402
from load import load_detector  # noqa: E402

ART = REPO / "command-center/receipts/IMP-0320.json"


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:IMP-0320-probe1 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    D = load_detector()
    collapsed = collapsed_council()
    diverse = diverse_council()

    # Detector-control first: known-present pathology.
    bad = D.detect(collapsed)
    print("detector_control_first=True fixture=collapsed")
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "id": collapsed["id"],
                "fired": bad["fired"],
                "status": bad["status"],
                "mean_pairwise_jaccard": bad["mean_pairwise_jaccard"],
                "mean_role_vocab_score": bad["mean_role_vocab_score"],
            }
        )
    )
    if not bad["fired"] or bad["status"] != "FIRE":
        return fail("collapsed council did not FIRE")
    print("detector_control_found_known_present=True")

    good = D.detect(diverse)
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "id": diverse["id"],
                "fired": good["fired"],
                "status": good["status"],
                "mean_pairwise_jaccard": good["mean_pairwise_jaccard"],
                "mean_role_vocab_score": good["mean_role_vocab_score"],
            }
        )
    )
    if good["fired"] or good["status"] != "SILENT":
        return fail("diverse council was not SILENT")

    bad_vals = [p["jaccard"] for p in bad["pairwise"]]
    good_vals = [p["jaccard"] for p in good["pairwise"]]
    if min(bad_vals) <= max(good_vals):
        return fail(
            f"distributions overlap collapsed_min={min(bad_vals)} "
            f"diverse_max={max(good_vals)}"
        )
    plot = D.side_by_side_histograms(bad_vals, good_vals)
    print(plot)

    decoy = D.declared_role_decoy(collapsed)
    print(
        f"decoy_declared_roles status={decoy['status']} "
        f"unique={decoy['unique_roles']}"
    )
    if decoy["fired"]:
        return fail("declared-role decoy FIRE on distinct labels; that measures labels")

    if not ART.is_file() or ART.stat().st_size <= 0:
        return fail("receipt missing")
    text = ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if "SUCCESS:IMP-0320" not in text:
        return fail("sentinel-absent")
    doc = json.loads(text)
    if str(doc.get("state") or "").upper() in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
        return fail(f"self-declared:{doc.get('state')}")

    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "probe": "probe1-collapsed-vs-diverse",
                "unit_id": "IMP-0320",
                "collapsed_fired": True,
                "diverse_fired": False,

--- probe2_hard_case_vocab.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: per-seat role vocabulary + honest same-model hard-case class.

Negative control: a single-model council with strong role prompts is scored
as-is. Model ids are not a fire input: swapping every model label must not
change the verdict. Receipt must report that same classification rather than
a tuned story.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from copy import deepcopy
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from fixtures import (  # noqa: E402
    collapsed_council,
    diverse_council,
    hard_same_model_strong_roles,
)
from load import load_detector  # noqa: E402

ART = REPO / "command-center/receipts/IMP-0320.json"


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:IMP-0320-probe2 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    D = load_detector()
    hard = hard_same_model_strong_roles()
    live = D.detect(hard)
    print("hard_case_id=" + str(hard["id"]))
    print(f"hard_status={live['status']} fired={live['fired']}")
    print(f"hard_mean_pairwise={live['mean_pairwise_jaccard']}")
    print(f"hard_mean_role_vocab={live['mean_role_vocab_score']}")
    print(f"hard_unique_models={live['unique_models']}")

    rows = live["role_vocab"]
    for row in rows:
        print(
            f"seat_role={row['role']} vocab_score={row['score']} "
            f"found={row['found']} missing={row['missing']}"
        )
        if row["score"] < D.VOCAB_FIRE:
            return fail(f"hard-case seat {row['role']} lost role vocabulary")

    mutated = deepcopy(hard)
    for i, seat in enumerate(mutated["seats"]):
        seat["model"] = f"mutant-model-{i}"
    mut = D.detect(mutated)
    print(f"model_label_mutation_status={mut['status']}")
    if mut["fired"] != live["fired"] or mut["status"] != live["status"]:
        return fail("verdict changed after model-id mutation; model id leaked into the rule")
    if abs(mut["mean_pairwise_jaccard"] - live["mean_pairwise_jaccard"]) > 1e-9:
        return fail("similarity moved after model-id mutation")

    # Hard case is expected SILENT given these outputs. If it FIREd, that
    # would still be reported; we refuse only a silent retune in the receipt.
    if live["fired"]:
        return fail(
            "hard same-model strong-role outputs FIRE; fixtures are role-shaped "
            "and should be SILENT, or thresholds were tuned onto model identity"
        )

    collapsed = D.detect(collapsed_council())
    diverse = D.detect(diverse_council())
    if not collapsed["fired"] or diverse["fired"]:
        return fail("anchor pair collapsed/diverse no longer separates")

    if not ART.is_file():
        return fail("receipt missing")
    doc = json.loads(ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if "SUCCESS:IMP-0320" not in ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8"):
        return fail("sentinel-absent")
    required = [
        "unit_id",
        "artifact_paths",
        "spec_anchor",
        "evidence",
        "test_ids",
        "negative_control_ids",
        "reviewer_verdict",
        "residual_unknowns",
    ]
    missing = [k for k in required if k not in doc]
    if missing:
        return fail(f"receipt missing profile keys {missing}")

    reported = doc.get("hard_same_model_strong_roles") or {}
    if reported.get("fired") is not False:
        return fail("receipt overclaims FIRE on the hard case")
    if str(reported.get("status") or "").upper() != "SILENT":
        return fail("receipt does not honestly report SILENT on the hard case")

IMP-0353
--- probe1_chaos_log.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: chaos abort at 20 random mid-write points; every log parses.

Evidence requirement (2): event-log integrity after aborting at 20 random
points mid-write. Control: a naive JSONL torn mid-line does NOT parse.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO))

from tools.council.abort import (  # noqa: E402
    chaos_midwrite_trials,
    naive_jsonl_torn_parses,
    parse_log_bytes,
)

SEED = 353
N = 20


def main() -> int:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0353-p1-") as td:
        root = Path(td)
        chaos = chaos_midwrite_trials(root / "chaos", n=N, seed=SEED)
        naive_parses = naive_jsonl_torn_parses(root / "naive.jsonl", 12)

        # Re-read each trial file from disk (not the in-memory parse).
        disk_ok = 0
        for i in range(N):
            log_path = root / "chaos" / f"trial-{i:02d}" / "events.clog"
            parsed = parse_log_bytes(log_path.read_bytes())
            if parsed["parseable"] and not parsed["torn"] and parsed["events"]:
                disk_ok += 1

        failed = [t for t in chaos["trials"] if not t["ok"]]
        out = {
            "probe": "probe1-chaos-midwrite",
            "unit_id": "IMP-0353",
            "n": N,
            "seed": SEED,
            "frame_len": chaos["frame_len"],
            "all_parse": chaos["all_parse"],
            "disk_ok": disk_ok,
            "naive_jsonl_torn_parses": naive_parses,
            "naive_control_fired": (not naive_parses),
            "failed_trials": failed[:5],
            "pass": (
                chaos["all_parse"]
                and disk_ok == N
                and not naive_parses
                and len(chaos["trials"]) == N
            ),
        }
        print(json.dumps(out, sort_keys=True))
        if not out["pass"]:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0353-probe1 chaos log integrity")
            return 1
        print("SUCCESS:IMP-0353-probe1 chaos abort at 20 points, log parses every time")
        return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe2_consumer_refuse.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: downstream consumer refuses an aborted artifact as a decision.

Starts from consume_as_decision / strip_aborted_label, not an author summary.

Evidence requirement (1): consumer refuses the aborted artifact.
Negative control: strip the aborted label and the same consumer accepts.
Silent-on-good: a completed council is accepted.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO))

from tools.council.abort import (  # noqa: E402
    ABORTED_LABEL,
    NotADecision,
    consume_as_decision,
    run_council,
    strip_aborted_label,
)


def main() -> int:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0353-p2-") as td:
        root = Path(td)
        done = run_council(
            root / "complete.clog",
            council_id="completed",
            artifact_path=root / "complete.json",
        )
        killed = run_council(
            root / "aborted.clog",
            council_id="aborted",
            abort_after_events=6,
            tear_after_bytes=None,
            owner_id="owner",
            abort_reason="kill-switch",
            artifact_path=root / "aborted.json",
        )
        art_aborted = killed["artifact"]
        art_done = done["artifact"]

        if art_aborted.get("abort_label") != ABORTED_LABEL:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0353-probe2 aborted artifact missing ABORTED label")
            return 1
        if art_aborted.get("decision") is None:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0353-probe2 aborted artifact has no decision payload to misuse")
            return 1
        if art_done.get("abort_label") is not None:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0353-probe2 completed artifact is labelled aborted")
            return 1

        refused = False
        reason = ""
        try:
            consume_as_decision(art_aborted)
        except NotADecision as exc:
            refused = True
            reason = str(exc)

        stripped = strip_aborted_label(art_aborted)
        if "abort_label" in stripped:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0353-probe2 strip_aborted_label left abort_label")
            return 1
        accepted_stripped = False
        try:
            consume_as_decision(stripped)
            accepted_stripped = True
        except NotADecision:
            accepted_stripped = False

        still_refused = False
        try:
            consume_as_decision(art_aborted)
        except NotADecision:
            still_refused = True

        accepted_done = False
        try:
            consume_as_decision(art_done)
            accepted_done = True
        except NotADecision:
            accepted_done = False

        out = {
            "probe": "probe2-consumer-refuse",
            "unit_id": "IMP-0353",
            "refused_aborted": refused,
            "refused_reason": reason,
            "accepted_stripped_label": accepted_stripped,
            "original_still_refused": still_refused,
            "accepted_completed": accepted_done,
            "aborted_has_decision_payload": art_aborted.get("decision") is not None,

IMP-0387
--- probe1_both_states.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: both-states inversion for load_mode.

A fixture rule with no frontmatter must resolve ALWAYS-ON. The same body with
paths: ["**/*"] must resolve LAZY. A resolver that returns the same answer
for both states fails. The inspect instrument must independently confirm.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import scenario as S  # noqa: E402

ART = S.RECEIPT


def main() -> int:
    if not ART.is_file() or ART.stat().st_size <= 0:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe1 receipt missing")
        return 1
    text = ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    doc = json.loads(text)
    if doc.get("unit_id") != S.UNIT:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe1 unit_id", doc.get("unit_id"))
        return 1
    if str(doc.get("state") or "").upper() != "SUCCESS":
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe1 state", doc.get("state"))
        return 1
    if S.SENTINEL not in text:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe1 sentinel absent")
        return 1

    live = S.both_states()
    if live["same_answer_for_both_states"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe1 resolver returned the same load_mode for both states")
        print(json.dumps(live, indent=2))
        return 1
    if not live["inversion_holds"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe1 inversion does not hold")
        print(json.dumps(live, indent=2))
        return 1
    if not live["instrument_agrees"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe1 instrument did not confirm inversion")
        print(json.dumps(live, indent=2))
        return 1
    if not live["other_keys_do_not_demote"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe1 non-paths frontmatter demoted the file")
        return 1

    recorded = doc.get("both_states") or {}
    if recorded.get("no_frontmatter", {}).get("predicted") != S.ALWAYS_ON:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe1 receipt both_states no_frontmatter")
        return 1
    if recorded.get("with_paths_globstar", {}).get("predicted") != S.LAZY:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe1 receipt both_states globstar")
        return 1
    if recorded.get("inversion_holds") is not True:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe1 receipt inversion_holds")
        return 1

    rec = json.dumps(doc)
    if '"load_mode"' not in rec:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe1 queryable load_mode field absent from receipt")
        return 1

    print(
        f"no_frontmatter={live['no_frontmatter']['predicted']} "
        f"with_paths={live['with_paths_globstar']['predicted']} "
        f"other_keys={live['frontmatter_without_paths']['predicted']} "
        f"inversion={live['inversion_holds']} "
        f"instrument={live['instrument_agrees']}"
    )
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0387-probe1 both-states inversion ALWAYS-ON vs LAZY")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe2_predicted_vs_measured.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: predicted load_mode vs instrument-measured load_mode.

Two-column table over every scanned rules file. Predicted = YAML resolver.
Measured = inspect_load.py in a subprocess (grok inspect equivalent).
Disagreements are findings; the instrument wins. A census with zero ALWAYS-ON
rows is a broken instrument, not evidence of universal laziness.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import scenario as S  # noqa: E402

ART = S.RECEIPT
WELL_FORMED = {
    "always-on-no-frontmatter.md",
    "always-on-other-keys.md",
    "always-on-nested-paths.md",
    "lazy-globstar.md",
    "lazy-narrow.md",
    "lazy-empty-paths-list.md",
    "mention-in-body.md",
}


def main() -> int:
    if not ART.is_file() or ART.stat().st_size <= 0:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe2 receipt missing")
        return 1
    text = ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    doc = json.loads(text)
    if S.SENTINEL not in text:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe2 sentinel absent")
        return 1

    R = S.load_resolver()
    census = S.full_census(resolver=R)
    table = census["table"]
    if census["n_always_on"] < 1:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe2 instrument named zero ALWAYS-ON files")
        return 1
    if census["n_lazy"] < 1:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe2 census has zero LAZY files")
        return 1

    fixture_rows = [r for r in table if r["label"] == "fixture_rules"]
    if len(fixture_rows) < 5:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe2 fixture census too small", len(fixture_rows))
        return 1

    known_always = known_lazy = None
    for row in fixture_rows:
        if row["path"].endswith("always-on-no-frontmatter.md"):
            known_always = row
        if row["path"].endswith("lazy-globstar.md"):
            known_lazy = row
    if known_always is None or known_always["measured_load_mode"] != S.ALWAYS_ON:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe2 known always-on fixture not ALWAYS-ON under instrument")
        return 1
    if known_lazy is None or known_lazy["measured_load_mode"] != S.LAZY:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe2 globstar fixture not LAZY under instrument")
        return 1

    for path in R.iter_rule_files(S.FIXTURE_RULES):
        predicted = R.resolve_load_mode(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        measured = S.inspect_via_subprocess(path)
        if path.name == "always-on-no-frontmatter.md" and measured != S.ALWAYS_ON:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe2 subprocess inspect missed always-on", measured)
            return 1
        if path.name == "lazy-globstar.md" and measured != S.LAZY:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe2 subprocess inspect missed lazy", measured)
            return 1
        if path.name in WELL_FORMED and predicted != measured:
            print(
                f"FAILED:IMP-0387-probe2 well-formed fixture disagreement "
                f"{path.name} pred={predicted} meas={measured}"
            )
            return 1

    recorded = doc.get("load_mode_table") or []
    if len(recorded) != len(table):
        print(
            f"FAILED:IMP-0387-probe2 receipt table size {len(recorded)} live {len(table)}"
        )
        return 1
    live_by_path = {r["path"]: r for r in table}
    for rec_row in recorded:
        live_row = live_by_path.get(rec_row.get("path"))
        if live_row is None:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe2 receipt row missing live", rec_row.get("path"))
            return 1
        if rec_row.get("predicted_load_mode") != live_row["predicted_load_mode"]:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0387-probe2 predicted drift", rec_row.get("path"))
            return 1
        if rec_row.get("measured_load_mode") != live_row["measured_load_mode"]:

--- scenario.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Shared IMP-0387 fixtures, both-states texts, and predicted-vs-measured census."""
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
RESOLVER_PATH = REPO / "tools" / "router" / "load_semantics.py"
INSTRUMENT_PATH = REPO / "tools" / "router" / "inspect_load.py"
FIXTURE_RULES = HERE / "fixtures" / "rules"
RECEIPT = REPO / "command-center" / "receipts" / "IMP-0387.json"
SENTINEL = "SENTINEL:IMP-0387:LOAD-SEMANTICS-RESOLVED"
UNIT = "IMP-0387"
BODY = "# shared body\nKeep this payload identical across both states.\n"

NO_FRONTMATTER = BODY
WITH_PATHS_GLOBSTAR = "---\npaths:\n  - \"**/*\"\n---\n" + BODY
WITH_OTHER_KEYS = "---\ndescription: not-the-load-switch\n---\n" + BODY

ALWAYS_ON = "ALWAYS-ON"
LAZY = "LAZY"


def _load(path: Path, name: str):
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise RuntimeError(f"cannot load {path}")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


def load_resolver():
    return _load(RESOLVER_PATH, "imp0387_load_semantics")


def load_instrument():
    return _load(INSTRUMENT_PATH, "imp0387_inspect_load")


def grok_on_path() -> dict[str, Any]:
    exe = shutil.which("grok")
    return {
        "which": exe,
        "present": exe is not None,
        "note": (
            "grok inspect --json is a catalog input. It is ABSENT on this host; "
            "tools/router/inspect_load.py is the equivalent instrument."
        ),
    }


def live_roots() -> dict[str, Path]:
    return {
        "claude_rules": Path.home() / ".claude" / "rules",
        "repo_rules": REPO / "rules",
        "fixture_rules": FIXTURE_RULES,
        "capability_index": REPO / "capability-index.json",
    }


def rel_or_abs(path: Path) -> str:
    try:
        return path.resolve().relative_to(REPO.resolve()).as_posix()
    except ValueError:
        return str(path.resolve())


def both_states(resolver=None, instrument=None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    R = resolver or load_resolver()
    I = instrument or load_instrument()
    pred_off = R.resolve_load_mode(NO_FRONTMATTER)
    pred_on = R.resolve_load_mode(WITH_PATHS_GLOBSTAR)
    pred_other = R.resolve_load_mode(WITH_OTHER_KEYS)
    meas_off = I.inspect_load_mode(NO_FRONTMATTER)
    meas_on = I.inspect_load_mode(WITH_PATHS_GLOBSTAR)
    meas_other = I.inspect_load_mode(WITH_OTHER_KEYS)
    inversion = pred_off == ALWAYS_ON and pred_on == LAZY and pred_off != pred_on
    instrument_agrees = (
        meas_off == ALWAYS_ON and meas_on == LAZY and meas_other == ALWAYS_ON
    )
    return {
        "no_frontmatter": {
            "predicted": pred_off,
            "measured": meas_off,
            "want": ALWAYS_ON,
        },
        "with_paths_globstar": {
            "predicted": pred_on,
            "measured": meas_on,
            "want": LAZY,
        },
        "frontmatter_without_paths": {
            "predicted": pred_other,

--- emit_receipt.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/IMP-0396.json from a live no-fit router run."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import scenario as S  # noqa: E402

REPO = S.REPO
OUT = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0396.json"
P1 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0396/probe1_terminal_audit.py"
)
P2 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0396/probe2_threshold_mutation.py"
)
QUESTION = (
    "Implement the no-fit path so the router emits an explicit typed "
    "NO-CAPABILITY-FITS verdict with the closest rejected candidates, rather "
    "than injecting a weak match. Exact target: silence must be impossible - "
    "every route call returns a terminal state, either a selection or an "
    "explicit no-fit."
)


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def dep_status(rel: str) -> dict:
    p = REPO / rel
    return {
        "path": rel,
        "present": p.is_file(),
        "bytes": p.stat().st_size if p.is_file() else 0,
    }


def main() -> int:
    n = S.load_nofit()
    caps = S.capabilities(n)
    nofit_q = S.nofit_queries(n)
    fit_q = S.fit_queries(n)
    corpus = n.nofit_corpus_all_nofit(caps, nofit_q)
    if not corpus["pass"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396 emit no-fit corpus is not all NO-CAPABILITY-FITS")
        return 1
    fit_fail = []
    for q in fit_q:
        result = n.route(q, caps)
        if result.get("verdict") != n.VERDICT_SELECTED:
            fit_fail.append(q)
    if fit_fail:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396 emit fit corpus not SELECTED", fit_fail)
        return 1
    audit = n.terminal_audit(caps, extra=nofit_q + fit_q)
    if not audit["pass"] or audit["terminal"] != 100 or audit["empty_or_null"] != 0:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396 emit terminal audit")
        print(json.dumps(audit, indent=2)[:2000])
        return 1
    mutation = n.threshold_mutation_control(caps, nofit_q)
    if not mutation["pass"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396 emit mutation control")
        return 1
    hunt = n.silence_hunt(caps)

    worked = []
    for q in nofit_q[:3] + [row["query"] for row in mutation["injected"][:2]]:
        result = n.route(q, caps)
        worked.append(
            {
                "query": n.coerce_query(q),
                "verdict": result["verdict"],
                "best_score": (result.get("ranked") or [{}])[0].get("score")
                if result.get("ranked")
                else 0.0,
                "closest_rejected": result.get("closest_rejected"),
                "reason": result.get("reason"),
            }
        )

    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    impl = REPO / "tools/router/nofit.py"
    impl_sha, impl_bytes = sha256_file(impl)
    idx_sha, idx_bytes = sha256_file(S.INDEX_PATH)
    nf_sha, nf_bytes = sha256_file(S.NOFIT_CORPUS)

    doc = {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "unit_id": "IMP-0396",
        "state": "SUCCESS",

--- probe1_terminal_audit.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Named probe 1: no-fit corpus + 100-query terminal-state audit.

Every labelled no-fit query must return NO-CAPABILITY-FITS. One hundred
seeded random queries (including adversarial non-strings) must all return a
terminal verdict; empty/null returns fail the probe. A labelled fit corpus
must still SELECT, so the detector is not 'always no-fit'.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import scenario as S  # noqa: E402

ART = S.RECEIPT


def main() -> int:
    if not ART.is_file() or ART.stat().st_size <= 0:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe1 receipt missing")
        return 1
    doc = json.loads(ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    text = ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if doc.get("unit_id") != "IMP-0396":
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe1 unit_id", doc.get("unit_id"))
        return 1
    if str(doc.get("state") or "").upper() != "SUCCESS":
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe1 state", doc.get("state"))
        return 1
    if S.SENTINEL not in text:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe1 sentinel absent")
        return 1

    n = S.load_nofit()
    caps = S.capabilities(n)
    nofit_q = S.nofit_queries(n)
    fit_q = S.fit_queries(n)

    corpus = n.nofit_corpus_all_nofit(caps, nofit_q)
    print(
        f"nofit_corpus n={corpus['n']} fail={corpus['n_fail']} "
        f"threshold={corpus['threshold']} pass={corpus['pass']}"
    )
    if not corpus["pass"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe1 no-fit corpus leaked a fit")
        print(json.dumps(corpus["failures"][:5], indent=2)[:2000])
        return 1

    for q in fit_q:
        result = n.route(q, caps)
        if not n.is_terminal(result) or result["verdict"] != n.VERDICT_SELECTED:
            print(f"FAILED:IMP-0396-probe1 fit query not SELECTED {q!r} {result.get('verdict')}")
            return 1
    print(f"fit_corpus n={len(fit_q)} all_selected=True")

    audit = n.terminal_audit(caps, extra=nofit_q + fit_q)
    print(
        f"terminal_audit n={audit['n']} terminal={audit['terminal']} "
        f"non_terminal={audit['non_terminal']} empty_or_null={audit['empty_or_null']} "
        f"verdicts={audit['verdicts']} pass={audit['pass']}"
    )
    if not audit["pass"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe1 non-terminal return in 100-query audit")
        print(json.dumps(audit["failures"][:8], indent=2)[:2000])
        return 1
    if audit["terminal"] != 100 or audit["empty_or_null"] != 0:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe1 audit counts")
        return 1

    recorded = doc.get("terminal_state_audit") or {}
    if int(recorded.get("n") or 0) != 100 or int(recorded.get("terminal") or 0) != 100:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe1 receipt audit does not claim 100/100")
        return 1
    if int(recorded.get("empty_or_null", -1)) != 0:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe1 receipt empty_or_null")
        return 1
    if int(recorded.get("seed") or 0) != audit["seed"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe1 receipt seed drift")
        return 1
    if recorded.get("verdicts") != audit["verdicts"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe1 receipt verdict tally drift")
        print("recorded", recorded.get("verdicts"))
        print("live", audit["verdicts"])
        return 1

    hunt = n.silence_hunt(caps)
    print(
        f"silence_hunt attempts={hunt['attempts']} hits={len(hunt['non_terminal_hits'])} "
        f"pass={hunt['pass']}"
    )
    if not hunt["pass"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe1 silence hunt found a non-terminal return")
        return 1

    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0396-probe1 no-fit corpus and terminal-state audit")
    return 0

--- probe2_threshold_mutation.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Named probe 2: threshold-lowering mutation injects a weak match.

The no-fit corpus test is green at the production FIT_THRESHOLD. Lowering the
threshold so a weak overlap is injected as SELECTED must turn that same test
red. A detector that stays green on both states is a broken detector.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import scenario as S  # noqa: E402

ART = S.RECEIPT


def main() -> int:
    if not ART.is_file():
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe2 receipt missing")
        return 1
    doc = json.loads(ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if S.SENTINEL not in ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8"):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe2 sentinel absent")
        return 1

    n = S.load_nofit()
    caps = S.capabilities(n)
    queries = S.nofit_queries(n)
    control = n.threshold_mutation_control(caps, queries)
    print(
        f"production pass={control['production']['pass']} "
        f"n={control['production']['n']} fail={control['production']['n_fail']} "
        f"threshold={control['production']['threshold']}"
    )
    print(
        f"mutant pass={control['mutant']['pass']} "
        f"n={control['mutant']['n']} fail={control['mutant']['n_fail']} "
        f"injected={control['mutant']['injected_weak_matches']} "
        f"threshold={control['mutant']['threshold']}"
    )
    print(
        f"silent_on_good={control['silent_on_good_state']} "
        f"fired_on_bad={control['fired_on_bad_state']}"
    )
    if not control["silent_on_good_state"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe2 production no-fit corpus is not green")
        return 1
    if not control["fired_on_bad_state"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe2 lowering the threshold did not inject a weak match")
        return 1
    if control["mutant"]["pass"] is not False:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe2 mutant corpus test stayed green")
        return 1
    if control["mutant"]["injected_weak_matches"] < 1:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe2 no weak match was injected")
        return 1

    recorded = doc.get("threshold_mutation") or {}
    if recorded.get("fired_on_bad_state") is not True:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe2 receipt missing fired_on_bad_state")
        return 1
    if recorded.get("silent_on_good_state") is not True:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0396-probe2 receipt missing silent_on_good_state")
        return 1
    rec_n = int((recorded.get("mutant") or {}).get("injected_weak_matches") or 0)
    if rec_n != control["mutant"]["injected_weak_matches"]:
        print(
            "FAILED:IMP-0396-probe2 receipt injected count drift "
            f"{rec_n} != {control['mutant']['injected_weak_matches']}"
        )
        return 1

    sample = control["injected"][0]
    print(
        f"sample_injection query={sample['query']!r} "
        f"prod={sample['production_verdict']}@{sample['production_best']} "
        f"mut={sample['mutant_verdict']} {sample['mutant_selected']}@{sample['mutant_score']}"
    )
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0396-probe2 threshold mutation injects weak match")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- scenario.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Load IMP-0396 fixtures and the production no-fit router."""
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
NOFIT_PATH = REPO / "tools" / "router" / "nofit.py"
INDEX_PATH = HERE / "fixtures" / "capability-index.json"
NOFIT_CORPUS = HERE / "fixtures" / "nofit-corpus.json"
FIT_CORPUS = HERE / "fixtures" / "fit-corpus.json"
RECEIPT = REPO / "command-center" / "receipts" / "IMP-0396.json"
SENTINEL = "SENTINEL:IMP-0396:NO-FIT-TERMINAL"


def load_nofit():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("imp0396_nofit", NOFIT_PATH)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise RuntimeError(f"cannot load {NOFIT_PATH}")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


def capabilities(mod=None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    n = mod or load_nofit()
    return n.load_index(INDEX_PATH)


def nofit_queries(mod=None) -> list[str]:
    n = mod or load_nofit()
    return n.load_queries(NOFIT_CORPUS)


def fit_queries(mod=None) -> list[str]:
    n = mod or load_nofit()
    return n.load_queries(FIT_CORPUS)

IMP-0401
--- emit_receipt.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/IMP-0401.json after a live host scan + fixture detect."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import importlib.util
import json
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
OUT = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0401.json"
ANNOTATED = "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0401/capability-index.annotated.json"
SENTINEL = "SENTINEL:IMP-0401:PRESENCE-GAP-ENUMERATED"
QUESTION = (
    "how many indexed capabilities are repo-only, and how many are live-only?"
)


def load_presence_gap():
    path = REPO / "tools" / "router" / "presence_gap.py"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("imp0401_presence_gap", path)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise ImportError(f"cannot load {path}")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


def sha256_file(rel: str) -> dict:
    path = REPO / rel
    raw = path.read_bytes() if path.is_file() else b""
    return {
        "path": rel,
        "bytes": len(raw),
        "sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw.replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")).hexdigest() if raw else "",
        "exists": path.is_file(),
        "role": "supporting",
        "eol": "LF",
    }


def main() -> int:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
    import fixtures as F  # noqa: E402

    pg = load_presence_gap()
    started = datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(microsecond=0).isoformat().replace(
        "+00:00", "Z"
    )

    host_repo = [pg.default_repo_skill_root(REPO)]
    host_live = pg.default_live_skill_roots()
    host = pg.detect(host_repo, host_live, index=None)
    fixture = pg.detect(
        F.fixture_repo_roots(), F.fixture_live_roots(), index=F.INDEX_PATH
    )
    if not fixture["disjoint"] or not host["disjoint"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401 emit lists were not disjoint")
        return 1
    if F.PLANTED_REPO_ONLY not in fixture["repo_only"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401 emit fixture repo-only missing")
        return 1
    if F.PLANTED_LIVE_ONLY not in fixture["live_only"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401 emit fixture live-only missing")
        return 1
    if F.PLANTED_BOTH in fixture["repo_only"] or F.PLANTED_BOTH in fixture["live_only"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401 emit fixture both leaked")
        return 1

    annotated_path = REPO / ANNOTATED
    annotated_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    annotated_doc = {
        "unit_id": "IMP-0401",
        "kind": "capability-index-presence-gap",
        "host": host["annotated_index"],
        "fixture_control": fixture["annotated_index"],
        "sentinel": SENTINEL,
    }
    annotated_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(annotated_doc, indent=1, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
    )

    host_trees_present = any(
        r["is_dir"] and r["skill_md_count"] > 0
        for r in (host["repo_roots"] + host["live_roots"])
    )
    ended = datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(microsecond=0).isoformat().replace(
        "+00:00", "Z"
    )

    receipt = {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "unit_id": "IMP-0401",
        "state": "SUCCESS",
        "sentinel": SENTINEL,
        "completion_sentinel": SENTINEL,
        "spec_anchor": "command-center/WORK-UNIT-CATALOG.json#IMP-0401",

--- probe1_detector_control.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: detector control on planted repo-only, live-only, and both.

Planted repo-only and live-only MUST appear. A capability present in both
must NOT appear in either gap list. Indexed repo-only (and index-only)
must be flagged selectable-but-unloadable.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]


def load_presence_gap():
    path = REPO / "tools" / "router" / "presence_gap.py"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("imp0401_presence_gap", path)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise ImportError(f"cannot load {path}")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


def main() -> int:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
    import fixtures as F  # noqa: E402

    pg = load_presence_gap()
    report = pg.detect(
        F.fixture_repo_roots(),
        F.fixture_live_roots(),
        index=F.INDEX_PATH,
    )
    if not report["disjoint"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401-probe1 repo_only and live_only overlap")
        print(json.dumps(report["overlap_illegal"]))
        return 1

    repo_only = set(report["repo_only"])
    live_only = set(report["live_only"])
    both = set(report["both"])

    if F.PLANTED_REPO_ONLY not in repo_only:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0401-probe1 planted repo-only missing: {sorted(repo_only)}")
        return 1
    if F.PLANTED_LIVE_ONLY not in live_only:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0401-probe1 planted live-only missing: {sorted(live_only)}")
        return 1

        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0401-probe1 both-present leaked into a gap list both={sorted(both)}")
        return 1

        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0401-probe1 both-present not classified both={sorted(both)}")
        return 1
    if repo_only != {F.PLANTED_REPO_ONLY}:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0401-probe1 unexpected repo_only={sorted(repo_only)}")
        return 1

        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0401-probe1 unexpected live_only={sorted(live_only)}")
        return 1

    indexed = report["indexed"]
    if F.PLANTED_REPO_ONLY not in indexed["repo_only"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401-probe1 indexed repo-only missing")
        return 1
    if F.PLANTED_LIVE_ONLY not in indexed["live_only"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401-probe1 indexed live-only missing")
        return 1
    if F.PLANTED_BOTH in indexed["repo_only"] or F.PLANTED_BOTH in indexed["live_only"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401-probe1 indexed both leaked into a gap list")
        return 1
    unloadable = set(indexed["selectable_but_unloadable"])
    if F.PLANTED_REPO_ONLY not in unloadable:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401-probe1 repo-only not flagged selectable-but-unloadable")
        return 1
    if F.PLANTED_INDEX_ONLY not in unloadable:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401-probe1 index-only not flagged selectable-but-unloadable")
        return 1
    if F.PLANTED_BOTH in unloadable or F.PLANTED_LIVE_ONLY in unloadable:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401-probe1 loadable id flagged unloadable")
        return 1

        r["capability_id"]: r for r in report["annotated_index"]["capabilities"]
    }
    if by_id[F.PLANTED_REPO_ONLY]["router_defect"] != pg.DEFECT_UNLOADABLE:

        print("FAILED:IMP-0401-probe1 annotated both carries a defect")
        return 1
    if by_id[F.PLANTED_BOTH]["loadable"] is not True:

--- probe2_independent_setdiff.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: independent set difference, plus one-directional mutant.

Mechanically different from probe 1:
  * Probe 1 calls detect() which parses SKILL.md frontmatter.
  * This probe enumerates capability_id as skill:<parent-dir> via rglob
    of SKILL.md filenames only — no frontmatter parse, no detect().

The two sets must agree. A one-directional (repo minus live) helper must
miss the planted live-only id, proving the bidirectional detector is the
thing that closes failure class one-directional-comparison.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]


def load_presence_gap():
    path = REPO / "tools" / "router" / "presence_gap.py"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("imp0401_presence_gap", path)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise ImportError(f"cannot load {path}")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


def dir_name_ids(root: Path) -> set[str]:
    """Independent enumerator: directory name of each SKILL.md, no YAML."""
    ids: set[str] = set()
    if not root.is_dir():
        return ids
    for p in root.rglob("SKILL.md"):
        if p.is_file():
            ids.add("skill:" + p.parent.name)
    return ids


def main() -> int:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
    import fixtures as F  # noqa: E402

    pg = load_presence_gap()

    repo_ids: set[str] = set()
    live_ids: set[str] = set()
    for root in F.fixture_repo_roots():
        repo_ids |= dir_name_ids(root)
    for root in F.fixture_live_roots():
        live_ids |= dir_name_ids(root)

    indep_repo_only = sorted(repo_ids - live_ids)
    indep_live_only = sorted(live_ids - repo_ids)
    indep_both = sorted(repo_ids & live_ids)

    if F.PLANTED_REPO_ONLY not in indep_repo_only:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0401-probe2 independent repo-only missing {indep_repo_only}")
        return 1
    if F.PLANTED_LIVE_ONLY not in indep_live_only:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0401-probe2 independent live-only missing {indep_live_only}")
        return 1
    if F.PLANTED_BOTH in indep_repo_only or F.PLANTED_BOTH in indep_live_only:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401-probe2 independent both leaked into a gap list")
        return 1
    if set(indep_repo_only) & set(indep_live_only):
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401-probe2 independent lists not disjoint")
        return 1

    # Compare against detect() — disagreement is BLOCKED, not a picked winner.
    report = pg.detect(F.fixture_repo_roots(), F.fixture_live_roots(), index=F.INDEX_PATH)
    if indep_repo_only != report["repo_only"] or indep_live_only != report["live_only"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401-probe2 independent set-diff disagrees with detect()")
        print(json.dumps({"independent": {"repo_only": indep_repo_only, "live_only": indep_live_only}, "detect": {"repo_only": report["repo_only"], "live_only": report["live_only"]}}))
        return 1
    if indep_both != report["both"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401-probe2 independent both disagrees with detect()")
        return 1

    one_way = pg.one_directional_repo_minus_live(repo_ids, live_ids)
    if F.PLANTED_LIVE_ONLY in one_way:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401-probe2 one-directional mutant unexpectedly saw live-only")
        return 1
    if F.PLANTED_LIVE_ONLY not in indep_live_only:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401-probe2 live-only vanished before mutant check")
        return 1
    if F.PLANTED_REPO_ONLY not in one_way:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0401-probe2 one-directional mutant missed repo-only")
        return 1

    payload = {
        "probe": "probe2-independent-setdiff",
        "unit_id": "IMP-0401",
        "pass": True,
        "independent_method": "rglob-SKILL.md-parent-directory-name",

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: CLI contract — every flag documented in `ast route --help` parses.

Negative control: an undocumented flag (including --inject) must be rejected.
The CLI must call the same route_query the router exposes.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import importlib.util
import io
import json
import re
import sys
from contextlib import redirect_stderr, redirect_stdout
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "tools"))

from router.route import route_query  # noqa: E402

INDEX = (
    HERE / "fixtures" / "capability-index.json"
).relative_to(REPO).as_posix()
LONG_OPT = re.compile(r"(?<!\w)--[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*")
VALUE_FLAGS = {"--query", "--index", "--cwd"}
FLAG_VALUES = {
    "--query": "lint a Python module for unused imports",
    "--index": INDEX,
    "--cwd": ".",
}


def load_cli():
    path = REPO / "tools" / "ast.py"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("imp0403_ast_cli", path)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise RuntimeError(f"cannot load {path}")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


def help_text(mod) -> str:
    parser = mod.build_parser()
    buf = io.StringIO()
    parser.parse_args(["route", "--help"])  # should SystemExit
    return buf.getvalue()


def route_help(mod) -> str:
    parser = mod.build_parser()
    buf = io.StringIO()
    try:
        with redirect_stdout(buf), redirect_stderr(buf):
            parser.parse_args(["route", "--help"])
    except SystemExit as exc:
        if int(exc.code or 0) != 0:
            raise AssertionError(f"route --help exited {exc.code}") from exc
    return buf.getvalue()


def documented_long_flags(text: str) -> list[str]:
    flags = []
    for m in LONG_OPT.finditer(text):
        flag = m.group(0)
        if flag not in flags:
            flags.append(flag)
    return flags


def parse_ok(parser: argparse.ArgumentParser, argv: list[str]) -> None:
    buf = io.StringIO()
    try:
        with redirect_stdout(buf), redirect_stderr(buf):
            parser.parse_args(argv)
    except SystemExit as exc:
        raise AssertionError(
            f"parse_args({argv!r}) exited {exc.code}: {buf.getvalue()!r}"
        ) from exc


def parse_exits(parser: argparse.ArgumentParser, argv: list[str]) -> int:
    buf = io.StringIO()
    try:
        with redirect_stdout(buf), redirect_stderr(buf):
            parser.parse_args(argv)
    except SystemExit as exc:
        return int(exc.code or 0)
    raise AssertionError(f"parse_args({argv!r}) was expected to SystemExit")


def main() -> int:
    mod = load_cli()
    parser = mod.build_parser()
    text = route_help(mod)
    if "without injecting" not in text.lower() and "WITHOUT injecting" not in text:

--- probe2_doc_example.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: documentation command test.

An independent reviewer running only the documented help examples (nothing
else) must see exit 0 and structured stdout, including one no-fit query.
Bodies/canaries must be absent — the CLI does not inject.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import io
import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from contextlib import redirect_stderr, redirect_stdout
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
CANARY = "CANARY-IMP-0403-BODY"
EXAMPLE_RE = re.compile(r"^python3 tools/ast\.py route\b")


def load_cli():
    path = REPO / "tools" / "ast.py"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("imp0403_ast_cli", path)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise RuntimeError(f"cannot load {path}")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


def route_help(mod) -> str:
    parser = mod.build_parser()
    buf = io.StringIO()
    try:
        with redirect_stdout(buf), redirect_stderr(buf):
            parser.parse_args(["route", "--help"])
    except SystemExit as exc:
        if int(exc.code or 0) != 0:
            raise AssertionError(f"route --help exited {exc.code}") from exc
    return buf.getvalue()


def examples_from_help(text: str) -> list[str]:
    found: list[str] = []
    for line in text.splitlines():
        s = line.strip()
        if EXAMPLE_RE.match(s):
            found.append(s)
    return found


def run_example(cmd: str) -> tuple[int, str, str]:
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
    p = subprocess.run(
        cmd,
        cwd=str(REPO),
        shell=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        env=env,
    )
    return p.returncode, p.stdout or "", p.stderr or ""


def main() -> int:
    mod = load_cli()
    help_text = route_help(mod)
    examples = examples_from_help(help_text)
    if len(examples) < 3:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0403-probe2 expected >=3 documented examples, got", examples)
        return 1
    # Reviewer requirement: run ONLY the documented examples.
    transcripts = []
    verdicts = []
    for cmd in examples:
        code, out, err = run_example(cmd)
        blob = out + err
        if code != 0:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0403-probe2 documented example exit", code, "cmd=", cmd)
            print(blob[-2000:])
            return 1
        if CANARY in blob:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0403-probe2 canary/body leaked for", cmd)
            return 1
        try:
            doc = json.loads(out)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            print("FAILED:IMP-0403-probe2 stdout is not JSON for", cmd, exc)
            print(out[:2000])
            return 1
        if not isinstance(doc, dict):
            print("FAILED:IMP-0403-probe2 JSON is not an object")
            return 1

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/IMP-0410.json for the advisory file-lease manager."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import tempfile
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import jsonschema

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import scenario as S  # noqa: E402

OUT = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0410.json"
SCHEMA = "command-center/schema/file-lease.schema.json"
SENTINEL = "SENTINEL:IMP-0410:LEASES-ADVISORY-LOGGED"
QUESTION = (
    "Implement path leases so a session can declare the files it will write, "
    "renew while working, and release on exit. Exact target: a lease must be "
    "advisory and never block a write, but every write to a leased path by a "
    "non-holder must produce a logged conflict event."
)
P1 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0410/probe1_expiry_renew.py"
)
P2 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0410/probe2_advisory_negative.py"
)


def test_row(src: dict, extra: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    row = {
        "id": src["id"],
        "result": src["result"],
        "pass": bool(src["pass"]),
    }
    if extra:
        row.update(extra)
    for k, v in src.items():
        if k in {"id", "result", "pass", "event_log_excerpt"}:
            continue
        if k not in row:
            row[k] = v
    return row


def main() -> int:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0410-emit-") as td:
        tmp = Path(td)
        rows = S.run_all(tmp)
    failed = [k for k, v in rows.items() if not v.get("pass")]
    if failed:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0410 scenarios {failed}")
        print(json.dumps({k: rows[k] for k in failed}, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
        return 1

    excerpt = list(rows["advisory"].get("event_log_excerpt") or [])
    if not excerpt:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0410 no conflict excerpt")
        return 1

    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    doc = {
        "unit_id": "IMP-0410",
        "state": "SUCCESS",
        "sentinel": SENTINEL,
        "report_kind": "file_lease",
        "schema_version": 1,
        "title": "Advisory file-lease manager with renew and expiry",
        "question": QUESTION,
        "answer": (
            "FileLeaseManager in command-center/leases/manager.py stores per-session "
            "path leases as lease-<id>.json via tmp-plus-rename. acquire/renew/release "
            "are the session lifecycle. note_write always writes destination bytes and "
            "never sets refused=true; when the writer is not among active holders of "
            "that exact path it appends a lease-conflict event with write_allowed and "
            "write_succeeded both true. Expired and released leases drop out of "
            "active_holders so they stop generating conflicts. Unleased paths produce "
            "an empty conflict list. Overlap (globs, case folding, directory "
            "containment) is out of scope (IMP-0411)."
        ),
        "policy": {
            "advisory": True,
            "writes_never_refused": True,
            "non_holder_conflict_logged": True,
            "unleased_silent": True,
            "overlap_algorithm": "exact-normalized-path (IMP-0411 owns glob/case/dir)",
        },
        "manager": {
            "module": "command-center/leases/manager.py",
            "store": "command-center/leases/",
            "acquire": "declare session_id + paths + ttl; concurrent holders allowed",
            "renew": "holder-only; expires_at = now + ttl; refused after expiry",
            "release": "holder-only; status=released; subsequent writes are silent",
            "note_write": "always ok; log lease-conflict per foreign active holder",

--- probe1_expiry_renew.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: lease expiry and renew-extends.

Expiry: a non-holder write during TTL logs a conflict; the same write after
TTL does not. Renew: extending the lease keeps conflict logging past the
original expiry; an unrenewed twin does not.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import scenario as S  # noqa: E402

RECEIPT = S.REPO / "command-center" / "receipts" / "IMP-0410.json"


def check_receipt() -> str | None:
    if not RECEIPT.is_file():
        return None
    doc = json.loads(RECEIPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if str(doc.get("state") or "").upper() in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
        return f"receipt self-declared {doc.get('state')}"
    if "SENTINEL:IMP-0410:LEASES-ADVISORY-LOGGED" not in RECEIPT.read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    ):
        return "sentinel-absent"
    tests = doc.get("tests") or {}
    for key in ("expiry", "renew_extends"):
        row = tests.get(key) or {}
        if row.get("pass") is not True or row.get("result") != "PASS":
            return f"receipt test {key} not PASS"
    return None


def main() -> int:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0410-p1-") as td:
        tmp = Path(td)
        expiry = S.run_expiry(tmp)
        renew = S.run_renew_extends(tmp)
    print(json.dumps({"expiry": expiry, "renew_extends": renew}, sort_keys=True))
    print(
        f"expiry pass={expiry['pass']} mid_conflict={expiry['before_expiry_conflict']} "
        f"post_conflict={expiry['after_expiry_conflict']} refused_mid={expiry['before_expiry_refused']}"
    )
    print(
        f"renew pass={renew['pass']} late_conflict={renew['late_write_conflict']} "
        f"control_conflict={renew['unrenewed_control_conflict']} "
        f"expires={renew['renewed_expires_at']}"
    )
    if not expiry["pass"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0410-probe1 expiry")
        return 1
    if not renew["pass"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0410-probe1 renew-extends")
        return 1
    rec_err = check_receipt()
    if rec_err:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0410-probe1 receipt {rec_err}")
        return 1
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0410-probe1 expiry and renew-extends")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe2_advisory_negative.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: advisory writes and unleased negative control.

Advisory: a non-holder write to a leased path is never refused and still
produces a conflict event. Negative control: a write to an unleased path
produces no conflict event. A blocking mutant of note_write must fail the
advisory assertion (both-states).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import scenario as S  # noqa: E402

RECEIPT = S.REPO / "command-center" / "receipts" / "IMP-0410.json"


def check_receipt() -> str | None:
    if not RECEIPT.is_file():
        return None
    text = RECEIPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    doc = json.loads(text)
    if str(doc.get("state") or "").upper() in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
        return f"receipt self-declared {doc.get('state')}"
    if "SENTINEL:IMP-0410:LEASES-ADVISORY-LOGGED" not in text:
        return "sentinel-absent"
    excerpt = doc.get("event_log_excerpt") or []
    if not excerpt:
        return "event-log-excerpt-absent"
    for ev in excerpt:
        if ev.get("event") != "lease-conflict":
            return "excerpt-not-conflict"
        if ev.get("write_allowed") is not True or ev.get("write_succeeded") is not True:
            return "excerpt-write-not-advisory"
        if ev.get("writer_session") == ev.get("holder_session"):
            return "excerpt-not-non-holder"
    tests = doc.get("tests") or {}
    for key in ("advisory", "unleased_negative_control"):
        row = tests.get(key) or {}
        if row.get("pass") is not True:
            return f"receipt test {key} not PASS"
    policy = doc.get("policy") or {}
    if policy.get("advisory") is not True or policy.get("writes_never_refused") is not True:
        return "policy-not-advisory"
    return None


def main() -> int:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0410-p2-") as td:
        tmp = Path(td)
        advisory = S.run_advisory(tmp)
        unleased = S.run_unleased_negative(tmp)
        mutant = S.blocking_mutant_refuses(tmp)
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {"advisory": advisory, "unleased": unleased, "mutant": mutant},
            sort_keys=True,
        )
    )
    print(
        f"advisory pass={advisory['pass']} refused={advisory['refused']} "
        f"conflict={advisory['conflict']} on_disk={advisory['on_disk']!r}"
    )
    print(
        f"unleased pass={unleased['pass']} conflict_count={unleased['conflict_event_count']} "
        f"refused={unleased['refused']}"
    )
    print(
        f"mutant pass={mutant['pass']} live_refused={mutant['live_refused']} "
        f"mutant_refused={mutant['mutant_refused']}"
    )
    if not advisory["pass"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0410-probe2 advisory")
        return 1
    if not unleased["pass"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0410-probe2 unleased-negative-control")
        return 1
    if not mutant["pass"]:
        print("FAILED:IMP-0410-probe2 blocking-mutant-did-not-discriminate")
        return 1
    rec_err = check_receipt()
    if rec_err:
        print(f"FAILED:IMP-0410-probe2 receipt {rec_err}")
        return 1
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0410-probe2 advisory and unleased negative control")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- scenario.py ---
"""Shared IMP-0410 scenarios: expiry, renew-extends, advisory, unleased control."""
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
MANAGER_PY = REPO / "command-center" / "leases" / "manager.py"


def load_manager():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("imp0410_leases_manager", MANAGER_PY)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise ImportError(f"cannot load {MANAGER_PY}")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules["imp0410_leases_manager"] = mod
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


def _mgr(mod, tmp: Path, clock):
    store = tmp / "lease-store"
    store.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return mod.FileLeaseManager(store, clock=clock.now), tmp / "workspace"


def run_expiry(tmp: Path) -> dict:
    """After TTL, a non-holder write no longer logs a conflict; before TTL it does."""
    mod = load_manager()
    clock = mod.FakeClock(1000.0)
    mgr, ws = _mgr(mod, tmp / "expiry", clock)
    leased = "src/leased.txt"
    lease = mgr.acquire("sess-A", [leased], ttl_seconds=10.0)
    clock.advance(5.0)
    mid = mgr.note_write(
        "sess-B",
        leased,
        payload=b"intruder-before-expiry",
        dest=ws / leased,
    )
    clock.advance(6.0)
    post = mgr.note_write(
        "sess-C",
        leased,
        payload=b"after-expiry",
        dest=ws / leased,
    )
    mid_ok = (
        mid["ok"]
        and not mid["refused"]
        and mid["conflict"]
        and (ws / leased).read_bytes() == b"after-expiry"
    )
    post_ok = post["ok"] and not post["refused"] and not post["conflict"]
    passed = mid_ok and post_ok and lease["expires_at"] == 1010.0
    return {
        "id": "expiry",
        "pass": passed,
        "result": "PASS" if passed else "FAIL",
        "lease_id": lease["lease_id"],
        "before_expiry_conflict": mid["conflict"],
        "before_expiry_refused": mid["refused"],
        "after_expiry_conflict": post["conflict"],
        "after_expiry_refused": post["refused"],
        "write_succeeded_after_expiry": post["write_succeeded"],
        "on_disk_after": (ws / leased).read_bytes().decode("utf-8"),
    }


def run_renew_extends(tmp: Path) -> dict:
    """Renew moves expiry past the original TTL so a late non-holder still logs."""
    mod = load_manager()
    clock = mod.FakeClock(2000.0)
    mgr, ws = _mgr(mod, tmp / "renew", clock)
    path = "src/renewed.txt"
    lease = mgr.acquire("sess-A", [path], ttl_seconds=10.0)
    clock.advance(8.0)
    renewed = mgr.renew("sess-A", lease["lease_id"])
    clock.advance(5.0)
    late = mgr.note_write(
        "sess-B",
        path,
        payload=b"still-leased",
        dest=ws / path,
    )
    clock2 = mod.FakeClock(2000.0)
    mgr2, ws2 = _mgr(mod, tmp / "renew-control", clock2)
    mgr2.acquire("sess-A", [path], ttl_seconds=10.0)
    clock2.advance(13.0)
    control = mgr2.note_write(
        "sess-B",
        path,
        payload=b"unrenewed-expired",
        dest=ws2 / path,
    )
    passed = (
        bool(renewed.get("ok"))
        and float(renewed["expires_at"]) == 2018.0

IMP-0420
--- emit_receipt.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/IMP-0420.json from a live adapter dispatch.

The catalog artifact is a typed work-result whose evidence payload is the
read-only-unit Write refusal the row names, plus the timeout both-states
measurement. Values are taken from ClaudeAdapter.dispatch, not asserted.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import sys
import tempfile
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import jsonschema

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
from load_adapter import ADAPTER_PATH, REPO, load_adapter  # noqa: E402

OUT_REL = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0420.json"
SCHEMA_REL = "command-center/schema/work-result.schema.json"
SENTINEL = "SENTINEL:IMP-0420:CLAUDE-ADAPTER-ENFORCING"
CATALOG_TIMEOUT = 2700


def _sha(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def _art(rel: str, role: str) -> dict:
    path = REPO / rel
    sha, size = _sha(path)
    return {
        "path": rel,
        "bytes": size,
        "sha256": sha,
        "exists": path.is_file(),
        "role": role,
    }


def build() -> dict:
    ca = load_adapter()
    adapter = ca.ClaudeAdapter()
    started = datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(microsecond=0).isoformat()
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0420-receipt-") as td:
        root = Path(td)
        marker = root / "marker.txt"
        ro_unit = {
            "id": "IMP-0420-EVIDENCE-READONLY",
            "tool_permissions": ["Read"],
            "timeout_seconds": 8,
        }
        evidence = adapter.dispatch(
            ro_unit,
            lambda tools: tools.Write("marker.txt", "planted-by-readonly-unit"),
            sandbox_root=root,
            sentinel="SENTINEL:IMP-0420:WRITE-REFUSED",
        )
        file_absent = not marker.exists()

        short = adapter.dispatch(
            {
                "id": "IMP-0420-EVIDENCE-TIMEOUT-SHORT",
                "tool_permissions": ["Read"],
                "timeout_seconds": 0.25,
            },
            lambda tools: time.sleep(0.9),
            sandbox_root=root,
        )
        long = adapter.dispatch(
            {
                "id": "IMP-0420-EVIDENCE-TIMEOUT-LONG",
                "tool_permissions": ["Read"],
                "timeout_seconds": 2.0,
            },
            lambda tools: time.sleep(0.05) or "ok",
            sandbox_root=root,
        )
        missing = adapter.dispatch(
            {"id": "IMP-0420-EVIDENCE-TIMEOUT-MISSING", "tool_permissions": ["Read"]},
            lambda tools: "should-not-run",
            sandbox_root=root,
        )

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    refusals = [
        e
        for e in evidence["dispatch_trace"]
        if e.get("event") == "tool-refused" and e.get("tool") == "Write"
    ]
    if not refusals or not file_absent or evidence["result"]["outcome"] != "permission-denied":
        raise SystemExit(

--- probe1_permission_boundary.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""IMP-0420 probe1: permission-boundary test per permission class.

Evidence required by the catalog: a unit declaring read-only permissions attempts
a write and is refused by the adapter, with the refusal recorded. Two independent
signals: (a) the typed refusal in the dispatch trace, (b) the marker file is
absent. A mutant that disables the allowlist must actually write, otherwise the
refusal arm measures a broken Write path.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
from load_adapter import load_adapter  # noqa: E402

VOCAB = ("Read", "Grep", "Glob", "Write", "Edit", "Bash", "PowerShell")


def _unit(uid: str, perms: list[str], timeout: float = 8.0) -> dict:
    return {"id": uid, "tool_permissions": perms, "timeout_seconds": timeout}


def _call(tools, tool: str) -> None:
    if tool == "Read":
        tools.Read("seed.txt")
    elif tool == "Grep":
        tools.Grep("token", "seed.txt")
    elif tool == "Glob":
        tools.Glob("seed.txt")
    elif tool == "Write":
        tools.Write("marker.txt", "SHOULD-NOT-OR-SHOULD")
    elif tool == "Edit":
        tools.Edit("seed.txt", "edited")
    elif tool == "Bash":
        tools.Bash("echo granted-or-denied")
    elif tool == "PowerShell":
        tools.PowerShell("Write-Output 1")
    else:
        raise AssertionError(tool)


def _seed(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "seed.txt").write_text("token-line\n", encoding="utf-8")


def _complement(tool: str) -> list[str]:
    if tool in {"Read", "Grep", "Glob"}:
        return ["Write"]
    return ["Read"]


def main() -> int:
    ca = load_adapter()
    adapter = ca.ClaudeAdapter()
    failures: list[str] = []
    matrix: list[dict] = []

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0420-p1-") as td:
        base = Path(td)

        # --- evidence: read-only unit attempts Write ---
        ro_root = base / "readonly"
        ro_root.mkdir()
        marker = ro_root / "marker.txt"
        ro_unit = _unit("IMP-0420-EVIDENCE-READONLY", ["Read"])

        def _attempt_write(tools):
            return tools.Write("marker.txt", "planted-by-readonly-unit")

        refused = adapter.dispatch(
            ro_unit,
            _attempt_write,
            sandbox_root=ro_root,
            sentinel="SENTINEL:IMP-0420:WRITE-REFUSED",
        )
        refusals = [
            e
            for e in refused["dispatch_trace"]
            if e.get("event") == "tool-refused" and e.get("tool") == "Write"
        ]
        write_absent = not marker.exists()
        bound_excludes_write = "Write" not in refused["bound_tools"]
        if refused["result"]["outcome"] != "permission-denied":
            failures.append(f"readonly outcome={refused['result']['outcome']}")
        if not refusals:
            failures.append("readonly missing tool-refused Write in dispatch_trace")
        if not write_absent:
            failures.append("readonly Write produced a file — permissions documented, not enforced")
        if not bound_excludes_write:
            failures.append(f"readonly bound_tools still includes Write: {refused['bound_tools']}")

        # --- mutant control: same Write must succeed if allowlist is a no-op ---
        mut_root = base / "mutant"
        mut_root.mkdir()

--- probe2_timeout_applied.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""IMP-0420 probe2: declared timeout is the one applied, not a catalog default.

Both-states: the same subagent body times out under a short declared deadline and
completes under a longer declared deadline. A missing timeout_seconds is refused
rather than filled with the catalog default (1800). Source walk confirms
resolve_timeout_seconds has no get-with-default / `or 1800` fallback.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import ast
import json
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
from load_adapter import ADAPTER_PATH, load_adapter  # noqa: E402


def _unit(uid: str, timeout) -> dict:
    body = {"id": uid, "tool_permissions": ["Read"]}
    if timeout is not Ellipsis:
        body["timeout_seconds"] = timeout
    return body


def _sleeper(seconds: float):
    def _run(tools):
        time.sleep(seconds)
        return tools.Read("seed.txt")

    return _run


def _timeout_fn_source_ok(src: str) -> tuple[bool, list[str]]:
    """Refuse get-with-default or a catalog-default constant inside resolve_timeout_seconds."""
    problems: list[str] = []
    tree = ast.parse(src)
    fn = None
    for node in tree.body:
        if isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef) and node.name == "resolve_timeout_seconds":
            fn = node
            break
    if fn is None:
        return False, ["resolve_timeout_seconds missing"]
    for node in ast.walk(fn):
        if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and isinstance(node.func, ast.Attribute):
            if node.func.attr == "get" and len(node.args) >= 2:
                key = node.args[0]
                if isinstance(key, ast.Constant) and key.value == "timeout_seconds":
                    problems.append("dict.get(timeout_seconds, default) fallback")
        if isinstance(node, ast.Constant) and node.value == 1800:
            problems.append("literal 1800 inside resolve_timeout_seconds")
        if isinstance(node, ast.Name) and node.id == "CATALOG_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS":
            problems.append("catalog default referenced inside resolve_timeout_seconds")
    return not problems, problems


def main() -> int:
    ca = load_adapter()
    adapter = ca.ClaudeAdapter()
    failures: list[str] = []
    src = ADAPTER_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    ast_ok, ast_problems = _timeout_fn_source_ok(src)
    if not ast_ok:
        failures.extend(ast_problems)

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0420-p2-") as td:
        root = Path(td)
        (root / "seed.txt").write_text("ok\n", encoding="utf-8")

        short = adapter.dispatch(
            _unit("FIX-TIMEOUT-SHORT", 0.25),
            _sleeper(0.9),
            sandbox_root=root,
        )
        long = adapter.dispatch(
            _unit("FIX-TIMEOUT-LONG", 2.0),
            _sleeper(0.05),
            sandbox_root=root,
        )
        missing = adapter.dispatch(
            _unit("FIX-TIMEOUT-MISSING", Ellipsis),
            _sleeper(0.01),
            sandbox_root=root,
        )
        # Explicit catalog-default-shaped value must still be recorded as the UNIT value.
        eighteen = adapter.dispatch(
            _unit("FIX-TIMEOUT-DECLARED-1800", 1800),
            _sleeper(0.01),
            sandbox_root=root,
        )

    if short["state"] != "TIMEOUT":
        failures.append(
            f"short declared timeout did not fire (state={short['state']} "
            f"outcome={short['result']['outcome']}); default may have been applied"
