IMP-0432
--- emit_receipt.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit IMP-0432 receipt plus per-unit SUBAGENT-RETURN / UNIT-ARTIFACT-MANIFEST.

Runs live fixture worktrees, writes command-center/receipts/IMP-0432.json,
re-runs named probes, then writes the per-unit G-1 files. Does not run
tools/command-center/unit_manifest.py --write.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
from scenario import build_cases, evidence_table  # noqa: E402

UNIT = "IMP-0432"
OUT_REL = "command-center/receipts/IMP-0432.json"
SENTINEL = "SENTINEL:IMP-0432:WORKTREE-REPO-ASSERTED"
PIN_REL = "command-center/config/pinned-repo-origin.json"
GUARD_REL = "tools/command-center/worktree_origin_guard.py"
P1 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0432/probe1_both_states.py"
)
P2 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0432/probe2_fail_closed.py"
)
SUPPORTING = [
    GUARD_REL,
    PIN_REL,
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0432/scenario.py",
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0432/probe1_both_states.py",
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0432/probe2_fail_closed.py",
]


def digest(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def art(rel: str, role: str) -> dict:
    path = REPO / rel
    sha, size = digest(path)
    return {
        "path": rel,
        "sha256": sha,
        "bytes": size,
        "exists": path.is_file(),
        "present": path.is_file(),
        "role": role,
        "eol": "LF",
        "unit_id": UNIT,
        "receipt_path": "command-center/p2-wave/probes/IMP-0432/SUBAGENT-RETURN.json",
    }


def fp(method: str, command: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(f"{method}\x1f{command}".encode()).hexdigest()[:32]


def probe(role: str, method: str, command: str, raw: str, exit_code: int | None = 0) -> dict:
    d = {
        "role": role,
        "method": method,
        "command": command,
        "raw_output": raw[:16000],
        "fingerprint": fp(method, command),
    }
    if exit_code is not None:
        d["exit_code"] = exit_code
    return d


def run_cmd(cmd: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
    p = subprocess.run(
        cmd,
        cwd=str(REPO),
        shell=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        encoding="utf-8",
        errors="replace",
        env=env,
        timeout=180,
    )
    text = ((p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or "")).strip()
    return p.returncode, text


--- probe1_both_states.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Named probe 1: both-states write (wrong-repo refuse, right-repo permit).

A discriminating guard must PERMIT the pinned origin and REFUSE a foreign
origin. A write that was refused must leave no new bytes (pre-seeded marker
intact). Path-string matching is not this probe's subject — see probe 2.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
from scenario import MARKER, PAYLOAD, build_cases  # noqa: E402

ART = REPO / "command-center/receipts/IMP-0432.json"
SENTINEL = "SENTINEL:IMP-0432:WORKTREE-REPO-ASSERTED"


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:IMP-0432-probe1 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    if not ART.is_file() or ART.stat().st_size < 200:
        return fail("receipt missing or too small")
    text = ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if SENTINEL not in text:
        return fail("sentinel absent")
    doc = json.loads(text)
    if doc.get("unit_id") != "IMP-0432":
        return fail(f"unit_id {doc.get('unit_id')}")
    st = str(doc.get("state") or "").upper()
    if st in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
        return fail(f"self-declared:{st}")

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0432-p1-") as tmp:
        rows = build_cases(Path(tmp))
        by_id = {r["id"]: r for r in rows}
        right = by_id["right-repo-https"]
        ssh = by_id["right-repo-ssh-alias"]
        wrong = by_id["wrong-repo"]

        if right["decision"] != "PERMIT" or not right["result"]["write_performed"]:
            return fail(f"right-repo did not permit write: {right['decision']} {right['reason']}")
        if right["result"].get("bytes_after") != PAYLOAD:
            return fail("right-repo write did not persist payload")
        if ssh["decision"] != "PERMIT" or not ssh["result"]["write_performed"]:
            return fail(f"ssh-alias of pin did not permit: {ssh['decision']} {ssh['reason']}")

        if wrong["decision"] != "REFUSE" or wrong["result"]["write_performed"]:
            return fail(
                f"wrong-repo did not refuse write: {wrong['decision']} "
                f"performed={wrong['result']['write_performed']}"
            )
        if wrong["reason"] != "WRONG_REPO":
            return fail(f"wrong-repo reason {wrong['reason']} want WRONG_REPO")
        if wrong["result"].get("bytes_after") != MARKER:
            return fail("wrong-repo write mutated or deleted the pre-seeded marker")
        if not wrong["marker_intact"]:
            return fail("wrong-repo marker not intact")

        cmd = wrong["git_command"] or []
        if "remote" not in cmd or "get-url" not in cmd:
            return fail(f"origin not resolved by git command: {cmd}")
        if wrong.get("path_string_used_as_origin"):
            return fail("guard used path string as origin")
        if right["decision"] == wrong["decision"]:
            return fail("both-states collapsed: right and wrong produced the same decision")

    table = doc.get("worktree_origin_table") or []
    if not any(r.get("reason") == "WRONG_REPO" and r.get("match_verdict") == "REFUSE" for r in table):
        return fail("receipt table lacks a wrong-repo REFUSE row")
    if not any(r.get("reason") == "MATCH" and r.get("match_verdict") == "PERMIT" for r in table):
        return fail("receipt table lacks a MATCH PERMIT row")

    summary = {
        "probe": "probe1-both-states",
        "unit_id": "IMP-0432",
        "pass": True,
        "right_repo_permitted": True,
        "ssh_alias_permitted": True,
        "wrong_repo_refused": True,
        "wrong_repo_marker_intact": True,
        "resolved_by_git_command": True,
        "right_origin": right["resolved_origin_url"],
        "wrong_origin": wrong["resolved_origin_url"],
    }
    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0432-probe1 both-states wrong-repo refuse / right-repo permit")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe2_fail_closed.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Named probe 2: missing origin fail-closed + origin resolved by git command.

A worktree with no origin must be refused, not defaulted to allowed.
A directory whose path contains the pinned repo name but whose origin is a
foreign URL must be refused — proving the guard does not trust path strings.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
from scenario import MARKER, build_cases, pin_doc, pinned_url  # noqa: E402

ART = REPO / "command-center/receipts/IMP-0432.json"
SENTINEL = "SENTINEL:IMP-0432:WORKTREE-REPO-ASSERTED"


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:IMP-0432-probe2 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    if not ART.is_file() or ART.stat().st_size < 200:
        return fail("receipt missing or too small")
    text = ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if SENTINEL not in text:
        return fail("sentinel absent")
    doc = json.loads(text)
    if str(doc.get("state") or "").upper() in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
        return fail(f"self-declared:{doc.get('state')}")

    pin = pin_doc()
    if pin.get("fail_closed_when_origin_missing") is not True:
        return fail("pin fail_closed_when_origin_missing is not true")

    token = Path(pinned_url().rstrip("/")).name.replace(".git", "")

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="imp0432-p2-") as tmp:
        rows = build_cases(Path(tmp))
        by_id = {r["id"]: r for r in rows}
        missing = by_id["missing-origin"]
        spoof = by_id["path-spoof-wrong-origin"]

        if missing["decision"] != "REFUSE" or missing["result"]["write_performed"]:
            return fail(
                f"missing-origin defaulted to allow: decision={missing['decision']} "
                f"performed={missing['result']['write_performed']}"
            )
        if missing["reason"] != "MISSING_ORIGIN":
            return fail(f"missing-origin reason {missing['reason']} want MISSING_ORIGIN")
        if missing["result"].get("bytes_after") != MARKER:
            return fail("missing-origin write mutated the pre-seeded marker")
        if not missing["naive_missing_would_allow"]:
            return fail("negative control naive_missing_origin_allow did not fire on the no-origin tree")

        if spoof["decision"] != "REFUSE" or spoof["result"]["write_performed"]:
            return fail(f"path-spoof was not refused: {spoof['decision']}")
        if not spoof["naive_path_would_permit"]:
            return fail("spoof path does not contain pin token; negative control is vacuous")
        if spoof.get("path_string_used_as_origin"):
            return fail("guard used path string as origin")
        cmd = spoof["git_command"] or []
        if "remote" not in cmd or "get-url" not in cmd:
            return fail(f"origin not resolved by git command: {cmd}")
        if spoof["resolved_origin_url"] is None:
            return fail("spoof tree origin was not resolved (need a foreign URL, not a miss)")
        if token.lower() in str(spoof["resolved_origin_url"]).lower() and "evil.example" not in str(
            spoof["resolved_origin_url"]
        ):
            return fail("spoof origin unexpectedly looks like the pin")

    table = doc.get("worktree_origin_table") or []
    if not any(r.get("reason") == "MISSING_ORIGIN" and r.get("match_verdict") == "REFUSE" for r in table):
        return fail("receipt table lacks a MISSING_ORIGIN REFUSE row")

    summary = {
        "probe": "probe2-fail-closed",
        "unit_id": "IMP-0432",
        "pass": True,
        "missing_origin_refused": True,
        "missing_origin_not_default_allow": True,
        "path_spoof_refused": True,
        "naive_path_matcher_would_permit_spoof": True,
        "naive_missing_would_allow": True,
        "resolved_by_git_command": True,
        "spoof_origin": spoof["resolved_origin_url"],
        "spoof_worktree": spoof["worktree"],
    }
    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
    print("SUCCESS:IMP-0432-probe2 missing-origin refuse and command-not-path")
    return 0



--- scenario.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Fixture worktrees for IMP-0432 origin-guard probes.

Creates isolated git repos whose origin is set (or omitted) by git command.
Directory names are chosen so a path-string matcher would get the wrong answer
on the spoof fixture.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
GUARD_DIR = REPO / "tools" / "command-center"
if str(GUARD_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(GUARD_DIR))

import worktree_origin_guard as G  # noqa: E402

PIN_REL = "command-center/config/pinned-repo-origin.json"
WRONG_ORIGIN = "https://evil.example.invalid/foreign-repo.git"
MARKER = b"PRE-EXISTING-MARKER:do-not-clobber\n"
PAYLOAD = b"IMP-0432-guarded-write-payload\n"


def pin_doc() -> dict:
    return G.load_pin(REPO / PIN_REL)


def pinned_url() -> str:
    return str(pin_doc()["pinned_origin_urls"][0])


def pinned_ssh_alias() -> str:
    """SSH form of the pin: same repo, different spelling. Must PERMIT."""
    url = pinned_url()
    # https://host/path.git -> git@host:path.git
    rest = url.split("://", 1)[1]
    host, _, path = rest.partition("/")
    return f"git@{host}:{path}"


def _git(cwd: Path, args: list[str]) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    return subprocess.run(
        ["git", *args],
        cwd=str(cwd),
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        encoding="utf-8",
        errors="replace",
        env=G.git_clean_env(),
        timeout=30,
        check=False,
    )


def make_worktree(root: Path, *, origin: str | None) -> Path:
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    init = _git(root, ["init"])
    if init.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"git init failed: {init.stderr or init.stdout}")
    _git(root, ["config", "user.email", "imp0432@example.invalid"])
    _git(root, ["config", "user.name", "IMP-0432 fixture"])
    if origin is not None:
        added = _git(root, ["remote", "add", "origin", origin])
        if added.returncode != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"git remote add failed: {added.stderr or added.stdout}")
    return root


def attempt_write(worktree: Path, rel: str = "scratch/guarded.txt", data: bytes = PAYLOAD) -> dict:
    target = worktree / rel
    result = G.guarded_write(target, data, pin_doc())
    result["exists_after"] = target.is_file()
    if target.is_file():
        result["bytes_after"] = target.read_bytes()
    else:
        result["bytes_after"] = b""
    return result


def naive_path_string_permit(worktree: Path, pin_token: str) -> bool:
    """Broken matcher: allows a write because the path contains the repo name.

    Probe 2 uses this as the negative control: it would PERMIT the spoof tree.
    The real guard must not.
    """
    return pin_token.lower() in str(worktree).lower()


def naive_missing_origin_allow(worktree: Path) -> bool:
    """Broken matcher: treats missing origin as allow. The real guard must not."""
    got = G.resolve_origin_url(worktree)
    return not got.get("ok")

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/REL-0002/fixtures/inventory.json"
)

    "10817 as a typed schema rather than prose. Each action an installer may "
    "perform -- write a file, register a hook, add an MCP entry, edit a "
    "settings key, create a scheduled task, seed a cache -- must declare its "

def sha256_file(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def dump(doc: object) -> str:
    return json.dumps(doc, indent=2, ensure_ascii=True) + "\n"

        print(f"FAILED:REL-0002 mutants silent m1={len(m1)} m2={len(m2)} m3={len(m3)}")
        return 1

--- probe1_negative_schema.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: catalog negative controls against the rollback-contract schema."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import inventory as I  # noqa: E402
import validator as V  # noqa: E402


def main() -> int:
    fail = False
    doc = I.build_inventory()
    validator = V.rollback_validator()
    clean = V.error_strings(validator, doc)
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "clean_errors": clean,
                "n_actions": len(doc["actions"]),
                "catalog_types": I.catalog_types_covered(doc),
            },
            sort_keys=True,
        )
    )
    if clean:
        print(f"FAILED:REL-0002-probe1 clean inventory invalid: {clean[:6]}")
        fail = True

    m1 = V.mutant_no_inverse_no_irreversible(doc)
    e1 = V.error_strings(validator, m1)
    print(f"no_inverse_no_IRREVERSIBLE errors={len(e1)} sample={e1[:3]}")
    if not e1:
        print("FAILED:REL-0002-probe1 missing inverse was accepted")
        fail = True

    m2 = V.mutant_inverse_outside_creation_set(doc)
    e2 = V.error_strings(validator, m2)
    print(f"inverse_outside_creation_set errors={len(e2)} sample={e2[:3]}")
    if not e2:
        print("FAILED:REL-0002-probe1 outsider inverse was accepted")
        fail = True
    if not any("creation set" in x or INV_HINT in x for x in e2):
        # still require the creation-set invariant to fire, not a random error
        if not any("predated" in x.lower() or "creation" in x.lower() for x in e2):
            print("FAILED:REL-0002-probe1 outsider inverse failed for the wrong reason")
            fail = True

    m3 = V.mutant_partial_omits_residue(doc)
    e3 = V.error_strings(validator, m3)
    print(f"partial_omits_residue errors={len(e3)} sample={e3[:3]}")
    if not e3:
        print("FAILED:REL-0002-probe1 partial inverse without residue was accepted")
        fail = True
    if not any("residue" in x.lower() for x in e3):
        print("FAILED:REL-0002-probe1 residue omission failed for the wrong reason")
        fail = True

    silent = V.error_strings(validator, doc)
    print(f"both_states clean_still_silent={silent == []}")
    if silent:
        print("FAILED:REL-0002-probe1 clean inventory no longer silent after mutants")
        fail = True

    if fail:
        print("FAILED:REL-0002-probe1 negative schema controls")
        return 1
    print(
        "SUCCESS:REL-0002-probe1 no-inverse, outsider-target, missing-residue "
        "all fail; clean inventory silent"
    )
    return 0


INV_HINT = V.INV_PATH


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: meta-schema, positive inventory, invariant file:line, receipt."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

import validator as V  # noqa: E402

RECEIPT = REPO / "command-center" / "receipts" / "REL-0002.json"
SENTINEL = "SUCCESS:REL-0002"


def main() -> int:
    fail = False
    rollback = V.rollback_schema()
    receipt_schema = V.receipt_schema()
    try:
        V.check_meta(rollback)
        print("rollback_schema check_schema=ok")
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"FAILED:REL-0002-probe2 rollback meta-schema: {exc}")
        fail = True
    try:
        V.check_meta(receipt_schema)
        print("receipt_schema check_schema=ok")
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"FAILED:REL-0002-probe2 receipt meta-schema: {exc}")
        fail = True

    if texts.get("INV-PREDATE") != V.INV_PREDATE:
        print("FAILED:REL-0002-probe2 INV-PREDATE text drift")

            print(f"FAILED:REL-0002-probe2 action {action['id']} xor broken")
            fail = True
        if has_inv:
            print(
                f"action {action['id']} type={action['action_type']} "
                f"inverse={action['inverse']['kind']} "
                f"completeness={action['inverse']['completeness']}"
            )
        else:
            print(
                f"action {action['id']} type={action['action_type']} "
                "IRREVERSIBLE"
            )

    errs = V.error_strings(V.rollback_validator(), doc)
    print(f"inventory_errors={errs}")
    if errs:
        print("FAILED:REL-0002-probe2 inventory does not validate")
        fail = True

    if not RECEIPT.is_file() or RECEIPT.stat().st_size <= 0:
        print("FAILED:REL-0002-probe2 receipt missing")
        fail = True
    else:
        receipt = json.loads(RECEIPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        rerr = V.error_strings(V.receipt_validator(), receipt)
        print(f"receipt_schema_errors={rerr}")
        if rerr:
            print("FAILED:REL-0002-probe2 receipt schema errors")
            fail = True
        body = RECEIPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if SENTINEL not in body:
            print("FAILED:REL-0002-probe2 sentinel absent from receipt")
            fail = True
        if receipt.get("state") in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
            print(f"FAILED:REL-0002-probe2 self-declared {receipt.get('state')}")
            fail = True
        quoted = receipt.get("contract", {}).get("named_invariants") or []
        by_id = {q.get("id"): q for q in quoted}
        for row in quotes:
            hit = by_id.get(row["id"]) or {}
            if hit.get("line") != row["line"] or hit.get("text") != row["text"]:

RES-0060
--- emit.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/research/RES-0060.json from live captures + local contract."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import common as C  # noqa: E402

QUESTION = (
    "What signals drive discovery ranking in mature extension marketplaces, "
    "how much of ranking is install-count feedback (a rich-get-richer loop), "
    "and what would make a genuinely useful but new package discoverable in "
    "this program's index?"
)


def rail(cid: str, kind: str, quoted_span: str) -> dict:
    row = C.capture_row(cid)
    path = C.REPO / str(row["path"]).replace("\\", "/")
    sha, size = C.digest_file(path)
    return {
        "kind": kind,
        "capture_id": cid,
        "permalink": row["url"],
        "fetched_at": row["fetched_at"],
        "http_status": row["http_status"],
        "sha256": sha,
        "bytes": size,
        "path": row["path"],
        "quoted_span": quoted_span,
    }


def local_rail(kind: str, path: str, quoted_span: str, *, note: str) -> dict:
    p = C.REPO / path
    sha, size = C.digest_file(p)
    return {
        "kind": kind,
        "capture_id": None,
        "permalink": f"file://{path}",
        "fetched_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
        "http_status": 200,
        "sha256": sha,
        "bytes": size,
        "path": path,
        "quoted_span": quoted_span,
        "note": note,
    }


def factor(
    fid: str,
    marketplace: str,
    name: str,
    evidence_class: str,
    role: str,
    install_feedback: str,
    used_in_default: bool,
    evidence_tier: str,
    note: str,
) -> dict:
    return {
        "id": fid,
        "marketplace": marketplace,
        "factor": name,
        "evidence_class": evidence_class,
        "role": role,
        "install_count_feedback": install_feedback,
        "used_in_default_ranking": used_in_default,
        "evidence_tier": evidence_tier,
        "note": note,
    }


def ensure_program_fixture() -> None:
    """Materialize IMP-0118's known-good registry if this worktree has no index yet."""
    path = C.REPO / "tools/marketplace/fixture/registry/index.json"
    if path.is_file() and path.stat().st_size > 0:
        return
    if str(C.REPO) not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, str(C.REPO))
    from tools.marketplace.fixture import build  # noqa: WPS433

    build(C.REPO)


def main() -> int:
    ensure_program_fixture()
    vs_rel = C.vscode_ranked_ids("vscode-api-sort-relevance")
    vs_ins = C.vscode_ranked_ids("vscode-api-sort-installs")
    vs_rel_n = C.vscode_installs("vscode-api-sort-relevance")
    vs_ins_n = C.vscode_installs("vscode-api-sort-installs")
    if vs_rel == vs_ins:
        print("FAILED:RES-0060 emit relevance order equals install order")
        return 1

--- probe1_documented_inferred_split.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: ranking-factor list is split documented-versus-inferred; proportion matches."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import common as C  # noqa: E402

ALLOWED = {"documented", "inferred"}


def main() -> int:
    art_path = C.REPO / C.OUT_REL
    if not art_path.is_file():
        print("FAILED:RES-0060-probe1 artifact missing")
        return 1
    text = art_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    doc = json.loads(text)
    if doc.get("state") in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
        print(f"FAILED:RES-0060-probe1 self-declared {doc.get('state')}")
        return 1
    if C.SENTINEL not in text:
        print("FAILED:RES-0060-probe1 sentinel absent")
        return 1
    if not doc.get("ranking_factor_list_split_documented_versus_inferred"):
        print("FAILED:RES-0060-probe1 split flag false")
        return 1
    factors = doc.get("ranking_factors") or []
    if len(factors) < 8:
        print(f"FAILED:RES-0060-probe1 too few factors n={len(factors)}")
        return 1
    documented: list[str] = []
    inferred: list[str] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for row in factors:
        if not isinstance(row, dict):
            print("FAILED:RES-0060-probe1 factor not object")
            return 1
        fid = str(row.get("id") or "")
        klass = str(row.get("evidence_class") or "")
        if not fid or fid in seen:
            print(f"FAILED:RES-0060-probe1 bad/duplicate id {fid!r}")
            return 1
        seen.add(fid)
        if klass not in ALLOWED:
            print(f"FAILED:RES-0060-probe1 unlabeled {fid} class={klass!r}")
            return 1
        if not row.get("factor") or not row.get("marketplace"):
            print(f"FAILED:RES-0060-probe1 incomplete row {fid}")
            return 1
        (documented if klass == "documented" else inferred).append(fid)
    if not documented or not inferred:
        print("FAILED:RES-0060-probe1 split is not both-sided")
        return 1
    total = len(documented) + len(inferred)
    proportion = len(inferred) / total
    prop = doc.get("undocumented_proportion") or {}
    if prop.get("documented_count") != len(documented):
        print("FAILED:RES-0060-probe1 documented_count mismatch")
        return 1
    if prop.get("inferred_count") != len(inferred):
        print("FAILED:RES-0060-probe1 inferred_count mismatch")
        return 1
    if prop.get("total") != total:
        print("FAILED:RES-0060-probe1 total mismatch")
        return 1
    got = float(prop.get("proportion"))
    if abs(got - proportion) > 1e-9:
        print(f"FAILED:RES-0060-probe1 proportion {got} != {proportion}")
        return 1
    statement = str(prop.get("statement") or "")
    if str(len(inferred)) not in statement or str(total) not in statement:
        print("FAILED:RES-0060-probe1 proportion not stated in prose")
        return 1
    answers = doc.get("answers") or {}
    listed_doc = (answers.get("signals_that_drive_discovery") or {}).get("documented") or []
    listed_inf = (answers.get("signals_that_drive_discovery") or {}).get("inferred") or []
    if set(listed_doc) != set(documented) or set(listed_inf) != set(inferred):
        print("FAILED:RES-0060-probe1 answers split != ranking_factors")
        return 1
    print(
        f"split documented={len(documented)} inferred={len(inferred)} "
        f"total={total} undocumented_proportion={proportion:.4f}"
    )
    print("SUCCESS:RES-0060-probe1 documented-versus-inferred split")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe2_dual_rail_and_api.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: dual-rail evidence, live capture hashes, API order inequality, local contract."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import common as C  # noqa: E402

COUNT_FIELDS = {"download_count", "downloads", "downloadCount", "installs", "install_count"}


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:RES-0060-probe2 {msg}")
    return 1


def rail_body(rail: dict) -> str:
    path = C.REPO / str(rail["path"]).replace("\\", "/")
    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")


def main() -> int:
    art_path = C.REPO / C.OUT_REL
    if not art_path.is_file():
        return fail("artifact missing")
    text = art_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    doc = json.loads(text)
    if C.SENTINEL not in text:
        return fail("sentinel absent")

    claims = doc.get("claims") or []
    if len(claims) < 4:
        return fail("too few claims")
    measured = 0
    for claim in claims:
        cid = str(claim.get("id") or "")
        tier = str(claim.get("evidence_tier") or "")
        rails = claim.get("rails") or []
        if not isinstance(rails, list) or not rails:
            return fail(f"{cid} has no rails")
        kinds = [str(r.get("kind") or "") for r in rails if isinstance(r, dict)]
        if tier == "E1":
            if len(rails) < 2:
                return fail(f"{cid} E1 with <2 rails")
            if not ({"api", "prose"} <= set(kinds)):
                return fail(f"{cid} E1 missing api+prose kinds {kinds}")
            if claim.get("measured_fact") is not True:
                return fail(f"{cid} E1 not marked measured_fact")
            measured += 1
        elif tier == "E3":
            if claim.get("measured_fact") is True:
                return fail(f"{cid} E3 cited as measured fact")
        for rail in rails:
            if not isinstance(rail, dict):
                return fail(f"{cid} rail not object")
            span = str(rail.get("quoted_span") or "")
            if len(span) < 8:
                return fail(f"{cid} quoted_span too short")
            body = rail_body(rail)
            if span not in body:
                return fail(f"{cid} quoted_span not in {rail.get('path')}")
            live_sha, live_n = C.digest_file(C.REPO / str(rail["path"]).replace("\\", "/"))
            if live_sha != str(rail.get("sha256") or ""):
                return fail(f"{cid} rail sha256 mismatch {rail.get('path')}")
            if int(rail.get("http_status") or 0) != 200:
                return fail(f"{cid} rail http_status {rail.get('http_status')}")
            if live_n <= 0:
                return fail(f"{cid} empty rail body")

    if measured < 5:
        return fail(f"too few measured E1 claims n={measured}")

    rel = C.vscode_ranked_ids("vscode-api-sort-relevance")
    ins = C.vscode_ranked_ids("vscode-api-sort-installs")
    rel_n = C.vscode_installs("vscode-api-sort-relevance")
    ins_n = C.vscode_installs("vscode-api-sort-installs")
    if len(rel) < 5 or len(ins) < 5:
        return fail("vscode API lists too short")
    if rel == ins:
        return fail("relevance order equals install order")
    if ins_n != sorted(ins_n, reverse=True):
        return fail("install sort is not descending install counts")
    obs = {}
    for claim in claims:
        if claim.get("id") == "C-VS-DEFAULT-NEQ-INSTALLS":
            obs = claim.get("observation") or {}
    if obs.get("relevance_top8") != rel or obs.get("installs_top8") != ins:
        return fail("artifact observation does not match live capture order")
    if obs.get("orders_equal") is not False:
        return fail("observation.orders_equal is not False")

    jb_html = C.capture_text("jetbrains-docs-ranking")
    if "log10(1 + 5 * downloads_count)" not in jb_html:
        return fail("jetbrains formula absent from ranking docs")
    jb_api = C.capture_text("jetbrains-api-search")
    if '"downloads":' not in jb_api:

RES-0125
--- emit.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/research/RES-0125.json from pinned captures + local marketplace schemas."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from check import (  # noqa: E402
    A2_MARKET,
    LOCKFILE,
    a2_generated_keys,
    check_artifact,
    load_indexes,
    lockfile_required_keys,
    quote_in_capture,
    sha256_bytes,
)
from labels import SCHEMA_DECLARED, SENTINEL, UNIT  # noqa: E402
from table import MARKETPLACE_FIELDS, ROWS  # noqa: E402

OUT = REPO / "command-center/research/RES-0125.json"
QUESTION = (
    "For blueprints and presets generated by models rather than written by a person, "
    "what provenance disclosure do the applicable vendor terms and emerging norms "
    "require, and what must the marketplace metadata carry?"
)


def utcnow() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(microsecond=0).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")


def main() -> int:
    now = utcnow()
    indexes = load_indexes()
    missing_quotes = []
    table = []
    for row in ROWS:
        rec = indexes.get(str(row["capture_id"]))
        if rec is None:
            print(f"FAILED:RES-0125 emit missing capture {row['capture_id']}")
            return 1
        try:
            if not quote_in_capture(row, indexes):
                missing_quotes.append(row["id"])
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            print(f"FAILED:RES-0125 emit quote {row['id']}: {exc}")
            return 1
        lineage = {
            "capture_id": row["capture_id"],
            "permalink": rec.get("final_url") or rec.get("requested_url"),
            "requested_url": rec.get("requested_url"),
            "fetch_timestamp": rec.get("fetch_timestamp"),
            "http_status": rec.get("http_status"),
            "sha256": rec.get("sha256"),
            "bytes": rec.get("bytes"),
            "version": row["document_version"],
            "commit_sha": row.get("commit_sha"),
            "archive_warc": row.get("archive_warc"),
            "capture_path": rec.get("capture_path"),
        }
        table.append(
            {
                **row,
                "required_versus_proposed": row["required_versus_proposed"],
                "source_lineage": lineage,
                "capture_sha256": rec.get("sha256"),
                "fetch_timestamp": rec.get("fetch_timestamp"),
            }
        )
    if missing_quotes:
        print(f"FAILED:RES-0125 emit quotes not in captures: {missing_quotes}")
        return 1

    lock_keys = sorted(lockfile_required_keys())
    a2_keys = sorted(a2_generated_keys())
    lock_sha = sha256_bytes(LOCKFILE.read_bytes())
    a2_sha = sha256_bytes(A2_MARKET.read_bytes())

    fields = []
    for field in MARKETPLACE_FIELDS:
        item = dict(field)
        if field["field"] in {"name", "version", "source_url", "digest"}:
            item["present_in_lockfile_schema"] = field["field"] in lock_keys or (
                field["field"] == "digest" and "digest" in lock_keys
            )
        fields.append(item)

    proposed = [r["id"] for r in table if r["required_versus_proposed"] == "proposed-norm"]
    by_terms = [r["id"] for r in table if r["required_versus_proposed"] == "required-by-terms"]
    by_law = [
        r["id"] for r in table if r["required_versus_proposed"] == "required-by-law-somewhere"
    ]

--- probe1_obligation_labels.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: obligation labels and the proposed-norm control.

Re-checks the live artifact, then runs two mutants that must go red:
unlabelled rows, and an all-required-by-terms table (the catalog control).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from check import (  # noqa: E402
    ART,
    check_artifact,
    load_indexes,
    mutant_all_required,
    mutant_drop_labels,
)
from labels import ALLOWED_LABELS, SENTINEL, UNIT  # noqa: E402


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:{UNIT}-probe1 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    if not ART.is_file():
        return fail(f"artifact absent {ART}")
    doc = json.loads(ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    indexes = load_indexes()
    live = check_artifact(doc, indexes)
    print(json.dumps({"live": live, "allowed_labels": list(ALLOWED_LABELS)}, sort_keys=True))
    if not live["pass"]:
        return fail(f"live {live['errors']}")

    dropped = check_artifact(mutant_drop_labels(doc), indexes)
    if dropped["pass"]:
        return fail("unlabelled mutant stayed green")
    if "bad-label" not in " ".join(dropped["errors"]):
        return fail(f"unlabelled mutant errors={dropped['errors']}")

    all_req = check_artifact(mutant_all_required(doc), indexes)
    if all_req["pass"]:
        return fail("all-required-by-terms mutant stayed green (proposed-norm control inert)")
    if "proposed-norm-control-failed" not in all_req["errors"]:
        return fail(f"all-required mutant errors={all_req['errors']}")

    rows = doc.get("obligation_table") or []
    labels = sorted({r.get("required_versus_proposed") for r in rows})
    proposed = [r["id"] for r in rows if r.get("required_versus_proposed") == "proposed-norm"]
    if SENTINEL not in ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8"):
        return fail("sentinel absent")
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "pass": True,
                "probe": "probe1-obligation-labels",
                "unit_id": UNIT,
                "rows": len(rows),
                "labels_present": labels,
                "proposed_norm_ids": proposed,
                "unlabelled_mutant_red": True,
                "all_required_mutant_red": True,
            },
            sort_keys=True,
        )
    )
    print(
        "SUCCESS:RES-0125-probe1 labels valid, proposed-norm present, "
        "both mutants red"
    )
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe2_pin_and_metadata.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: pin integrity, quote-in-capture, marketplace field gap.

Independent of probe 1's label-enum mutants. Starts from captures and the
lockfile schema, then checks the artifact. A pin-stripped mutant must go red.
A2 generator_version is not accepted as LLM generation_method.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from check import (  # noqa: E402
    A2_MARKET,
    ART,
    LOCKFILE,
    a2_generated_keys,
    check_artifact,
    load_indexes,
    lockfile_required_keys,
    mutant_strip_pins,
    quote_in_capture,
    sha256_bytes,
)
from labels import UNIT  # noqa: E402


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:{UNIT}-probe2 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    if not ART.is_file():
        return fail("artifact absent")
    doc = json.loads(ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    indexes = load_indexes()

    lock_keys = lockfile_required_keys()
    want_lock = {"name", "version", "source_url", "digest"}
    if want_lock - lock_keys:
        return fail(f"lockfile required moved: {sorted(lock_keys)}")
    if "generation_method" in lock_keys:
        return fail("generation_method unexpectedly required by lockfile")

    a2_keys = a2_generated_keys()
    if "generator_id" not in a2_keys or "generator_version" not in a2_keys:
        return fail(f"A2 _generated keys {sorted(a2_keys)}")
    a2 = json.loads(A2_MARKET.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if (a2.get("_generated") or {}).get("generator_id") == "model-generated":
        return fail("A2 header collides with LLM generation_method")

    live = check_artifact(doc, indexes)
    if not live["pass"]:
        return fail(f"live {live['errors']}")

    stripped = check_artifact(mutant_strip_pins(doc), indexes)
    if stripped["pass"]:
        return fail("pin-stripped mutant stayed green")
    if not any(e.startswith("unpinned:") for e in stripped["errors"]):
        return fail(f"pin mutant errors={stripped['errors']}")

    missing_quotes = []
    for row in doc.get("obligation_table") or []:
        if not quote_in_capture(row, indexes):
            missing_quotes.append(row.get("id"))
    if missing_quotes:
        return fail(f"quotes absent {missing_quotes}")

    fields = {f.get("field"): f for f in doc.get("marketplace_metadata") or []}
    gen = fields.get("generation_method") or {}
    if gen.get("required_versus_proposed") != "required-by-terms":
        return fail("generation_method not required-by-terms")
    if gen.get("present_in_lockfile_schema") is True:
        return fail("generation_method falsely claimed present in lockfile")
    c2pa = fields.get("c2pa_manifest") or {}
    if c2pa.get("required_versus_proposed") != "proposed-norm":
        return fail("c2pa_manifest not proposed-norm")

    lock_sha = sha256_bytes(LOCKFILE.read_bytes())
    art_lock = (doc.get("current_marketplace_surface") or {}).get("lockfile_sha256")
    if art_lock != lock_sha:
        return fail("lockfile sha drift vs artifact")

    if doc.get("not_legal_advice") is not True:
        return fail("not_legal_advice")

    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "pass": True,
                "probe": "probe2-pin-and-metadata",
                "unit_id": UNIT,
                "lockfile_required": sorted(lock_keys),
                "generation_method_in_lockfile": False,

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/TST-0001.json (conformance-report receipt)."""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

    production_reducer,

from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator  # noqa: E402
from jsonschema.validators import RefResolver  # noqa: E402

OUT = "command-center/receipts/TST-0001.json"

QUESTION = (

    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/TST-0001/probe1_twelve_conditions.py",
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/TST-0001/probe2_reducer_parity.py",
)


def digest_file(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def fingerprint(method: str, command: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(f"{method}|{command}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def clip(text: str, n: int = 60000) -> str:
    text = text.strip()
    if len(text) <= n:
        return text
    return text[: n - 14] + "\n...[clipped]"


def run_cmd(cmd: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
    proc = subprocess.run(
        cmd,
        cwd=str(REPO),
        shell=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        env=env,
        timeout=180,
    )
    text = ((proc.stdout or "") + (proc.stderr or "")).strip()
    return proc.returncode, text


def find_line(rel: str, snippet: str) -> int:
    text = (REPO / rel).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    idx = text.find(snippet)
    if idx < 0:
        raise SystemExit(f"FAILED:TST-0001 snippet not in {rel}: {snippet[:80]!r}")
    return text[:idx].count("\n") + 1


def artifact_row(rel: str, role: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    sha, size = digest_file(REPO / rel)

--- probe1_twelve_conditions.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: oracle has 12 independently-addressable, distinguishable conditions."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from case import snapshot_from_case  # noqa: E402
from conditions import CONDITION_IDS  # noqa: E402
from corpus import cases, isolation_cases  # noqa: E402
import oracle as O  # noqa: E402


def main() -> int:
    missing = [cid for cid in CONDITION_IDS if cid not in O.CHECKS]
    extra = [cid for cid in O.CHECKS if cid not in CONDITION_IDS]
    if missing or extra:
        print(f"FAILED:TST-0001-probe1 check-map missing={missing} extra={extra}")
        return 1
    if len(O.CHECKS) != 12:
        print(f"FAILED:TST-0001-probe1 expected 12 checks got {len(O.CHECKS)}")
        return 1

    seen: dict[str, str] = {}
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="tst0001-p1-") as tmp:
        root = Path(tmp)
        for case in isolation_cases():
            snap = snapshot_from_case(case, root / case["id"])
            vector = O.evaluate_all(snap)
            verdict = O.verdict(snap)
            expect = case["expect_failing_condition_id"]
            cid = verdict.get("failing_condition_id")
            print(
                f"isolation {case['id']} expect={expect} got={cid} "
                f"vector_false={[k for k, v in vector.items() if not v]}"
            )
            if verdict.get("verdict") != "FAIL":
                print(f"FAILED:TST-0001-probe1 {case['id']} did not FAIL")
                return 1
            if cid != expect:
                print(f"FAILED:TST-0001-probe1 {case['id']} id {cid!r} != {expect!r}")
                return 1
            if vector.get(expect) is not False:
                print(f"FAILED:TST-0001-probe1 {case['id']} check {expect} was not False")
                return 1
            if cid in seen:
                print(
                    f"FAILED:TST-0001-probe1 duplicate id {cid} ({seen[cid]} and {case['id']})"
                )
                return 1
            seen[cid] = case["id"]

        pass_case = next(c for c in cases() if c["id"] == "pass-control")
        snap = snapshot_from_case(pass_case, root / "pass-control")
        vector = O.evaluate_all(snap)
        verdict = O.verdict(snap)
        print(f"pass-control verdict={verdict['verdict']} vector={vector}")
        if verdict.get("verdict") != "PASS" or verdict.get("failing_condition_id") is not None:
            print("FAILED:TST-0001-probe1 pass-control did not PASS")
            return 1
        if not all(vector[cid] is True for cid in CONDITION_IDS):
            print(f"FAILED:TST-0001-probe1 pass-control vector not all True: {vector}")
            return 1

    if set(seen) != set(CONDITION_IDS):
        print(f"FAILED:TST-0001-probe1 ids {sorted(seen)} != {list(CONDITION_IDS)}")
        return 1
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {"distinct_failing_condition_ids": sorted(seen), "n": len(seen)},
            sort_keys=True,
        )
    )
    print(
        "SUCCESS:TST-0001-probe1 oracle has 12 distinguishable independently-addressable conditions"
    )
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe2_reducer_parity.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: production reducer matches oracle; always-true stub fails the harness."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from conditions import CONDITION_IDS  # noqa: E402
from harness import (  # noqa: E402
    always_true_reducer,
    compare_candidate,
    production_reducer,
)


def main() -> int:
    prod = compare_candidate(production_reducer())
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "mode": "production",
                "all_agree": prod["all_agree"],
                "mismatch_count": len(prod["mismatches"]),
                "per_condition": prod["per_condition"],
                "row_count": prod["row_count"],
            },
            indent=1,
            sort_keys=True,
        )
    )
    if not prod["all_agree"]:
        print("FAILED:TST-0001-probe2 production reducer disagrees with oracle")
        print(json.dumps(prod["mismatches"][:4], indent=1, sort_keys=True))
        return 1
    for cid in CONDITION_IDS:
        bucket = prod["per_condition"].get(cid) or {}
        if bucket.get("rows", 0) < 1 or bucket.get("agree") != bucket.get("rows"):
            print(f"FAILED:TST-0001-probe2 per-condition {cid} {bucket}")
            return 1
    pass_bucket = prod["per_condition"].get("PASS") or {}
    if pass_bucket.get("rows", 0) < 1 or pass_bucket.get("agree") != pass_bucket.get("rows"):
        print(f"FAILED:TST-0001-probe2 PASS bucket {pass_bucket}")
        return 1

    stub = compare_candidate(always_true_reducer())
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "mode": "always-true",
                "all_agree": stub["all_agree"],
                "mismatch_count": len(stub["mismatches"]),
                "row_count": stub["row_count"],
            },
            indent=1,
            sort_keys=True,
        )
    )
    if stub["all_agree"]:
        print("FAILED:TST-0001-probe2 always-true reducer was accepted (harness vacuous)")
        return 1
    if len(stub["mismatches"]) < 12:
        print(
            f"FAILED:TST-0001-probe2 always-true mismatches={len(stub['mismatches'])} want>=12"
        )
        return 1
    print(
        "SUCCESS:TST-0001-probe2 reducer matches oracle per-condition; "
        "always-true stub fails the harness"
    )
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe1_live_inventory.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: live inventory scan. Enumerator must find a known-present detector.
Identical good/broken output is named INERT; never summarised as all clean.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:TST-0018-probe1 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    result = scan()
    print(json.dumps(
        {
            "enumerator": {
                k: result["enumerator"][k]
                for k in (
                    "known_present",
                    "found_known_present",
                    "count",
                    "disk_known_present",
                    "found_disk_known_present",
                    "found_census_constant",
                    "enumerator_live",
                )
                if k in result["enumerator"]
            },
            "counts": result["counts"],
            "inert": result["inert"],
            "measures_nothing": result["measures_nothing"],
            "not_checked_count": len(result["not_checked"]),
            "all_clean_claim": result["all_clean_claim"],
            "summary": result["summary"],
            "pass": False,
        },
        indent=2,
        sort_keys=True,
    ))

    enum = result["enumerator"]
    if not enum.get("enumerator_live"):
        return fail(
            "enumerator missed known-present detector "
            f"{DISK_KNOWN_PRESENT} (census constant={enum.get('known_present')})"
        )
    if result["counts"]["checked"] < 1:
        return fail("zero shipped detectors exercised")
    if result["all_clean_claim"] and result["measures_nothing"]:
        return fail("all-clean summary while inert detectors named")
    if result["inert"] and "all clean" in result["summary"].lower():
        return fail("summary says all clean while inert list is non-empty")

    print(
        f"inventory={result['counts']['inventory']} "
        f"checked={result['counts']['checked']} "
        f"inert={result['counts']['inert']} "
        f"not_checked={result['counts']['not_checked']}"
    )
    print(result["summary"])
    print("SUCCESS:TST-0018-probe1 live inventory both-states scan")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe2_planted_inert.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: scanner flags a planted inert detector and does not flag a known-good one."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from scanner import (  # noqa: E402
    known_good_adapter,
    planted_inert_adapter,
    scan,
)


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:TST-0018-probe2 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    planted = planted_inert_adapter()
    good = known_good_adapter()
    result = scan(extra=[planted, good])
    extras = {r["detector_id"]: r for r in result["extra_rows"]}
    plant = extras.get(planted.detector_id)
    control = extras.get(good.detector_id)
    payload = {
        "planted": plant,
        "known_good": control,
        "planted_flagged_inert": bool(plant and plant.get("inert")),
        "known_good_flagged_inert": bool(control and control.get("inert")),
        "pass": False,
    }
    print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True, default=str))

    if plant is None:
        return fail("planted inert detector missing from extra rows")
    if not plant.get("inert"):
        return fail(
            f"scanner did not flag planted inert detector (verdict={plant.get('verdict')})"
        )
    if control is None:
        return fail("known-good detector missing from extra rows")
    if control.get("inert"):
        return fail(
            f"scanner flagged known-good detector as INERT (verdict={control.get('verdict')})"
        )
    if control.get("verdict") != "DIFFERS":
        return fail(f"known-good detector verdict {control.get('verdict')} != DIFFERS")

    print(
        "planted_inert_flagged known_good_not_flagged "
        f"planted_verdict={plant['verdict']} good_verdict={control['verdict']}"
    )
    print("SUCCESS:TST-0018-probe2 planted inert flagged; known-good not flagged")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe1_blob_not_worktree.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: gate hashes/formats BLOB bytes, not worktree or git-archive bytes.

Catalog tests: test_gate_reads_blob_bytes_not_worktree_bytes
Evidence: three-route table + LF-normalisation identity on this box.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

import blob_gate as G  # noqa: E402
from harness_load import load_both_states  # noqa: E402

UNIT = "TST-0031"


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:{UNIT}-probe1 {msg}")
    return 1


def sha(data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def main() -> int:
    bs = load_both_states()
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="tst0031-p1-") as td:
        root = Path(td)
        good = G.init_repo(root / "good")
        lf = b"line-one\nline-two\n" * 40
        G.commit_bytes(good, "sample.txt", lf, "lf-blob")
        G.commit_bytes(
            good,
            ".gitattributes",
            b"sample.txt text eol=crlf\n",
            "archive-export-crlf",
        )
        crlf_wt = lf.replace(b"\n", b"\r\n")
        (good / "sample.txt").write_bytes(crlf_wt)

        gate = G.evaluate(good, "HEAD", "sample.txt")
        blob = G.read_blob(good, "HEAD", "sample.txt")
        show = G.read_show(good, "HEAD", "sample.txt")
        arch = G.read_archive(good, "HEAD", "sample.txt")
        wt = G.worktree_bytes(good, "sample.txt")
        table = G.three_route(good, "HEAD", "sample.txt")

        print(json.dumps({"three_route": table, "gate": gate}, sort_keys=True))

        if blob != lf:
            return fail(f"cat-file did not return LF blob: {blob[:40]!r}")
        if show != blob:
            return fail("git show disagreed with git cat-file")
        if wt != crlf_wt:
            return fail("worktree was not the planted CRLF bytes")
        if gate["blob_sha256"] != sha(lf):
            return fail("gate hashed something other than the LF blob")
        if gate["blob_sha256"] == sha(crlf_wt):
            return fail("gate hash collided with CRLF worktree (read worktree?)")
        if gate["worktree_sha256"] != sha(crlf_wt):
            return fail("recorded worktree hash is wrong")
        if gate["hash_matches_worktree"]:
            return fail("gate claimed blob hash equals worktree hash")
        if not gate["format_ok"] or gate["verdict"] != "PASS":
            return fail("LF blob should format-PASS")
        if G.format_ok_text_blob(crlf_wt):
            return fail("format check on worktree CRLF should fail; control broken")
        if arch == blob:
            return fail("git archive equalled blob; eol=crlf fixture did not convert")
        if arch.count(b"\r\n") < 1:
            return fail("git archive produced no CRLF on eol=crlf fixture")
        if not table["archive_differs_from_blob"]:
            return fail("three-route did not record archive≠blob")
        if not table["cat_file_equals_show"]:
            return fail("cat-file !== show")
        if not table["identical_after_lf_normalisation"]:
            return fail("LF-normalisation identity failed")
        if table["recipe_used_by_gate"] != G.AUTHORITATIVE_RECIPE:
            return fail("gate recipe drifted")

        def format_fires(state: dict) -> bool:
            result = G.evaluate(Path(state["repo"]), "HEAD", state["path"])
            return not result["format_ok"]

        broken = G.init_repo(root / "broken")
        crlf_blob = lf.replace(b"\n", b"\r\n")
        G.commit_bytes(broken, "sample.txt", crlf_blob, "crlf-as-blob")
        (broken / "sample.txt").write_bytes(lf)

        rec = bs.control(
            format_fires,

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: archive-as-blob lint + RELEASE-CHECKLIST recipe matches the gate.

Catalog tests:
  test_lint_flags_git_archive_used_as_a_blob_source
  test_release_checklist_recipe_matches_the_gate
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from harness_load import load_both_states  # noqa: E402

UNIT = "TST-0031"
CHECKLIST = REPO / "command-center" / "RELEASE-CHECKLIST.md"

BAD_MD = """# planted prescription

```bash
git archive HEAD -- sample.txt | tar -xO > /tmp/bytes
```
"""

MENTION_MD = """# documentary mention

Never use git archive | tar -x to read blob bytes; it returns CRLF
on export. Use git cat-file -p instead.
"""

PACKAGING_MD = """# legitimate packaging

```bash
git archive --format=tar.gz -o plugin.tar.gz HEAD
```
"""

MENTION_SH = "# never git archive | tar -x as a blob source\ngit cat-file -p HEAD:foo.py | sha256sum\n"


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:{UNIT}-probe2 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    bs = load_both_states()
    if not CHECKLIST.is_file():
        return fail(f"missing {CHECKLIST}")
    text = CHECKLIST.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if G.AUTHORITATIVE_RECIPE.split(" <")[0] not in text and "git cat-file -p" not in text:
        return fail("RELEASE-CHECKLIST.md does not prescribe git cat-file -p")
    if "git show" not in text:
        return fail("RELEASE-CHECKLIST.md missing equivalent git show recipe")
    if G.AUTHORITATIVE_RECIPE not in text and "git cat-file -p" not in text:
        return fail("checklist recipe does not match the gate")
    cl_findings = L.scan_path(CHECKLIST, repo=REPO)
    cl_prov = [f for f in cl_findings if f["kind"] == "provenance"]
    if cl_prov:
        return fail(f"checklist still prescribes archive as blob source: {cl_prov}")
    # Gate module recipe strings must appear so the documents cannot drift apart.
    gate_src = (HERE / "blob_gate.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if "git cat-file" not in gate_src or "archive" not in gate_src:
        return fail("gate source lost cat-file / archive vocabulary")

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="tst0031-p2-") as td:
        root = Path(td)
        (root / "bad.md").write_text(BAD_MD, encoding="utf-8")
        (root / "mention.md").write_text(MENTION_MD, encoding="utf-8")
        (root / "pack.md").write_text(PACKAGING_MD, encoding="utf-8")
        (root / "bad.sh").write_text(BAD_SH, encoding="utf-8")
        (root / "mention.sh").write_text(MENTION_SH, encoding="utf-8")

        def lint_fires(state: str) -> bool:
            path = root / state
            hits = [f for f in L.scan_path(path) if f["kind"] == "provenance"]
            return bool(hits)

        rec_md = bs.control(
            lint_fires,
            "mention.md",
            "bad.md",
            check_id="test_lint_flags_git_archive_used_as_a_blob_source",
            description="Lint fires on a fenced git-archive|tar blob recipe and is silent on a prose mention.",
        )
        if rec_md.verdict != bs.VALID:
            return fail(f"md both-states rejected: {rec_md.to_dict()}")
        rec_sh = bs.control(
            lint_fires,
            "mention.sh",
            "bad.sh",
            check_id="test_lint_shell_prescription",

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/TST-0055.json from a live encoding matrix."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import matrix as M  # noqa: E402
import schema_check as S  # noqa: E402

OUT = "command-center/receipts/TST-0055.json"

SENTINEL = "SUCCESS:TST-0055"
P1 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/TST-0055/probe1_bom_loud.py"
)
P2 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/TST-0055/probe2_utf16_silent.py"
)
QUESTION = (
    "Target: every config/artifact reader must be exercised against UTF-8, "
    "UTF-8-with-BOM, UTF-16-LE and Latin-1 inputs. On Windows, PowerShell "
    "historically emits UTF-8-with-BOM and a strict reader chokes on the "
    "leading \\ufeff. Which readers fail, and do they fail loudly?"
)
SPEC_ANCHOR = "command-center/catalog-slices/TST-1.json#TST-0055"
MANIFEST = "command-center/p2-wave/probes/TST-0055/UNIT-ARTIFACT-MANIFEST.json"
PLACEHOLDER_SHA = "0" * 64


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def sha256_bytes(raw: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(raw.replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")).hexdigest()


def probe_fp(method: str, command: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(f"{method}|{command}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:32]


def compact_cell(cell: dict) -> dict:
    keep = (
        "reader_id",
        "module_rel",
        "source_anchor",
        "kind",
        "in_required_tests",
        "encoding",
        "codec",
        "payload_kind",
        "byte_count",
        "hex_prefix",
        "nul_count",
        "contains_latin1_mark_byte",
        "classification",
        "exception_type",
        "exception_text",
        "handled",
        "silent_empty",
        "rejected_loudly",
        "utf8_decode_prefix",
        "utf8_decode_error",
    )
    out = {k: cell.get(k) for k in keep}
    if cell.get("classification") in {"HANDLE", "SILENT_EMPTY", "SILENT_WRONG"}:
        out["value"] = cell.get("value")
    else:
        out["value"] = None
    return out


def cell_exc(measured: dict, reader_id: str, encoding: str) -> str:
    for cell in measured["matrix"]:
        if cell["reader_id"] == reader_id and cell["encoding"] == encoding:
            return str(cell.get("exception_text") or "")
    return ""


def build_answer(measured: dict) -> str:
    silent = measured["summary"]["silent"]
    bom_exc = cell_exc(measured, "catalog.load", "utf-8-bom")
    utf16_exc = cell_exc(measured, "catalog.load", "utf-16-le")
    latin_exc = cell_exc(measured, "catalog.load", "latin-1")
    required = [c for c in measured["matrix"] if c["in_required_tests"]]
    n_req = len({c["reader_id"] for c in required})
    return (
        f"On this {measured['platform']['system']} Python "
        f"{measured['platform']['python']} worktree, {n_req} live production "
        "config/artifact readers that do json.loads(path.read_text("

--- probe1_bom_loud.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: BOM-prefixed JSON is handled or rejected loudly.

Re-runs the live matrix. Requires every config/artifact reader to HANDLE
UTF-8 and to HANDLE or REJECT_LOUD UTF-8-with-BOM, matching the receipt.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import jsonschema

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import matrix as M  # noqa: E402

ART = REPO / "command-center/receipts/TST-0055.json"
SCHEMA = REPO / "command-center/schema/test-receipt.schema.json"
SENTINEL = "SUCCESS:TST-0055"


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:TST-0055-probe1 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    live = M.run_matrix()
    if not live["encoding_self_check"]["pass"]:
        return fail("encoding self-check")
    if not live["summary"]["required_utf8_handle"]:
        return fail("required utf-8 HANDLE missing")
    if not live["summary"]["required_bom_handled_or_loud"]:
        return fail("required BOM cell was not HANDLE or REJECT_LOUD")
    if not live["tests"]["test_bom_prefixed_json_is_handled_or_rejected_loudly"]:
        return fail("BOM test false")

    bom_cells = [
        c
        for c in live["matrix"]
        if c["in_required_tests"] and c["encoding"] == "utf-8-bom"
    ]
    if not bom_cells:
        return fail("no BOM cells")
    loud = [c for c in bom_cells if c["classification"] == "REJECT_LOUD"]
    if not loud:
        return fail("no required reader rejected BOM loudly")
    for cell in loud:
        text = cell.get("exception_text") or ""
        if "BOM" not in text and "utf-8-sig" not in text:
            return fail(f"BOM exception text not diagnostic: {text!r}")

    if not ART.is_file() or ART.stat().st_size <= 0:
        return fail("artifact missing")
    text = ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if SENTINEL not in text:
        return fail("sentinel absent")
    doc = json.loads(text)
    if doc.get("state") in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
        return fail(f"self-declared {doc.get('state')}")
    if doc.get("unit_id") != "TST-0055":
        return fail(f"unit_id {doc.get('unit_id')}")
    schema = json.loads(SCHEMA.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    errs = list(jsonschema.Draft7Validator(schema).iter_errors(doc))
    if errs:
        return fail(f"schema {errs[0].message}")

    art_bom = [
        c
        for c in (doc.get("matrix") or [])
        if c.get("in_required_tests") and c.get("encoding") == "utf-8-bom"
    ]
    live_fp = {
        (c["reader_id"], c["encoding"]): (
            c["classification"],
            c.get("exception_type"),
            c.get("exception_text"),
        )
        for c in bom_cells
    }
    art_fp = {
        (c["reader_id"], c["encoding"]): (
            c["classification"],
            c.get("exception_type"),
            c.get("exception_text"),
        )
        for c in art_bom
    }
    if live_fp != art_fp:
        return fail(f"artifact BOM matrix drift {sorted(set(live_fp) ^ set(art_fp))[:6]}")

    tests = {t.get("id"): t for t in (doc.get("tests") or []) if isinstance(t, dict)}
    row = tests.get("test_bom_prefixed_json_is_handled_or_rejected_loudly")
    if not row or row.get("result") != "PASS":
        return fail("artifact BOM test not PASS")

    print(

--- probe2_utf16_silent.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: UTF-16-LE must not silently parse as empty.

Derives classifications from live readers BEFORE opening the receipt.
Requires: planted decoder SILENT_EMPTY on UTF-16, catalog REJECT_LOUD on
UTF-16, inventory._load_json SILENT_EMPTY on UTF-16 (swallow control), and
every config/artifact reader not SILENT_EMPTY.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import shutil
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import matrix as M  # noqa: E402
import schema_check as S  # noqa: E402

ART = REPO / "command-center/receipts/TST-0055.json"
SENTINEL = "SUCCESS:TST-0055"


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:TST-0055-probe2 {msg}")
    return 1


def pick(cells: list[dict], reader_id: str, encoding: str) -> dict:
    for cell in cells:
        if cell["reader_id"] == reader_id and cell["encoding"] == encoding:
            return cell
    raise KeyError(f"{reader_id}/{encoding}")


def main() -> int:
    # Derive first. Do not load the artifact until these numbers exist.
    live = M.run_matrix()
    cells = live["matrix"]
    plant_utf16 = pick(cells, "planted.silent_empty_decoder", "utf-16-le")
    plant_utf8 = pick(cells, "planted.silent_empty_decoder", "utf-8")
    catalog_utf16 = pick(cells, "catalog.load", "utf-16-le")
    inventory_utf16 = pick(cells, "inventory._load_json", "utf-16-le")
    inventory_utf8 = pick(cells, "inventory._load_json", "utf-8")

    if plant_utf16["classification"] != "SILENT_EMPTY":
        return fail("planted decoder did not SILENT_EMPTY on utf-16-le")
    if plant_utf8["classification"] != "HANDLE":
        return fail("planted decoder did not HANDLE utf-8")
    if catalog_utf16["classification"] != "REJECT_LOUD":
        return fail(
            f"catalog.load utf-16-le was {catalog_utf16['classification']} not REJECT_LOUD"
        )
    if catalog_utf16.get("silent_empty"):
        return fail("catalog.load utf-16-le silent_empty")
    exc = catalog_utf16.get("exception_text") or ""
    if not exc:
        return fail("catalog.load utf-16-le missing exception text")
    if inventory_utf16["classification"] != "SILENT_EMPTY":
        return fail("inventory utf-16-le did not swallow to SILENT_EMPTY")
    if inventory_utf8["classification"] != "HANDLE":
        return fail("inventory utf-8 did not HANDLE planted token")
    if not live["tests"]["test_utf16_input_does_not_silently_parse_as_empty"]:
        return fail("required utf-16 silent-empty test false")

    required_utf16 = [
        c
        for c in cells
        if c["in_required_tests"] and c["encoding"] == "utf-16-le"
    ]
    silent_req = [c["reader_id"] for c in required_utf16 if c["classification"] == "SILENT_EMPTY"]
    if silent_req:
        return fail(f"required readers silently empty on utf-16: {silent_req}")
    if catalog_utf16["nul_count"] <= 0:
        return fail("utf-16-le fixture was not NUL-dense")

    shells = {
        "bash": shutil.which("bash"),
        "pwsh": shutil.which("pwsh"),
        "powershell": shutil.which("powershell"),
    }

    # Now open the artifact and compare.
    if not ART.is_file() or ART.stat().st_size <= 0:
        return fail("artifact missing")
    text = ART.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if SENTINEL not in text:
        return fail("sentinel absent")
    doc = json.loads(text)
    if doc.get("state") in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
        return fail(f"self-declared {doc.get('state')}")

    def fp(cell: dict) -> tuple:
        return (
            cell.get("classification"),
            cell.get("exception_type"),
            cell.get("exception_text"),
            cell.get("silent_empty"),

    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/TST-0115/probe1_documented_artifacts.py",
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/TST-0115/probe2_package_not_source.py",
]


def digest_file(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def art(rel: str, role: str) -> dict:
    path = REPO / rel
    sha, size = digest_file(path) if path.is_file() else ("", 0)
    return {
        "path": rel,
        "role": role,
        "exists": path.is_file(),
        "bytes": size,
        "sha256": sha,
    }


def run_example(launcher: Path, example_id: str, profile: Path) -> dict:
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
    env.pop("PYTHONPATH", None)
    proc = subprocess.run(
        [str(launcher), "run", "--id", example_id, "--profile", str(profile)],
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        env=env,
    )
    artifact = profile / "artifacts" / f"{example_id}.json"
    errs = check_artifact(example_id, artifact)
    body = None
    if artifact.is_file():
        body = json.loads(artifact.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return {
        "id": example_id,

--- probe1_documented_artifacts.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: each documented example, installed package, artifact postcondition."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from install import (  # noqa: E402
    build_wheel,
    clean_profile,
    install_wheel,
    isolated_python_cmd,
    repo_on_path,
    with_workspace,
)
from postcondition import (  # noqa: E402
    check_artifact,
    documented_advanced_ids,
    documented_quickstart_ids,
)

ART = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4] / "command-center" / "receipts" / "TST-0115.json"


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:TST-0115-probe1 {msg}")
    return 1


def run_launcher(launcher: Path, example_id: str, profile: Path) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
    env.pop("PYTHONPATH", None)
    env["AST_EXAMPLES_PROFILE"] = str(profile)
    return subprocess.run(
        [str(launcher), "run", "--id", example_id, "--profile", str(profile)],
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        env=env,
    )


def main() -> int:
    quick = documented_quickstart_ids()
    advanced = documented_advanced_ids()
    if len(quick) < 2:
        return fail(f"quickstart ids {quick}")
    if len(advanced) < 2:
        return fail(f"advanced ids {advanced}")

    results: list[dict] = []
    with with_workspace() as tmp_s:
        work = Path(tmp_s)
        wheel = build_wheel(work)
        paths = install_wheel(wheel, work / "prefix")
        profile = clean_profile(work)
        before = {p.name for p in profile.iterdir()}
        if before:
            return fail(f"profile-not-clean {sorted(before)}")

        loc = subprocess.run(
            isolated_python_cmd(
                paths["site"],
                "import ast_examples; print(ast_examples.__file__); print(ast_examples.ORIGIN)",
            ),
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            env={**os.environ, "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1", "PYTHONPATH": ""},
        )
        if loc.returncode != 0:
            return fail(f"import-installed {loc.stderr}")
        file_line, origin_line = loc.stdout.strip().splitlines()[:2]
        if repo_on_path(file_line):
            return fail(f"installed-module-in-repo {file_line}")
        if "site-packages" not in file_line.replace("\\", "/"):
            return fail(f"not-site-packages {file_line}")
        if origin_line.strip() != "wheel":
            return fail(f"origin {origin_line!r}")

        for lane, ids in (("quickstart", quick), ("advanced", advanced)):
            for example_id in ids:
                proc = run_launcher(paths["launcher"], example_id, profile)
                artifact = profile / "artifacts" / f"{example_id}.json"
                errs = check_artifact(example_id, artifact)
                row = {
                    "id": example_id,
                    "lane": lane,
                    "exit_code": proc.returncode,
                    "artifact": str(artifact),
                    "artifact_exists": artifact.is_file(),
                    "artifact_bytes": artifact.stat().st_size if artifact.is_file() else 0,
                    "postcondition_errors": errs,
                }

--- probe2_package_not_source.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: examples run from the wheel, not the source tree; exit 0 is not enough."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from install import (  # noqa: E402
    SRC_PKG,
    build_wheel,
    clean_profile,
    install_wheel,
    isolated_python_cmd,
    repo_on_path,
    with_workspace,
)
from postcondition import check_artifact, check_doc_object  # noqa: E402

REPO = HERE.parents[3]


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:TST-0115-probe2 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    source_only = SRC_PKG / "ast_examples" / "_source_tree_only.py"
    if not source_only.is_file():
        return fail("source-tree marker missing from repo (control is broken)")

    with with_workspace() as tmp_s:
        work = Path(tmp_s)
        wheel = build_wheel(work)
        paths = install_wheel(wheel, work / "prefix")
        profile = clean_profile(work)

        planted = paths["site"] / "ast_examples" / "_source_tree_only.py"
        if planted.is_file():
            return fail("source-tree-only module leaked into the wheel install")

        env = {
            **os.environ,
            "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1",
            "PYTHONPATH": "",
        }
        leak = subprocess.run(
            isolated_python_cmd(
                paths["site"],
                "import ast_examples._source_tree_only as m; print(m.MARKER)",
            ),
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            env=env,
        )
        if leak.returncode == 0:
            return fail("installed env imported source-tree-only module")

        loc = subprocess.run(
            isolated_python_cmd(
                paths["site"],
                "import ast_examples, pathlib; "
                "p=pathlib.Path(ast_examples.__file__).resolve(); "
                "print(p); print(ast_examples.ORIGIN)",
            ),
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            env=env,
        )
        if loc.returncode != 0:
            return fail(loc.stderr)
        module_path, origin = loc.stdout.strip().splitlines()[:2]
        if repo_on_path(module_path):
            return fail(f"module resolved into repo {module_path}")
        if origin.strip() != "wheel":
            return fail(f"ORIGIN {origin!r} is not wheel")
        if str(SRC_PKG.resolve()) in module_path:
            return fail("module path contains packaging src")

        # Contrast: putting the source tree on PYTHONPATH exposes the marker.
        sourced = subprocess.run(
            [
                sys.executable,
                "-B",
                "-c",
                "import ast_examples._source_tree_only as m; print(m.MARKER)",
            ],
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            env={
                **os.environ,
                "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1",
                "PYTHONPATH": str(SRC_PKG),

    )

                "role": "supporting",
            }
        )

        "adv_rebuild_circle": False,
        "answer": answer,
        "artifacts": arts,
        "both_states": {
            "frozen_clock_raw_identical": True,
            "undeclared_field": "session_nonce",
            "undeclared_field_fails_normalized": True,
            "wall_clock_raw_differs": True,
            "wall_clock_normalized_identical": True,
        },
        "byte_diff": {
            "file_diffs": wall["byte_diff"]["file_diffs"],
            "first_byte_diff": wall["byte_diff"]["first_byte_diff"],
            "first_file": wall["byte_diff"]["first_file"],
            "identical": False,
            "mechanism": wall["byte_diff"]["mechanism"],

            "tree_sha_b": wall["byte_diff"]["tree_sha_b"],
