#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: mid-phase resume vs uninterrupted, raw byte-diff + declared-mask norm.

Does not import probe2. Re-runs the pipeline live.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from engine import PAUSE_AFTER, SEED  # noqa: E402
from measure import collect, independent_continue_matches_helper  # noqa: E402

RECEIPT = REPO / "command-center" / "receipts" / "TST-0164.json"
SENTINEL = "SUCCESS:TST-0164"


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:TST-0164-probe1 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    data = collect()
    flags = data["flags"]
    wall = data["wall"]
    frozen = data["frozen"]
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "pause_after": PAUSE_AFTER,
                "seed": SEED,
                "declared_mask": data["declared_nondeterministic_fields"],
                "wall_raw_identical": wall["byte_diff"]["identical"],
                "wall_normalized_identical": wall["normalized_diff"]["identical"],
                "wall_first_file": wall["byte_diff"]["first_file"],
                "wall_first_byte": wall["byte_diff"]["first_byte_diff"],
                "wall_raw_sha_a": wall["byte_diff"]["tree_sha_a"],
                "wall_raw_sha_b": wall["byte_diff"]["tree_sha_b"],
                "wall_norm_sha_a": wall["normalized_diff"]["tree_sha_a"],
                "wall_norm_sha_b": wall["normalized_diff"]["tree_sha_b"],
                "frozen_raw_identical": frozen["byte_diff"]["identical"],
                "resume_executions": wall["resume_executions"],
                "uninterrupted_executions": wall["uninterrupted_executions"],
                "semantic_equal": wall["semantic_payloads_equal"],
            },
            indent=2,
            sort_keys=True,
        )
    )
    if not flags["wall_raw_differs"]:
        return fail("wall-clock uninterrupted vs resume were raw-identical (ND fields never fired)")
    if not flags["wall_normalized_identical"]:
        diffs = wall["normalized_diff"]["file_diffs"]
        return fail(f"declared-mask normalized trees still differ: {diffs}")
    if wall["byte_diff"]["tree_sha_a"] == wall["byte_diff"]["tree_sha_b"]:
        return fail("raw tree hashes collided while identical flag was false")
    if wall["normalized_diff"]["tree_sha_a"] != wall["normalized_diff"]["tree_sha_b"]:
        return fail("normalized tree hashes disagree with identical=true")
    if not flags["wall_semantic_equal"]:
        return fail("semantic payload/records/downstream diverged (resume bug, not ND)")
    if not flags["frozen_raw_identical"]:
        return fail("frozen-clock pair was not raw-identical (resume injected extra diffs)")
    if not flags["frozen_normalized_identical"]:
        return fail("frozen-clock pair failed normalized identity")
    if not flags["resume_skips_ingest"]:
        return fail(f"resume re-ran ingest executions={wall['resume_executions']}")
    if not flags["uninterrupted_runs_all_phases"]:
        return fail(f"uninterrupted executions={wall['uninterrupted_executions']}")
    if wall["ingest_reexecuted"]:
        return fail("resume notes claimed ingest re-execution")
    if not independent_continue_matches_helper():
        return fail("continue_from_checkpoint helper diverged from uninterrupted frozen run")
    if data["pause_after"] != "ingest":
        return fail("checkpoint is not mid-phase (ingest)")
    if wall["byte_diff"]["mechanism"] == wall["normalized_diff"]["mechanism"]:
        return fail("byte-diff and normalized-diff used the same mechanism string")
    if not data["ledger"]["uninterrupted"]["valid"]:
        return fail(f"ledger schema {data['ledger']['uninterrupted']['errors']}")

    if RECEIPT.is_file():
        text = RECEIPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        doc = json.loads(text)
        if SENTINEL not in text:
            return fail("receipt sentinel absent")
        if str(doc.get("state") or "").upper() in {
            "BLOCKED",
            "FAILED",
            "SEVERED",
            "TIMEOUT",
        }:
            return fail(f"receipt self-declared {doc.get('state')}")
        if doc.get("normalized_diff", {}).get("identical") is not True:
            return fail("receipt normalized_diff.identical is not true")
        if doc.get("mask_discovered_by_diffing") is not False:

--- probe2_mask_controls.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: independent reviewer — undeclared ND field + mask-coverage ceiling.

Does not import probe1. Rebuilds measurements and AST-checks that the mask
list is a literal declared up front.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import jsonschema

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from mask import (  # noqa: E402
    DECLARED_NONDETERMINISTIC_FIELDS,
    MaskCoverageError,
    check_mask_coverage,
    declared_fields_are_literals,
    mask_module_imports_diff_walker,
)
from measure import collect  # noqa: E402

RECEIPT = REPO / "command-center" / "receipts" / "TST-0164.json"
SCHEMA = HERE / "resume_report.schema.json"
SENTINEL = "SUCCESS:TST-0164"


def fail(msg: str) -> int:
    print(f"FAILED:TST-0164-probe2 {msg}")
    return 1


def main() -> int:
    data = collect()
    flags = data["flags"]
    cov = data["mask_coverage"]
    undecl = data["undeclared_nd"]
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "declared_fields_are_literals": flags["declared_fields_are_literals"],
                "mask_not_imported_from_diff": flags["mask_not_imported_from_diff"],
                "undeclared_normalized_identical": undecl["normalized_diff"]["identical"],
                "undeclared_first_file": undecl["normalized_diff"]["first_file"],
                "blanket_rejected": cov["blanket"]["rejected"],
                "blanket_reason": cov["blanket"].get("reason"),
                "mask_everything_rejected": cov["mask_everything"]["rejected"],
                "mask_everything_reason": cov["mask_everything"].get("reason"),
                "semantic_rejected": cov["semantic_field"]["rejected"],
                "session_nonce_in_declared_mask": "session_nonce"
                in data["declared_nondeterministic_fields"],
            },
            indent=2,
            sort_keys=True,
        )
    )

    if not declared_fields_are_literals():
        return fail("DECLARED_NONDETERMINISTIC_FIELDS is not a tuple of string literals")
    if mask_module_imports_diff_walker():
        return fail("mask.py imports a diff walker (mask would be discovered, not declared)")
    if "session_nonce" in DECLARED_NONDETERMINISTIC_FIELDS:
        return fail("session_nonce is in the declared mask; undeclared control is dead")
    if not flags["undeclared_nd_fails_normalized"]:
        return fail("undeclared session_nonce was absorbed by the mask (blanket)")
    if undecl["normalized_diff"]["identical"] is True:
        return fail("defective run normalized-identical; mask is not discriminating")
    if not flags["undeclared_nd_semantic_still_equal"]:
        return fail("session_nonce plant also moved semantic payload (control is mixed)")
    if not flags["blanket_mask_rejected"]:
        return fail("blanket token '*' was not rejected")
    if cov["blanket"].get("reason") != "mask-coverage-ceiling":
        return fail(f"blanket reason {cov['blanket'].get('reason')!r} != mask-coverage-ceiling")
    if not flags["mask_everything_rejected"]:
        return fail("masking every leaf was not rejected")
    if not flags["semantic_mask_rejected"]:
        return fail("masking downstream.transform_digest was not rejected")
    try:
        check_mask_coverage(["payload.sha256"])
        return fail("payload.sha256 was accepted as a mask field")
    except MaskCoverageError as exc:
        if exc.reason != "unmaskable-semantic-field":
            return fail(f"payload.sha256 reason {exc.reason!r}")

    schema = json.loads(SCHEMA.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    garbage = {"totally": "unrelated", "passing_tests": 999999, "state": "SUCCESS"}
    g_errs = list(jsonschema.Draft7Validator(schema).iter_errors(garbage))
    print(f"garbage_schema_errors={len(g_errs)}")
    if not g_errs:
        return fail("resume_report schema accepted unrelated garbage")

    if not RECEIPT.is_file():
        return fail("receipt missing")
    text = RECEIPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    doc = json.loads(text)

TST-0210
--- emit_receipt.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/TST-0210.json from live child-env measurements."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from harness import (  # noqa: E402
    ADAPTER_PATH,
    CANARY_KEY,
    SECURITY_MJS,
    dump_keys_via_adapter,
    dump_keys_with_env,
    load_env_mod,
    planted_canary,
)

UID = "TST-0210"
OUT = "command-center/receipts/TST-0210.json"
SCHEMA = "command-center/schema/receipt.schema.json#/$defs/security_report"
SENTINEL = "SUCCESS:TST-0210"
P1 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/TST-0210/probe1_canary_absent_and_allowlist.py"
)
P2 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/TST-0210/probe2_detector_and_inherit.py"
)
QUESTION = (
    "Does a spawned agent/worker process receive an ALLOWLISTED env set? "
    "PASS requires a planted canary variable to be absent from the child, "
    "and the allowlist to be declared in code."
)


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def catalog_unit() -> dict:
    data = json.loads(
        (REPO / "command-center" / "WORK-UNIT-CATALOG.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    )
    for unit in data.get("work_units") or []:
        if isinstance(unit, dict) and unit.get("id") == UID:
            return unit
    raise SystemExit("FAILED:TST-0210 catalog unit missing")


def live() -> dict:
    env_mod = load_env_mod()
    declared = sorted(env_mod.load_declared_allowlist(SECURITY_MJS))
    static = env_mod.adapter_run_calls_pass_env(
        ADAPTER_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    )
    with planted_canary():
        parent_has = CANARY_KEY in os.environ
        adapter_keys, dispatched = dump_keys_via_adapter()
        filtered_keys = dump_keys_with_env(env_mod.child_env())
        detector_keys = dump_keys_with_env(
            env_mod.filter_env(os.environ, extra_allow=[CANARY_KEY])
        )
        inherit_keys = dump_keys_with_env(dict(os.environ))
    extras = env_mod.extras_vs_allowlist(adapter_keys, declared)
    inherit_extras = env_mod.extras_vs_allowlist(inherit_keys, declared)
    ok = (
        parent_has
        and CANARY_KEY not in adapter_keys
        and CANARY_KEY not in filtered_keys
        and CANARY_KEY in detector_keys
        and CANARY_KEY in inherit_keys
        and extras == []
        and inherit_extras != []
        and static.get("bash_env")
        and static.get("powershell_env")
    )
    return {
        "ok": ok,
        "parent_has_canary": parent_has,
        "adapter_keys": adapter_keys,
        "filtered_keys": filtered_keys,
        "detector_canary_present": CANARY_KEY in detector_keys,
        "inherit_canary_present": CANARY_KEY in inherit_keys,
        "extras": extras,
        "inherit_extras_count": len(inherit_extras),
        "declared": declared,
        "static": static,
        "adapter_state": dispatched.get("state"),
        "adapter_unit_id": dispatched.get("unit_id"),
    }

--- probe1_canary_absent_and_allowlist.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: planted canary is absent in a live adapter child; allowlist matches keys.

Two independent signals:
  (a) child's dumped environment keys (canary absent)
  (b) static allowlist in tools/lib/security.mjs compared to the observed set
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from harness import (  # noqa: E402
    ADAPTER_PATH,
    CANARY_KEY,
    SECURITY_MJS,
    dump_keys_via_adapter,
    load_env_mod,
    planted_canary,
)


def main() -> int:
    env_mod = load_env_mod()
    declared = env_mod.load_declared_allowlist(SECURITY_MJS)
    adapter_src = ADAPTER_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    static = env_mod.adapter_run_calls_pass_env(adapter_src)
    problems: list[str] = []
    if CANARY_KEY in declared:
        problems.append("canary is in the declared allowlist; detector would be vacuous")
    if not static.get("perform_found"):
        problems.append("ClaudeAdapter._perform not found for env= walk")
    if not static.get("bash_env"):
        problems.append("Bash subprocess.run does not pass env=")
    if not static.get("powershell_env"):
        problems.append("PowerShell subprocess.run does not pass env=")

    with planted_canary():
        if os_canary_missing():
            problems.append("failed to plant canary in parent")
        observed, dispatched = dump_keys_via_adapter()

    extras = env_mod.extras_vs_allowlist(observed, declared)
    canary_present = CANARY_KEY in observed
    if canary_present:
        problems.append("canary key present in child dump")
    if extras:
        problems.append(f"child keys outside allowlist: {extras[:20]}")
    if not observed:
        problems.append("child dump produced zero keys (dump may be fake-empty)")

    payload = {
        "probe": "probe1-canary-absent-and-allowlist",
        "unit_id": "TST-0210",
        "pass": not problems,
        "problems": problems,
        "canary_key": CANARY_KEY,
        "canary_present_in_child": canary_present,
        "declared_allowlist_size": len(declared),
        "observed_key_count": len(observed),
        "observed_keys": observed,
        "extras_vs_allowlist": extras,
        "static_adapter_env": static,
        "adapter_state": dispatched.get("state"),
        "signals": {
            "a_child_dump_canary_absent": not canary_present,
            "b_static_allowlist_covers_observed": extras == [],
        },
    }
    print(json.dumps(payload, indent=1, sort_keys=True))
    if problems:
        print("FAILED:TST-0210-probe1 " + "; ".join(problems))
        return 1
    print(
        "SUCCESS:TST-0210-probe1 canary_absent="
        f"{not canary_present} extras={len(extras)} keys={len(observed)}"
    )
    return 0


def os_canary_missing() -> bool:
    import os

    return os.environ.get(CANARY_KEY) is None


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe2_detector_and_inherit.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: detector (canary on allowlist appears) and inherit-all negative control.

Detector: add the canary to the allowlist and confirm it DOES appear — the child
dump is live, not a hardcoded empty dict.
Negative: full inheritance must fail the canary-absent test.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from harness import (  # noqa: E402
    CANARY_KEY,
    dump_keys_via_adapter,
    dump_keys_with_env,
    load_env_mod,
    planted_canary,
)


def main() -> int:
    env_mod = load_env_mod()
    problems: list[str] = []

    with planted_canary():
        parent_has = CANARY_KEY in os.environ
        default_keys = dump_keys_with_env(env_mod.child_env())
        detector_env = env_mod.filter_env(os.environ, extra_allow=[CANARY_KEY])
        detector_keys = dump_keys_with_env(detector_env)
        inherit_keys = dump_keys_with_env(dict(os.environ))
        adapter_keys, _dispatched = dump_keys_via_adapter()

    if not parent_has:
        problems.append("canary was not in parent env")
    if CANARY_KEY in default_keys:
        problems.append("filtered spawn still leaked canary")
    if CANARY_KEY not in detector_keys:
        problems.append("detector failed: canary on allowlist did not appear in child")
    if CANARY_KEY not in inherit_keys:
        problems.append("inherit-all child missing canary; dump is not a real env")
    inherit_fails_canary_test = CANARY_KEY in inherit_keys
    if not inherit_fails_canary_test:
        problems.append("full inheritance did not fail the canary-absent test")
    if CANARY_KEY in adapter_keys:
        problems.append("adapter child leaked canary during detector probe")
    inherit_extras = env_mod.extras_vs_allowlist(
        inherit_keys, env_mod.load_declared_allowlist()
    )
    if not inherit_extras:
        problems.append(
            "inherit-all observed set is a subset of the allowlist; control is vacuous"
        )

    payload = {
        "probe": "probe2-detector-and-inherit",
        "unit_id": "TST-0210",
        "pass": not problems,
        "problems": problems,
        "parent_has_canary": parent_has,
        "filtered_canary_present": CANARY_KEY in default_keys,
        "detector_canary_present": CANARY_KEY in detector_keys,
        "inherit_canary_present": CANARY_KEY in inherit_keys,
        "adapter_canary_present": CANARY_KEY in adapter_keys,
        "inherit_fails_canary_absent_test": inherit_fails_canary_test,
        "inherit_extras_count": len(inherit_extras),
        "detector_key_count": len(detector_keys),
        "inherit_key_count": len(inherit_keys),
        "filtered_key_count": len(default_keys),
    }
    print(json.dumps(payload, indent=1, sort_keys=True))
    if problems:
        print("FAILED:TST-0210-probe2 " + "; ".join(problems))
        return 1
    print(
        "SUCCESS:TST-0210-probe2 detector_appeared="
        f"{CANARY_KEY in detector_keys} inherit_fails={inherit_fails_canary_test}"
    )
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

TST-0256
--- emit_receipt.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Emit command-center/receipts/TST-0256.json from live migration measurements."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

import capture as C  # noqa: E402
import harness as H  # noqa: E402

UID = "TST-0256"
OUT = "command-center/receipts/TST-0256.json"
SCHEMA = "command-center/schema/conformance-report.schema.json"
CATALOG_SCHEMA = (
    "command-center/schema/receipt.schema.json#/$defs/docs_conformance_report"
)
GUIDE = "docs/layout-migration.md"
MIGRATE = "tools/layout/migrate.py"
QUESTION = (
    "Does the documented old-to-new migration, run verbatim against a genuine "
    "old-layout fixture, produce the expected new layout - and produce no "
    "further change on a second run? PASS requires both the conversion and "
    "the idempotence."
)
SENTINEL = (
    "SUCCESS:TST-0256 documented apply converts the ARC-0007-captured "
    "old-layout fixture to the guide-oracle config-first tree and is a "
    "no-op on the second run"
)
P1 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/TST-0256/probe1_convert_and_diff.py"
)
P2 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/TST-0256/probe2_idempotence_and_noop.py"
)


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    raw = path.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw)


def run_cmd(cmd: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
    p = subprocess.run(
        cmd,
        cwd=str(REPO),
        shell=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        timeout=180,
        env=env,
    )
    return p.returncode, ((p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or "")).strip()


def parse_payload(text: str) -> dict:
    start = text.find("{")
    end = text.rfind("}")
    if start < 0 or end < start:
        return {}
    try:
        return json.loads(text[start : end + 1])
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return {}


def main() -> int:
    cap_rc, cap_out = run_cmd(
        "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
        "command-center/p2-wave/probes/TST-0256/capture.py"
    )
    if cap_rc != 0:
        print("FAILED:TST-0256 capture")
        print(cap_out[-2000:])
        return 1
    prov = C.verify()
    if not prov["ok"]:
        print("FAILED:TST-0256 provenance", prov["problems"][:8])
        return 1

    c1, t1 = run_cmd(P1)
    c2, t2 = run_cmd(P2)
    p1 = parse_payload(t1)
    p2 = parse_payload(t2)
    if c1 != 0 or c2 != 0 or not p1.get("pass") or not p2.get("pass"):
        print(f"FAILED:TST-0256 live probes c1={c1} c2={c2}")
        print(t1[-2000:])

--- probe1_convert_and_diff.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: documented apply vs guide-parsed oracle, plus fixture provenance.

Signal (a): tree diff of the migrated fixture against the expected layout.
The candidate is the fenced command in docs/layout-migration.md. The oracle
relocates the same captured bytes using the mapping table parsed from that
guide — it does not import tools/layout/migrate.py.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

import capture as C  # noqa: E402
import harness as H  # noqa: E402

UID = "TST-0256"


def main() -> int:
    if not (H.REPO / H.PROVENANCE_REL).is_file():
        rc = C.main()
        if rc != 0:
            return rc
    prov = C.verify()
    if not prov["ok"]:
        print(json.dumps({"probe": "probe1", "pass": False, "provenance": prov}, sort_keys=True))
        print("FAILED:TST-0256-probe1 fixture provenance")
        return 1

    guide = H.GUIDE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    mapping = H.parse_mapping(guide)
    apply_cmd = H.documented_apply_command(guide)
    if apply_cmd != H.APPLY_CMD:
        print(json.dumps({"probe": "probe1", "pass": False, "apply_cmd": apply_cmd}, sort_keys=True))
        print("FAILED:TST-0256-probe1 documented apply command drifted")
        return 1

    with H.work_copy() as tmp:
        tmp_path = Path(tmp)
        candidate = tmp_path / "candidate"
        expected = tmp_path / "expected"
        H.copy_tree(H.CAPTURED, candidate)
        crc, cout = H.run_documented(
            'python3 tools/layout/migrate.py classify --tree "$TREE"', candidate
        )
        if crc != 0:
            print(
                json.dumps(
                    {
                        "probe": "probe1",
                        "pass": False,
                        "classify_rc": crc,
                        "out": cout[-1500:],
                    },
                    sort_keys=True,
                )
            )
            print("FAILED:TST-0256-probe1 classify exited non-zero")
            return 1
        before_layout = H.last_json_object(cout)
        rc, out = H.run_documented(apply_cmd, candidate)
        if rc != 0:
            print(json.dumps({"probe": "probe1", "pass": False, "apply_rc": rc, "out": out[-1500:]}, sort_keys=True))
            print("FAILED:TST-0256-probe1 documented apply exited non-zero")
            return 1
        applied = H.last_json_object(out)
        cand_rows = H.census(candidate)
        oracle_rows = H.oracle_apply(H.CAPTURED, expected, mapping)
        diffs = H.tree_diff(cand_rows, oracle_rows)

    payload = {
        "probe": "probe1-convert-and-diff",
        "unit_id": UID,
        "pass": applied.get("action") == "migrated"
        and applied.get("layout_after") == "new"
        and before_layout.get("layout") == "old"
        and not diffs
        and prov["ok"]
        and len(cand_rows) >= 8,
        "documented_command": apply_cmd,
        "mapping": [{"from": a, "to": b} for a, b in mapping],
        "layout_before": before_layout.get("layout"),
        "apply_action": applied.get("action"),
        "layout_after": applied.get("layout_after"),
        "files_written": applied.get("files_written"),
        "candidate_tree_hash": H.tree_hash_from_census(cand_rows),
        "oracle_tree_hash": H.tree_hash_from_census(oracle_rows),
        "tree_diff_count": len(diffs),
        "tree_diff": diffs[:12],
        "candidate_paths": [r["path"] for r in cand_rows],
        "provenance": {
            "ok": prov["ok"],
            "file_count": prov["file_count"],
            "source_root": prov["source_root"],
            "synthesized_from_migration_script": prov["synthesized_from_migration_script"],

--- probe2_idempotence_and_noop.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: second-run idempotence, already-migrated no-op, mutant detector.

Signal (b): byte-identical tree hash after a second documented apply.
Both-states: an already-migrated tree is a no-op. A stamp-on-every-file
mutant must change the hash so the detector is red-capable.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

import capture as C  # noqa: E402
import harness as H  # noqa: E402

UID = "TST-0256"


def mutate_stamp(tree: Path) -> None:
    for path in H.walk_files(tree):
        data = path.read_bytes()
        if not data.endswith(b"\n"):
            data += b"\n"
        path.write_bytes(data + b"# TST-0256-MUTANT-STAMP\n")


def hash_via_doc(tree: Path) -> tuple[int, str, str]:
    rc, out = H.run_documented(H.HASH_CMD, tree)
    if rc != 0:
        return rc, "", out
    doc = H.last_json_object(out)
    return rc, str(doc.get("tree_hash") or ""), out


def main() -> int:
    if not (H.REPO / H.PROVENANCE_REL).is_file():
        rc = C.main()
        if rc != 0:
            return rc

    guide = H.GUIDE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    apply_cmd = H.documented_apply_command(guide)

    with H.work_copy() as tmp:
        tmp_path = Path(tmp)
        tree = tmp_path / "twice"
        H.copy_tree(H.CAPTURED, tree)
        rc1, out1 = H.run_documented(apply_cmd, tree)
        if rc1 != 0:
            print(json.dumps({"probe": "probe2", "pass": False, "first_rc": rc1, "out": out1[-1200:]}, sort_keys=True))
            print("FAILED:TST-0256-probe2 first apply")
            return 1
        first = H.last_json_object(out1)
        h1_rc, hash1, _ = hash_via_doc(tree)
        census1 = H.census(tree)
        rc2, out2 = H.run_documented(apply_cmd, tree)
        if rc2 != 0:
            print(json.dumps({"probe": "probe2", "pass": False, "second_rc": rc2, "out": out2[-1200:]}, sort_keys=True))
            print("FAILED:TST-0256-probe2 second apply")
            return 1
        second = H.last_json_object(out2)
        h2_rc, hash2, _ = hash_via_doc(tree)
        census2 = H.census(tree)
        idempotent = (
            h1_rc == 0
            and h2_rc == 0
            and hash1 == hash2
            and hash1 == first.get("tree_hash") == second.get("tree_hash")
            and H.tree_diff(census1, census2) == []
            and second.get("action") == "noop"
        )

        already = tmp_path / "already"
        H.copy_tree(tree, already)
        pre_hash = hash2
        rc_n, out_n = H.run_documented(apply_cmd, already)
        third = H.last_json_object(out_n) if rc_n == 0 else {}
        _, post_hash, _ = hash_via_doc(already)
        already_noop = (
            rc_n == 0
            and third.get("action") == "noop"
            and post_hash == pre_hash
            and H.tree_diff(H.census(already), census2) == []
        )

        mutant = tmp_path / "mutant"
        H.copy_tree(tree, mutant)
        before_mut = H.tree_hash_from_census(H.census(mutant))
        mutate_stamp(mutant)
        mut_hash = H.tree_hash_from_census(H.census(mutant))
        mutant_fired = mut_hash != hash2 and mut_hash != before_mut

    payload = {
        "probe": "probe2-idempotence-and-noop",
        "unit_id": UID,
        "pass": bool(idempotent and already_noop and mutant_fired),

==============================================================================
TST-0305
--- probe1_noop_distribution.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: 100 no-op iterations, distribution, per-stage attribution."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from scenario import (  # noqa: E402
    CATALOG_SENTINEL,
    OUT_REL,
    REPO,
    STAGES,
    compact,
    run_campaign,
)


def main() -> int:
    art = REPO / OUT_REL
    if not art.is_file() or art.stat().st_size <= 0:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe1 artifact missing")
        return 1
    text = art.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if CATALOG_SENTINEL not in text:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe1 sentinel absent")
        return 1
    doc = json.loads(text)
    if doc.get("unit_id") != "TST-0305":
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe1 unit_id", doc.get("unit_id"))
        return 1
    if str(doc.get("state") or "").upper() in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe1 self-declared", doc.get("state"))
        return 1

    live = run_campaign()
    print(json.dumps(compact(live), sort_keys=True, default=str))

    if not live.get("ok"):
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe1 live campaign ok=false")
        return 1
    noop = live["noop"]
    if noop["n"] != 100:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe1 noop n", noop["n"])
        return 1
    if len(live["noop_totals_ms"]) != 100:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe1 totals length", len(live["noop_totals_ms"]))
        return 1
    missing = [s for s in STAGES if s not in noop["stages"]]
    if missing:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe1 stages missing", missing)
        return 1
    for name in STAGES:
        n = noop["stages"][name]["n"]
        if n != 100:
            print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe1 stage n", name, n)
            return 1
    if float(live["overhead_ms"]) <= 0:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe1 overhead_ms not positive", live["overhead_ms"])
        return 1
    if not (0 < float(live["fraction_of_p50_real"]) < 1):
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe1 fraction", live["fraction_of_p50_real"])
        return 1
    dom = (live.get("dominating_stage") or {}).get("stage")
    if dom not in STAGES:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe1 dominating stage", dom)
        return 1

    art_over = doc.get("overhead") or {}
    if int((art_over.get("noop") or {}).get("n") or 0) != 100:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe1 artifact noop n", art_over.get("noop"))
        return 1
    art_stages = ((art_over.get("noop") or {}).get("stages") or {})
    if any(s not in art_stages for s in STAGES):
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe1 artifact stages", sorted(art_stages))
        return 1
    art_total = (art_over.get("noop") or {}).get("total") or {}
    if "p50_ms" not in art_total:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe1 artifact missing total p50")
        return 1

    print(
        f"noop_n=100 overhead_ms={live['overhead_ms']} "
        f"fraction={live['fraction_of_p50_real']:.6f} "
        f"dominating={dom}"
    )
    print("SUCCESS:TST-0305-probe1 noop distribution + stage attribution")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- probe2_ceiling_alarm.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: ceiling alarm both-states (fires on fast, silent on slow)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from scenario import (  # noqa: E402
    CATALOG_SENTINEL,
    OUT_REL,
    REPO,
    compact,
    inverted_ceiling_alarm,
    load_artifact,
    run_campaign,
)


def main() -> int:
    try:
        doc = load_artifact()
    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, FileNotFoundError) as exc:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe2 artifact", type(exc).__name__)
        return 1
    text = (REPO / OUT_REL).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if CATALOG_SENTINEL not in text:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe2 sentinel absent")
        return 1
    if str(doc.get("state") or "").upper() in {"BLOCKED", "FAILED", "SEVERED", "TIMEOUT"}:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe2 self-declared", doc.get("state"))
        return 1

    live = run_campaign()
    print(json.dumps(compact(live), sort_keys=True, default=str))

    tiny = live["tiny"]
    real = live["real"]
    ceil = live["ceiling"]
    if not live.get("both_states"):
        print(
            "FAILED:TST-0305-probe2 both-states "
            f"fast_fired={ceil.get('fast_p50_fired')} "
            f"slow_fired={ceil.get('slow_p50_fired')}"
        )
        return 1
    if ceil.get("inverted_both_states"):
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe2 inverted detector also both-states (vacuous)")
        return 1
    if not tiny["ceiling_p50"]:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe2 tiny p50 did not fire", tiny)
        return 1
    if real["ceiling_p50"]:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe2 real p50 fired (want silent)", real)
        return 1

    inv_fast = inverted_ceiling_alarm(tiny["p50_oracle_ms"], tiny["p50_harness_ms"])
    inv_slow = inverted_ceiling_alarm(real["p50_oracle_ms"], real["p50_harness_ms"])
    if inv_fast is True and inv_slow is False:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe2 inverted alarm matched production polarity")
        return 1
    if inv_fast is not False:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe2 inverted fast should be silent", inv_fast)
        return 1
    if inv_slow is not True:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe2 inverted slow should fire", inv_slow)
        return 1

    art_ceil = (doc.get("overhead") or {}).get("ceiling") or doc.get("ceiling") or {}
    if art_ceil.get("fast_p50_fired") is not True:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe2 artifact fast_p50_fired", art_ceil)
        return 1
    if art_ceil.get("slow_p50_fired") is not False:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe2 artifact slow_p50_fired", art_ceil)
        return 1
    if art_ceil.get("inverted_both_states") is True:
        print("FAILED:TST-0305-probe2 artifact inverted_both_states true")
        return 1

    print(
        f"fired_on_fast={tiny['ceiling_p50']} silent_on_slow={not real['ceiling_p50']} "
        f"inv_fast={inv_fast} inv_slow={inv_slow} "
        f"payload_s={live['payload_s']}"
    )
    print("SUCCESS:TST-0305-probe2 ceiling alarm both-states")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

--- scenario.py ---
"""TST-0305 paths, sentinels, and campaign entry.

Loads tools/bench/overhead.py by file path so a sibling rewrite of
tools/bench/__init__.py cannot break these probes.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]


def _load_overhead():
    path = REPO / "tools" / "bench" / "overhead.py"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("tst0305_bench_overhead", path)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise ImportError(f"cannot load {path}")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[spec.name] = mod
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


overhead = _load_overhead()
NOOP_N = overhead.NOOP_N
REAL_N = overhead.REAL_N
STAGES = overhead.STAGES
TINY_N = overhead.TINY_N
WARMUP_N = overhead.WARMUP_N
compact = overhead.compact
inverted_ceiling_alarm = overhead.inverted_ceiling_alarm
measure_overhead = overhead.measure_overhead

OUT_REL = "command-center/receipts/TST-0305.json"
RESULTS_REL = "command-center/bench/results/TST-0305.jsonl"
LEDGER = HERE / "_campaign_ledger.jsonl"
SCRATCH = HERE / "_scratch"
CATALOG_SENTINEL = "SUCCESS:TST-0305"
QUESTION = (
    "Question: how much of the measured latency is the thing being measured, "
    "and how much is the instrumentation around it? Measure the harness "
    "overhead directly (run a no-op case through the full pipeline), express "
    "it as a fraction of the smallest real case, and set a ceiling above "
    "which the platform must warn that its own overhead dominates."
)
P1 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/TST-0305/probe1_noop_distribution.py"
)
P2 = (
    "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B "
    "command-center/p2-wave/probes/TST-0305/probe2_ceiling_alarm.py"
)


def run_campaign() -> dict:
    return measure_overhead(
        ledger=LEDGER,
        scratch_dir=SCRATCH,
        noop_n=NOOP_N,
        real_n=REAL_N,
        tiny_n=TINY_N,
        warmup=WARMUP_N,
    )


def append_results_summary(report: dict) -> None:
    dest = REPO / RESULTS_REL
    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    row = {
        "compact": compact(report),
        "dominating_stage": report.get("dominating_stage"),
        "fraction_of_p50_real": report.get("fraction_of_p50_real"),
        "overhead_ms": report.get("overhead_ms"),
        "unit_id": "TST-0305",
    }
    line = json.dumps(row, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n"
    with dest.open("a", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
        fh.write(line)
        fh.flush()


def load_artifact() -> dict:
    path = REPO / OUT_REL
    if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size <= 0:
        raise FileNotFoundError(OUT_REL)
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

WFA-0031
--- probe1_adverse_and_restrictive.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 1: adverse inference on unlicensed sources; planted copyleft fires.

Both-states: the same gate must stay silent (not ADVERSE) on an agreeing MIT
pair, and must not treat a README MIT badge as a licence blob.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import corpus  # noqa: E402

gate = corpus.load_gate()
by_id = {r.source_id: r for r in gate.evaluate_many(corpus.planted_sources(gate))}

unlic = by_id["plant:plant-unlicensed"]
gpl = by_id["plant:plant-gpl3"]
mit = by_id["plant:plant-mit-agree"]

failures: list[str] = []

if unlic.classification != "ADVERSE-UNLICENSED":
    failures.append(f"unlicensed classification={unlic.classification}")
if unlic.ingest_mode not in {"MECHANISM_ONLY", "REFUSE"}:
    failures.append(f"unlicensed ingest_mode={unlic.ingest_mode}")
if unlic.code_carried:
    failures.append("unlicensed carried code")
if unlic.signal_a_spdx is not None:
    failures.append(f"unlicensed signal A leaked {unlic.signal_a_spdx}")
if unlic.readme_badge_spdx != "MIT":
    failures.append(f"badge not observed: {unlic.readme_badge_spdx}")
if unlic.readme_badge_used:
    failures.append("README badge was used for classification")
if unlic.signal_b_spdx != "MIT":
    failures.append(f"API MIT not recorded on B: {unlic.signal_b_spdx}")
if not any(o.get("kind") == "adverse-inference" for o in unlic.obligations):
    failures.append("missing adverse-inference obligation")

if gpl.classification != "COPYLEFT":
    failures.append(f"planted GPL classification={gpl.classification}")
if gpl.signal_a_spdx != "GPL-3.0":
    failures.append(f"planted GPL A={gpl.signal_a_spdx}")
if gpl.ingest_mode != "MECHANISM_ONLY":
    failures.append(f"planted GPL ingest_mode={gpl.ingest_mode}")
if gpl.code_carried:
    failures.append("planted GPL carried code")
if gpl.signals_agree is not True:
    failures.append(f"planted GPL signals_agree={gpl.signals_agree}")

if mit.classification == "ADVERSE-UNLICENSED":
    failures.append("agreeing MIT pair wrongly treated as unlicensed")
if mit.classification != "PERMISSIVE":
    failures.append(f"agreeing MIT classification={mit.classification}")
if mit.signals_agree is not True:
    failures.append(f"agreeing MIT signals_agree={mit.signals_agree}")
if mit.signal_a_spdx != "MIT" or mit.signal_b_spdx != "MIT":
    failures.append(f"agreeing MIT A/B={mit.signal_a_spdx}/{mit.signal_b_spdx}")

payload = {
    "probe": "probe1-adverse-and-restrictive",
    "unlicensed": unlic.as_dict(),
    "planted_gpl": gpl.as_dict(),
    "mit_agree_control": mit.as_dict(),
    "failures": failures,
    "ok": not failures,
}
print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
if failures:
    print("FAIL:" + "; ".join(failures))
    raise SystemExit(1)
print(
    "adverse_on_unlicensed=True badge_ignored=True "
    f"gpl_fired={gpl.classification} mit_agree={mit.classification}"
)
print("SUCCESS:WFA-0031-probe1 adverse inference and planted restrictive licence")
raise SystemExit(0)

--- probe2_obligations_and_disagreement.py ---
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe 2: attribution + rider obligations; A/B disagreement is UNCLASSIFIED.

Both-states: agreeing MIT is classified, not UNCLASSIFIED. Disagreement is
not resolved by preferring the file or the API.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import corpus  # noqa: E402

gate = corpus.load_gate()
by_id = {r.source_id: r for r in gate.evaluate_many(corpus.planted_sources(gate))}

apache = by_id["plant:plant-apache2"]
rider = by_id["plant:plant-rider"]
disagree = by_id["plant:plant-disagree"]
agree = by_id["plant:plant-mit-agree"]
tos = by_id["plant:plant-tos-evade"]

failures: list[str] = []

attr_kinds = {o.get("kind") for o in apache.obligations}
if "attribution-required" not in attr_kinds:
    failures.append(f"apache missing attribution obligations: {attr_kinds}")
if apache.signal_a_spdx != "Apache-2.0":
    failures.append(f"apache A={apache.signal_a_spdx}")
if apache.classification != "PERMISSIVE":
    failures.append(f"apache classification={apache.classification}")

if rider.classification != "RIDER-RESTRICTED":
    failures.append(f"rider classification={rider.classification}")
if not rider.riders:
    failures.append("rider produced no rider records")
verbatim = " ".join(r.get("verbatim") or "" for r in rider.riders)
if "Commons Clause" not in verbatim and "Sell the Software" not in verbatim:
    failures.append("rider verbatim missing Commons Clause text")
if not any(o.get("kind") == "rider-restricted" for o in rider.obligations):
    failures.append("rider missing rider-restricted obligation")
if rider.ingest_mode != "MECHANISM_ONLY":
    failures.append(f"rider ingest_mode={rider.ingest_mode}")
if rider.code_carried:
    failures.append("rider carried code")

if disagree.classification != "UNCLASSIFIED":
    failures.append(f"disagreement classification={disagree.classification}")
if disagree.signals_agree is not False:
    failures.append(f"disagreement signals_agree={disagree.signals_agree}")
if disagree.signal_a_spdx != "MIT" or disagree.signal_b_spdx != "Apache-2.0":
    failures.append(
        f"disagreement A/B={disagree.signal_a_spdx}/{disagree.signal_b_spdx}"
    )
if disagree.ingest_mode != "MECHANISM_ONLY":
    failures.append(f"disagreement ingest_mode={disagree.ingest_mode}")
if not disagree.escalated:
    failures.append("disagreement not escalated")
# Must not silently prefer A or B.
if disagree.classification in {"PERMISSIVE", "CUSTOM"}:
    failures.append("disagreement resolved by preference")

if agree.classification == "UNCLASSIFIED":
    failures.append("agreeing MIT wrongly UNCLASSIFIED")
if agree.signals_agree is not True:
    failures.append(f"agreeing MIT signals_agree={agree.signals_agree}")

if tos.classification != "REFUSED-TOS":
    failures.append(f"ToS/Tor plant classification={tos.classification}")
if tos.ingest_mode != "REFUSE":
    failures.append(f"ToS/Tor ingest_mode={tos.ingest_mode}")

payload = {
    "probe": "probe2-obligations-and-disagreement",
    "apache": apache.as_dict(),
    "rider": rider.as_dict(),
    "disagreement": disagree.as_dict(),
    "mit_agree_control": agree.as_dict(),
    "tos_tor": tos.as_dict(),
    "failures": failures,
    "ok": not failures,
}
print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
if failures:
    print("FAIL:" + "; ".join(failures))
    raise SystemExit(1)
print(
    "apache_obligations=True rider_verbatim=True "
    f"disagree={disagree.classification} agree={agree.classification} "
    f"tos={tos.classification}"
)
print("SUCCESS:WFA-0031-probe2 obligations, riders, and unclassified disagreement")
raise SystemExit(0)
